    -- ============================================================================
-- KINETIC OS v2.5 — CIRUGÍA 1: LIMPIEZA QUIRÚRGICA Y CUENTA ÚNICA DE ADMIN
-- Archivo: sql/cirugia1_limpieza_y_admin_oficial.sql
-- Destino: Supabase SQL Editor (Proyecto gcppxrlzxlgkuapktxvl)
-- ============================================================================

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pgcrypto;

-- ============================================================================
-- 1. PROTECCIÓN ESTRICTA DE public.afiliados (CONSERVANDO CLUBES REALES)
-- ============================================================================
DELETE FROM public.afiliados
WHERE LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(nombre, ''))) IN (
        'kinetic oficial',
        'sede kinetic',
        'sede oficial kinetic'
      )
   OR LOWER(COALESCE(nombre, '')) LIKE '%atleta 5975%'
   OR LOWER(COALESCE(nombre, '')) LIKE '%atleta kinetic%';

UPDATE public.afiliados
SET propietario_id = NULL,
    email_contacto = CASE
      WHEN LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(email_contacto, ''))) IN (
        'club@kinetic.com',
        'especialista@kinetic.com',
        'kinetic@kinetic.com',
        'atleta@kinetic.com'
      ) THEN NULL
      ELSE email_contacto
    END;

-- ============================================================================
-- 2. VACIADO TOTAL DE DATOS DE PRUEBA (EVENTOS, RESERVAS, CHATS, HISTORIAS, WAITLIST)
-- ============================================================================
DELETE FROM public.reservas;
DELETE FROM public.servicios;
DELETE FROM public.equipo_mensajes;
DELETE FROM public.equipo_miembros;
DELETE FROM public.equipos_kinetic;
DELETE FROM public.admin_audit_logs;

DO $$
DECLARE
  v_tbl TEXT;
BEGIN
  FOREACH v_tbl IN ARRAY ARRAY[
    'comunidad_comentarios',
    'comunidad_posts',
    'historias_24h',
    'kinetic_stories',
    'mensajes_directos',
    'chats_privados',
    'negocios'
  ]
  LOOP
    IF EXISTS (
      SELECT 1
      FROM information_schema.tables
      WHERE table_schema = 'public'
        AND table_name = v_tbl
    ) THEN
      EXECUTE format('DELETE FROM public.%I', v_tbl);
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
END $$;

ALTER TABLE public.fundadores_waitlist
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS estado TEXT DEFAULT 'pendiente';

DELETE FROM public.fundadores_waitlist;

-- ============================================================================
-- 3. CUENTA ÚNICA DE ADMINISTRADOR ('administrador@kinetic.com.mx' -> 'Kinetic Oficial')
-- ============================================================================
ALTER TABLE public.perfiles
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS email TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS kc_membresia NUMERIC DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS kc_topup NUMERIC DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS saldo_kc NUMERIC DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS xp_total INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS racha_dias INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS recompensas_reclamadas TEXT[] DEFAULT '{}';

DO $$
DECLARE
  v_admin_id UUID;
  v_has_tipo_cuenta BOOLEAN;
  r_fk RECORD;
BEGIN
  -- Localizar la cuenta base kinetic@kinetic.com o administrador@kinetic.com.mx conservando su encrypted_password
  SELECT u.id
    INTO v_admin_id
  FROM auth.users u
  WHERE LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) IN (
    'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
    'kinetic@kinetic.com',
    'administrador@kinetic.com'
  )
  ORDER BY
    (LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) = 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx') DESC,
    (LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) = 'kinetic@kinetic.com') DESC,
    u.created_at ASC
  LIMIT 1;

  IF v_admin_id IS NULL THEN
    SELECT p.id
      INTO v_admin_id
    FROM public.perfiles p
    JOIN auth.users u ON u.id = p.id
    WHERE LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.rol, ''))) IN ('admin', 'fundador')
    ORDER BY p.created_at ASC NULLS LAST
    LIMIT 1;
  END IF;

  IF v_admin_id IS NULL THEN
    v_admin_id := gen_random_uuid();
    INSERT INTO auth.users (
      id,
      instance_id,
      email,
      encrypted_password,
      email_confirmed_at,
      created_at,
      updated_at,
      raw_app_meta_data,
      raw_user_meta_data,
      is_super_admin,
      role,
      aud
    ) VALUES (
      v_admin_id,
      '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000',
      'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
      crypt('KineticAdmin2026!', gen_salt('bf')),
      NOW(),
      NOW(),
      NOW(),
      '{"provider":"email","providers":["email"]}'::jsonb,
      '{"nombre":"Kinetic Oficial","rol":"admin","tipo_cuenta":"admin"}'::jsonb,
      FALSE,
      'authenticated',
      'authenticated'
    );
  END IF;

  -- Limpiar referencias residuales en tablas satélite públicas que apunten a perfiles/users de prueba
  FOR r_fk IN
    SELECT DISTINCT
      kcu.table_name,
      kcu.column_name
    FROM information_schema.table_constraints tc
    JOIN information_schema.key_column_usage kcu
      ON tc.constraint_name = kcu.constraint_name
     AND tc.table_schema = kcu.table_schema
    JOIN information_schema.constraint_column_usage ccu
      ON ccu.constraint_name = tc.constraint_name
    WHERE tc.constraint_type = 'FOREIGN KEY'
      AND tc.table_schema = 'public'
      AND tc.table_name NOT IN ('perfiles', 'afiliados')
      AND (
        (ccu.table_schema = 'auth' AND ccu.table_name = 'users')
        OR (ccu.table_schema = 'public' AND ccu.table_name = 'perfiles')
      )
  LOOP
    EXECUTE format(
      'DELETE FROM public.%I WHERE %I IS NOT NULL AND %I <> $1',
      r_fk.table_name,
      r_fk.column_name,
      r_fk.column_name
    ) USING v_admin_id;
  END LOOP;

  -- Eliminar TODAS las cuentas de prueba en public.perfiles, auth.identities y auth.users
  DELETE FROM public.perfiles WHERE id <> v_admin_id;
  DELETE FROM auth.identities WHERE user_id <> v_admin_id;
  DELETE FROM auth.users WHERE id <> v_admin_id;

  -- Transformar la cuenta Admin a 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx' conservando su encrypted_password actual
  UPDATE auth.users
  SET email = 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
      email_confirmed_at = NOW(),
      updated_at = NOW(),
      raw_app_meta_data = COALESCE(raw_app_meta_data, '{"provider":"email","providers":["email"]}'::jsonb),
      raw_user_meta_data = '{"nombre":"Kinetic Oficial","rol":"admin","tipo_cuenta":"admin"}'::jsonb
  WHERE id = v_admin_id;

  IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM auth.identities WHERE user_id = v_admin_id AND provider = 'email'
  ) THEN
    UPDATE auth.identities
    SET identity_data = COALESCE(identity_data, '{}'::jsonb) || jsonb_build_object(
          'sub', v_admin_id::text,
          'email', 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
          'email_verified', true
        ),
        updated_at = NOW()
    WHERE user_id = v_admin_id
      AND provider = 'email';
  ELSE
    INSERT INTO auth.identities (
      id,
      user_id,
      identity_data,
      provider,
      provider_id,
      last_sign_in_at,
      created_at,
      updated_at
    ) VALUES (
      v_admin_id,
      v_admin_id,
      jsonb_build_object(
        'sub', v_admin_id::text,
        'email', 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
        'email_verified', true
      ),
      'email',
      v_admin_id::text,
      NOW(),
      NOW(),
      NOW()
    );
  END IF;

  -- Asegurar registro único en public.perfiles con saldos y contadores en 0
  INSERT INTO public.perfiles (
    id,
    email,
    nombre,
    rol,
    nivel,
    kc_membresia,
    kc_topup,
    saldo_kc,
    xp_total,
    racha_dias,
    recompensas_reclamadas
  ) VALUES (
    v_admin_id,
    'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
    'Kinetic Oficial',
    'admin',
    'Black',
    0,
    0,
    0,
    0,
    0,
    '{}'
  )
  ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE
  SET email = 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
      nombre = 'Kinetic Oficial',
      rol = 'admin',
      nivel = 'Black',
      kc_membresia = 0,
      kc_topup = 0,
      saldo_kc = 0,
      xp_total = 0,
      racha_dias = 0,
      recompensas_reclamadas = '{}';

  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM information_schema.columns
    WHERE table_schema = 'public'
      AND table_name = 'perfiles'
      AND column_name = 'tipo_cuenta'
  ) INTO v_has_tipo_cuenta;

  IF v_has_tipo_cuenta THEN
    EXECUTE 'UPDATE public.perfiles SET tipo_cuenta = ''admin'' WHERE id = $1' USING v_admin_id;
  END IF;
END $$;

-- ============================================================================
-- 4. FUNCIÓN Y POLÍTICAS RLS DE ADMIN MAESTRO + RPC INYECCIÓN BÓVEDA
-- ============================================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_kinetic_master_admin()
RETURNS BOOLEAN
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
STABLE
SET search_path = public
AS $$
  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM auth.users u
    LEFT JOIN public.perfiles p ON p.id = u.id
    WHERE u.id = (SELECT auth.uid())
      AND (
        LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) IN (
          'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
          'administrador@kinetic.com',
          'kinetic@kinetic.com'
        )
        OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.rol, ''))) IN ('admin', 'fundador')
      )
  );
$$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.is_kinetic_master_admin() TO authenticated, anon;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.inyectar_saldo_boveda(
  p_user_id UUID,
  p_cantidad NUMERIC,
  p_billetera TEXT DEFAULT 'topup',
  p_nuevo_nivel TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_is_admin BOOLEAN;
  v_mem NUMERIC;
  v_top NUMERIC;
  v_nivel TEXT;
BEGIN
  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM auth.users u
    LEFT JOIN public.perfiles p ON p.id = u.id
    WHERE u.id = (SELECT auth.uid())
      AND (
        LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(u.email, ''))) IN (
          'administrador@kinetic.com.mx',
          'administrador@kinetic.com',
          'kinetic@kinetic.com'
        )
        OR LOWER(TRIM(COALESCE(p.rol, ''))) IN ('admin', 'fundador')
      )
  ) INTO v_is_admin;

  IF NOT COALESCE(v_is_admin, FALSE) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'No autorizado: requiere privilegios de Admin Maestro Kinetic.';
  END IF;

  SELECT COALESCE(kc_membresia, 0), COALESCE(kc_topup, 0), COALESCE(nivel, 'Acceso Base')
    INTO v_mem, v_top, v_nivel
  FROM public.perfiles
  WHERE id = p_user_id
  FOR UPDATE;

  IF LOWER(COALESCE(p_billetera, 'topup')) = 'membresia' THEN
    v_mem := GREATEST(0, v_mem + COALESCE(p_cantidad, 0));
  ELSE
    v_top := GREATEST(0, v_top + COALESCE(p_cantidad, 0));
  END IF;

  IF p_nuevo_nivel IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TRIM(p_nuevo_nivel)) > 0 THEN
    v_nivel := TRIM(p_nuevo_nivel);
  END IF;

  UPDATE public.perfiles
  SET kc_membresia = v_mem,
      kc_topup = v_top,
      saldo_kc = v_mem + v_top,
      nivel = v_nivel
  WHERE id = p_user_id;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'kc_membresia', v_mem,
    'kc_topup', v_top,
    'saldo_kc', v_mem + v_top,
    'nivel', v_nivel
  );
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.inyectar_fondos_boveda(
  p_user_id UUID,
  p_cantidad NUMERIC,
  p_billetera TEXT DEFAULT 'topup',
  p_nuevo_nivel TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
  SELECT public.inyectar_saldo_boveda(p_user_id, p_cantidad, p_billetera, p_nuevo_nivel);
$$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.inyectar_saldo_boveda(UUID, NUMERIC, TEXT, TEXT) TO authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.inyectar_fondos_boveda(UUID, NUMERIC, TEXT, TEXT) TO authenticated;

-- Políticas RLS de Admin Maestro sobre tablas operativas y fundadores_waitlist
ALTER TABLE public.fundadores_waitlist ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Insercion publica fundadores_waitlist" ON public.fundadores_waitlist;
CREATE POLICY "Insercion publica fundadores_waitlist"
ON public.fundadores_waitlist
FOR INSERT
TO anon, authenticated
WITH CHECK (true);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admin Maestro gestiona fundadores_waitlist" ON public.fundadores_waitlist;
CREATE POLICY "Admin Maestro gestiona fundadores_waitlist"
ON public.fundadores_waitlist
FOR ALL
USING ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()))
WITH CHECK ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admin Maestro gestiona afiliados" ON public.afiliados;
CREATE POLICY "Admin Maestro gestiona afiliados"
ON public.afiliados
FOR ALL
USING ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()))
WITH CHECK ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admin Maestro gestiona servicios" ON public.servicios;
CREATE POLICY "Admin Maestro gestiona servicios"
ON public.servicios
FOR ALL
USING ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()))
WITH CHECK ((SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admin Maestro gestiona perfiles" ON public.perfiles;
CREATE POLICY "Admin Maestro gestiona perfiles"
ON public.perfiles
FOR ALL
USING ((SELECT auth.uid()) = id OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()))
WITH CHECK ((SELECT auth.uid()) = id OR (SELECT public.is_kinetic_master_admin()));

NOTIFY pgrst, 'reload schema';

-- ============================================================================
SELECT
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM auth.users) AS total_auth_users,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.perfiles) AS total_perfiles,
  (SELECT email || ' — ' || nombre FROM public.perfiles LIMIT 1) AS admin_unico,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.servicios) AS total_servicios,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.fundadores_waitlist) AS total_fundadores_waitlist,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM public.afiliados) AS total_afiliados_reales_conservados;

    FILE: src/__tests__/adversarial/adminHubStage7.adversarial.test.ts

    // === src/__tests__/adversarial/adminHubStage7.adversarial.test.ts === //
import fc from 'fast-check';
import { buildPartnerSpeiWhatsappUrl, buildSpeiTransferClipboardPayload, computeSafeAdminTreasuryMutation, sanitizePendingB2BChanges } from '../../services/admin/adminSecurityAndSpeiHelpers';
import { calcularCorteSemanalB2B, isAdminOrB2BAccount } from '../../services/adminB2BCutEngine';

declare const describe: (name: string, fn: () => void) => void;
declare const it: (name: string, fn: () => void) => void;

const expectTrue = (cond: boolean, msg: string): void => { if (!cond) throw new Error(msg); };
const expectEq = <T>(actual: T, expected: T, msg: string): void => {
  if (actual !== expected) throw new Error(`${msg} (expected=${String(expected)}, actual=${String(actual)})`);
};
const ALLOWED_KEYS = new Set(['nombre', 'ubicacion', 'whatsapp', 'horarios', 'disciplina', 'portada_url', 'avatar_url']);

describe('Admin Hub CEO v2.6.8 — Allowlist B2B, Tesorería ACID, Consolidación SPEI & Ficha/WhatsApp (fast-check 500x)', () => {
  it('Propiedad 1: sanitizePendingB2BChanges bloquea 100% claves sensibles/__proto__ y URLs javascript:/data:', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.record({
          nombre: fc.string({ minLength: 0, maxLength: 320 }),
          ubicacion: fc.string({ minLength: 0, maxLength: 320 }),
          whatsapp: fc.string({ minLength: 0, maxLength: 40 }),
          horarios: fc.string({ minLength: 0, maxLength: 320 }),
          disciplina: fc.string({ minLength: 0, maxLength: 80 }),
          portada_url: fc.oneof(
            fc.constant('https://cdn.kineticapp.com.mx/cover.jpg'),
            fc.constant('javascript:alert(1)'),
            fc.constant('data:text/html;base64,PHNjcmlwdD4='),
            fc.constant('http://insecure.example.com/a.jpg'),
            fc.string({ maxLength: 100 })
          ),
          avatar_url: fc.oneof(
            fc.constant('https://cdn.kineticapp.com.mx/avatar.jpg'),
            fc.constant('javascript:void(0)'),
            fc.constant('data:image/svg+xml;utf8,<svg/>')
          ),
          id: fc.uuid(),
          propietario_id: fc.uuid(),
          estado: fc.constantFrom('activo', 'aprobado', 'admin'),
          verificado: fc.boolean(),
          strikes_operativos: fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 3 }),
          estrellas_exclusividad: fc.integer({ min: 1, max: 5 }),
          rfc: fc.constant('EKU9003173C9'),
          regimen_fiscal: fc.constant('persona_moral'),
          clabe_bancaria: fc.constant('012345678901234567'),
          csf_validada: fc.constant(true),
          email_contacto: fc.constant('attacker@evil.com'),
        }),
        fc.boolean(),
        (rawObj, asJson) => {
          const payload = asJson
            ? JSON.stringify({ ...rawObj, ['__proto__']: { isAdmin: true }, constructor: 'hack', prototype: 'hack' })
            : Object.assign(Object.create(null), rawObj, { constructor: 'hack', prototype: 'hack' });

          const safe = sanitizePendingB2BChanges(payload);
          for (const k of Object.keys(safe)) {
            expectTrue(ALLOWED_KEYS.has(k), `forbidden key leaked: ${k}`);
            expectTrue(typeof safe[k] === 'string' && safe[k].length > 0, `empty or non-string value in ${k}`);
            if (k === 'portada_url' || k === 'avatar_url') {
              expectTrue(safe[k].startsWith('https://') && safe[k].length <= 600, `unsafe URL in ${k}: ${safe[k]}`);
            } else {
              expectTrue(safe[k].length <= 240, `unbounded text length in ${k}`);
            }
          }
          expectEq(({} as any).isAdmin, undefined, 'zero prototype pollution');
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );
  });

  it('Propiedad 2: computeSafeAdminTreasuryMutation garantiza saldos >= 0, enteros exactos y mem + top === total en secuencias', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.integer({ min: -500, max: 250_000 }),
        fc.integer({ min: -500, max: 250_000 }),
        fc.array(
          fc.record({
            mode: fc.constantFrom<'inyectar' | 'retirar' | 'reset'>('inyectar', 'retirar', 'reset'),
            wallet: fc.constantFrom<'membresia' | 'topup'>('membresia', 'topup'),
            rawDelta: fc.oneof(
              fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 100_000 }),
              fc.integer({ min: -50_000, max: -1 }),
              fc.integer({ min: 100_001, max: 999_999 }),
              fc.constant(NaN),
              fc.constant(Infinity),
              fc.constant(-Infinity)
            ),
          }),
          { minLength: 1, maxLength: 15 }
        ),
        (initMem, initTop, ops) => {
          let mem = Math.max(0, initMem);
          let top = Math.max(0, initTop);
          for (const op of ops) {
            const res = computeSafeAdminTreasuryMutation({
              currentMem: mem, currentTop: top, rawDelta: op.rawDelta, wallet: op.wallet, mode: op.mode,
            });
            expectTrue(Number.isSafeInteger(res.effectiveDelta), 'effectiveDelta must be integer');
            expectTrue(Number.isSafeInteger(res.projectedMem) && res.projectedMem >= 0, 'projectedMem >= 0');
            expectTrue(Number.isSafeInteger(res.projectedTop) && res.projectedTop >= 0, 'projectedTop >= 0');
            expectEq(res.projectedMem + res.projectedTop, res.projectedTotal, 'mem + top === total');

            if (!Number.isFinite(op.rawDelta) || op.rawDelta < 0 || op.rawDelta > 100_000) {
              expectTrue(!res.ok && res.effectiveDelta === 0, 'invalid delta must fail closed');
            } else {
              expectTrue(res.ok, 'valid bounded delta must succeed');
              mem = res.projectedMem;
              top = res.projectedTop;
            }
          }
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );
  });

  it('Propiedad 3: calcularCorteSemanalB2B cuadra al centavo (<= 0.01) con totales CEO y filtra cuentas Admin/Oficiales', () => {
    const period = { startMs: 1_760_000_000_000, endMs: 1_760_604_799_999, inicioIso: '2025-10-09', finIso: '2025-10-15', strictWindow: true };
    const validTsIso = new Date(period.startMs + 36_000_000).toISOString();

    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.array(
          fc.record({
            id: fc.uuid(),
            nombre: fc.constantFrom('Alpha Box Cabo', 'Beta Padel Club', 'Gamma Pilates Studio', 'Delta Surf Lab'),
            es_especialista: fc.boolean(),
            rfc: fc.constantFrom('EKU9003173C9', 'GODE561231GR8', ''),
            regimen_fiscal: fc.constantFrom('persona_moral', 'persona_fisica_con_rfc', 'sin_rfc'),
            clabe_bancaria: fc.constantFrom('012180001234567890', ''),
          }),
          { minLength: 1, maxLength: 6 }
        ),
        fc.array(
          fc.record({
            id: fc.uuid(),
            negocio_nombre: fc.constantFrom('Alpha Box Cabo', 'Beta Padel Club', 'Gamma Pilates Studio', 'Delta Surf Lab'),
            estado: fc.constantFrom('completada', 'no_show', 'activa', 'cancelada'),
            costo_total: fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 8_000 }),
            isAdminActor: fc.boolean(),
            flatRowOnly: fc.boolean(),
          }),
          { minLength: 0, maxLength: 25 }
        ),
        (rawAffs, rawRes) => {
          const uniqueAffs = Array.from(new Map(rawAffs.map((a) => [a.nombre, a])).values());
          const reservas = rawRes.map((r) => ({
            ...r,
            monto_escrow: Math.round(r.costo_total * 0.4),
            monto_puerta: r.costo_total - Math.round(r.costo_total * 0.4),
            updated_at: validTsIso,
            atleta_nombre: r.isAdminActor ? 'Kinetic Oficial' : 'Carlos Ruiz',
            perfiles: r.flatRowOnly
              ? undefined
              : r.isAdminActor
              ? { email: 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx', nombre: 'Kinetic Oficial', rol: 'admin' }
              : { email: 'atleta@cliente.mx', nombre: 'Carlos Ruiz', rol: 'atleta' },
          }));

          expectTrue(isAdminOrB2BAccount({ email: 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx', nombre: 'Kinetic Oficial', rol: 'admin' }), 'admin detected');
          expectTrue(!isAdminOrB2BAccount({ email: 'atleta@cliente.mx', nombre: 'Carlos Ruiz', rol: 'atleta' }), 'athlete preserved');

          const rep = calcularCorteSemanalB2B(uniqueAffs, reservas, period);
          const sum = (fn: (x: any) => number) => rep.cortes_por_afiliado.reduce((acc, c) => acc + fn(c), 0);

          expectTrue(Math.abs(sum((c) => c.volumen_bruto_mxn) - rep.total_volumen_bruto_mxn) <= 0.01, 'bruto cent match');
          expectTrue(Math.abs(sum((c) => c.comision_kinetic_total_mxn) - rep.total_comision_kinetic_20_mxn) <= 0.01, 'comision 20% cent match');
          expectTrue(Math.abs(sum((c) => c.total_retenciones_sat_mxn) - rep.total_retenciones_sat_mxn) <= 0.01, 'SAT retenciones cent match');
          expectTrue(Math.abs(sum((c) => c.neto_dispersar_spei_mxn) - rep.total_neto_dispersar_spei_mxn) <= 0.01, 'neto SPEI cent match');
          if (rawRes.every((r) => r.isAdminActor)) expectEq(rep.total_volumen_bruto_mxn, 0, 'admin test passes never inflate partner gross');
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );
  });

  it('Propiedad 4: buildSpeiTransferClipboardPayload exige /^\\d{18}$/ y buildPartnerSpeiWhatsappUrl emite https://wa.me/\\d+ o null', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.oneof(fc.stringMatching(/^\d{18}$/), fc.string({ maxLength: 25 })),
        fc.double({ min: -1000, max: 250_000, noNaN: false }),
        fc.oneof(fc.stringMatching(/^\d{10}$/).filter((d) => !/^0+$/.test(d)), fc.string({ maxLength: 18 })),
        fc.boolean(),
        (clabeCand, rawNet, phoneCand, isEn) => {
          const slip = buildSpeiTransferClipboardPayload(
            { clabe_bancaria: clabeCand, neto_dispersar_spei_mxn: rawNet, afiliado_nombre: 'Club Kinetic Cabo', periodo_fin: '2026-11-15' },
            isEn
          );
          if (/^\d{18}$/.test(clabeCand.trim())) {
            expectTrue(slip.ok && slip.clabeClean === clabeCand.trim() && slip.clipboardText.includes(slip.clabeClean), 'valid 18-digit CLABE must pass');
          } else {
            expectTrue(!slip.ok && slip.clabeClean === '' && slip.clipboardText === '', 'invalid CLABE must fail closed');
          }

          const wa = buildPartnerSpeiWhatsappUrl({
            whatsappRaw: phoneCand, partnerName: 'Club Kinetic <script>', periodoInicio: '2026-11-09', periodoFin: '2026-11-15',
            brutoMxn: 1000, retencionSatMxn: 0, netoSpeiMxn: 800, isEn,
          });
          const digits = phoneCand.replace(/\D/g, '');
          if (digits.length >= 10 && digits.length <= 15 && !/^0+$/.test(digits)) {
            expectTrue(typeof wa === 'string' && /^https:\/\/wa\.me\/\d{11,15}\?text=/.test(wa) && !wa!.includes('<script>'), 'valid encoded wa.me URL');
          } else {
            expectEq(wa, null, 'short/invalid phone must return null');
          }
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );
  });
});

    // === src/__tests__/adversarial/sprint4AdminClubLootV275.adversarial.test.ts === //
import fc from 'fast-check';
import {
  clampChestSquashX,
  resolveLootRewardVisualKind,
} from '../../components/kineticlub/KineticLootBoxStage';
import {
  KINETIC_DARK_FROSTED_VEIL_RGBA,
  buildLiquidGlassPillStyle,
  resolveLiquidGlassSurfaceSpec,
} from '../../components/shared/KineticLiquidGlassSurface';
import { computeHasKinetiClubTabAlert } from '../../services/kineticlubTabAlertStore';
import { isOffTopicGamingSpamPost } from '../../services/kineticlubWallSnapshotStore';

const g = globalThis as Record<string, unknown>;
const describeSuite =
  typeof g.describe === 'function'
    ? (g.describe as (name: string, fn: () => void) => void)
    : (_name: string, fn: () => void) => fn();
const itCase =
  typeof g.it === 'function'
    ? (g.it as (name: string, fn: () => void) => void)
    : (_name: string, fn: () => void) => fn();

const assertTrue = (cond: boolean, msg: string) => {
  if (!cond) throw new Error(msg);
};

const parseRgbaAlpha = (rgba: string): number => {
  const m = String(rgba || '').match(/rgba?\([^,]+,[^,]+,[^,]+,\s*([0-9.]+)\s*\)/i);
  return m ? Number(m[1]) : 1;
};

describeSuite('KINETIC OS v2.7.5 Sprint 4 — Admin, KinetiClub, Purga & Cofre 3D (fast-check 500x)', () => {
  itCase(
    'Propiedad 1: isOffTopicGamingSpamPost purga 100% posts XBOX/GTA VI/GTA 6 sin afectar deportes reales',
    () => {
      fc.assert(
        fc.property(
          fc.constantFrom('xbox', 'XBOX Series X', 'GTA VI', 'gta 6', 'Grand Theft Auto VI', 'playstation ps5'),
          fc.string({ maxLength: 35 }),
          fc.string({ maxLength: 25 }),
          (spamTerm, prefix, suffix) => {
            const p1 = { contenido: `${prefix} ${spamTerm} ${suffix}`, disciplina: 'Fuerza' };
            const p2 = { bodyEs: `${prefix} ${spamTerm} ${suffix}`, bodyEn: 'Release' };
            assertTrue(isOffTopicGamingSpamPost(p1), `No purgó spam en contenido: ${spamTerm}`);
            assertTrue(isOffTopicGamingSpamPost(p2), `No purgó spam en bodyEs: ${spamTerm}`);
          }
        ),
        { numRuns: 500 }
      );

      fc.assert(
        fc.property(
          fc.constantFrom('Boxeo', 'Kickboxing', 'CrossFit', 'Pádel', 'Gym', 'Pilates Reformer', 'Barre'),
          fc.constantFrom('Sesión intensa de fuerza y técnica', 'Gran torneo nocturno en Los Cabos', 'Clase funcional matutina'),
          (disc, txt) => {
            const sportPost = { contenido: `${disc}: ${txt}`, disciplina: disc, sede: 'Kinetic Oficial' };
            assertTrue(!isOffTopicGamingSpamPost(sportPost), `Falso positivo deportivo en ${disc}`);
          }
        ),
        { numRuns: 500 }
      );
    }
  );

  itCase(
    'Propiedad 2: computeHasKinetiClubTabAlert retorna false en cero real aun con racha_dias > 0 y true ante cualquier pendiente',
    () => {
      fc.assert(
        fc.property(fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 500 }), (rachaDias) => {
          const res = computeHasKinetiClubTabAlert({
            unseenNotificationsCount: 0,
            pendingDmCount: 0,
            pendingSquadInvitesCount: 0,
            pendingSplitInvitesCount: 0,
            hasUnclaimedReadyReward: false,
            racha_dias: rachaDias,
          });
          assertTrue(res === false, `Debió retornar false con contadores en 0 y racha=${rachaDias}`);
        }),
        { numRuns: 500 }
      );

      fc.assert(
        fc.property(
          fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 20 }),
          fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 20 }),
          fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 20 }),
          fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 20 }),
          fc.boolean(),
          fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 365 }),
          (u, dm, sq, sp, rew, racha) => {
            const expected = u >= 1 || dm >= 1 || sq >= 1 || sp >= 1 || rew;
            const actual = computeHasKinetiClubTabAlert({
              unseenNotificationsCount: u,
              pendingDmCount: dm,
              pendingSquadInvitesCount: sq,
              pendingSplitInvitesCount: sp,
              hasUnclaimedReadyReward: rew,
              racha_dias: racha,
            });
            assertTrue(actual === expected, `Desfase de alerta tab: got ${actual}, expected ${expected}`);
          }
        ),
        { numRuns: 500 }
      );
    }
  );

  itCase(
    'Propiedad 3: resolveLootRewardVisualKind bloquea moneda KC con kc<=0 y clampChestSquashX mantiene rango [0.96, 1.08]',
    () => {
      fc.assert(
        fc.property(fc.integer({ min: -50, max: 0 }), fc.integer({ min: 5, max: 200 }), (nonPosKc, xpVal) => {
          const kind = resolveLootRewardVisualKind({
            id: 'xp_daily',
            type: 'xp',
            kcAmount: nonPosKc,
            amountLabel: `+${xpVal} XP`,
          });
          assertTrue(kind === 'xp', `Premio con kc=${nonPosKc} devolvió '${kind}' en vez de 'xp'`);
        }),
        { numRuns: 500 }
      );

      fc.assert(
        fc.property(fc.integer({ min: 1, max: 500 }), (posKc) => {
          const kind = resolveLootRewardVisualKind({
            id: 'loot_kc',
            type: 'kc',
            kcAmount: posKc,
            amountLabel: `+${posKc} KC`,
          });
          assertTrue(kind === 'kc', `Premio con kc=${posKc} debió devolver 'kc'`);
        }),
        { numRuns: 500 }
      );

      fc.assert(
        fc.property(
          fc.oneof(
            fc.double({ min: -10, max: 10, noNaN: false }),
            fc.constant(NaN),
            fc.constant(Infinity),
            fc.constant(-Infinity)
          ),
          (rawSquash) => {
            const clamped = clampChestSquashX(rawSquash);
            assertTrue(
              Number.isFinite(clamped) && clamped >= 0.96 && clamped <= 1.08,
              `clampChestSquashX fuera de rango [0.96, 1.08]: ${clamped}`
            );
          }
        ),
        { numRuns: 500 }
      );
    }
  );

  itCase(
    'Propiedad 4: KineticLiquidGlassSurface en modo oscuro cumple alpha >= 0.78 y elevation === 0 en Android',
    () => {
      assertTrue(parseRgbaAlpha(KINETIC_DARK_FROSTED_VEIL_RGBA) >= 0.78, 'Constante frosted dark < 0.78');
      fc.assert(
        fc.property(
          fc.boolean(),
          fc.constantFrom<'cyan' | 'purple' | 'neutral'>('cyan', 'purple', 'neutral'),
          (active, accent) => {
            const spec = resolveLiquidGlassSurfaceSpec(false, active, accent);
            const pill = buildLiquidGlassPillStyle(false, active, accent);
            const pillAlpha = parseRgbaAlpha(String(pill.backgroundColor || ''));
            const frostedAlpha = parseRgbaAlpha(spec.androidFrostedBg);
            assertTrue(spec.elevation === 0 && pill.elevation === 0, 'elevation debe ser 0 en Android');
            assertTrue(spec.alpha >= 0.78, `spec.alpha ${spec.alpha} < 0.78`);
            assertTrue(pillAlpha >= 0.78, `pill backgroundColor alpha ${pillAlpha} < 0.78`);
            assertTrue(frostedAlpha >= 0.78, `androidFrostedBg alpha ${frostedAlpha} < 0.78`);
          }
        ),
        { numRuns: 500 }
      );
    }
  );
});

    FILE: src/app/(tabs)/admin.tsx

    // === src/app/(tabs)/admin.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  Platform,
  RefreshControl,
  ScrollView,
  StatusBar,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import {
  AdminHubDashboardCards,
  baseAdm,
} from '../../components/admin/AdminHubDashboardCards';
import { AdminModuleModal, AdminModuleType } from '../../components/AdminModuleModal';
import { TacticalAlertModal } from '../../components/BovedaModals';
import { EventDetailsModal } from '../../components/EventDetailsModal';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../../components/SharedUI';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { useAdmin } from '../../hooks/useAdmin';
import {
  buildDefaultAdminEvent,
  buildEditableAdminEvent,
  normalizeAdminTopDisciplinas,
} from '../../services/admin/adminScreenHelpers';
import { isMasterAdminAccount } from '../../services/adminService';
import { COLORS, SPACING, globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { subscribeKineticRootRefresh } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';

export type WalletContextFilter = 'all' | 'membresia' | 'topup';

export default function AdminHubScreen() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { user, profile } = useAuth();
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, t, tx, language } = useKineticPreferences();
  const adm = useMemo(
    () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseAdm, resolvedTheme),
    [resolvedTheme]
  );

  const [alertConfig, setAlertConfig] = useState<any>({ visible: false });
  const showTacticalAlert = useCallback(
    (
      title: string,
      message: string,
      type = 'alert',
      onConfirm: (() => void) | null = null,
      confirmText = tx('ENTENDIDO', 'GOT IT'),
      cancelText = tx('CANCELAR', 'CANCEL')
    ) => {
      setAlertConfig({ visible: true, title, message, type, confirmText, cancelText, onConfirm });
    },
    [tx]
  );

  const canAdminDelete = useMemo(() => {
    if (!user) return false;
    const pRec = profile as Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined;
    const uRec = user as Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined;
    const rol = String(pRec?.rol ?? pRec?.tipo_cuenta ?? uRec?.rol ?? uRec?.tipo_cuenta ?? '').toLowerCase().trim();
    return isMasterAdminAccount(user.email) || rol === 'admin' || rol === 'fundador' || (rol === 'ceo' && isMasterAdminAccount(user.email));
  }, [user, profile]);

  const {
    hasHydratedAdminKpis, refreshing, isSyncing, lastUpdatedLabel, kpis, solicitudes, preRegistrosWeb,
    preRegistrosPendientesCount, ugcPendingCount, experienciasActivas, sociosReales, atletasList,
    reservasEscrowActivas, reservasHistoricas, auditLogs, distribucionNiveles,
    topDisciplinas, reporteCorteSemanal, isInjectingKC, isUpdatingFiscal, isExportingPDF,
    isSubmitting, togglingAffiliateName, isSavingManualAffiliate, isSweepingNoShow, cargarDatos, onRefresh,
    cambiarEstadoAfiliado, crearAfiliadoManual, eliminarSocioB2B, cambiarEstadoServicio,
    gestionarPreRegistroWeb, guardarEvento, eliminarEvento, eliminarCuentaAtleta, inyectarKCAtleta,
    actualizarFiscalSocio, exportarReporteMaestroPDF, exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF, copiarDatoBancario,
    ejecutarBarridoNoShow40,
  } = useAdmin(showTacticalAlert, { enabled: canAdminDelete, userId: user?.id });

  const [activeModule, setActiveModule] = useState<AdminModuleType>(null);
  const [moduleHistory, setModuleHistory] = useState<AdminModuleType[]>([]);
  const [walletContextFilter, setWalletContextFilter] = useState<WalletContextFilter>('all');
  const [isCreateModalOpen, setIsCreateModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isEditingEvent, setIsEditingEvent] = useState(false);
  const [hasScrolledHeader, setHasScrolledHeader] = useState(false);
  const hasScrolledRef = useRef(false);
  const returnModuleAfterEditRef = useRef<AdminModuleType>(null);
  const modalSwitchTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const scrollViewRef = useRef<ScrollView>(null);
  const scrollYRef = useRef(0);
  const pullStartYRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  const handlePullToRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    scrollViewRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: true });
    setWalletContextFilter('all');
    await onRefresh();
  }, [onRefresh]);

  useEffect(() => () => {
    if (modalSwitchTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(modalSwitchTimerRef.current);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    return subscribeKineticRootRefresh(() => {
      cargarDatos(true, true);
    });
  }, [cargarDatos]);

  const [nuevoEvento, setNuevoEvento] = useState<any>(() => buildDefaultAdminEvent(tx));

  const normalizedTopDisciplinas = useMemo(
    () =>
      normalizeAdminTopDisciplinas(
        topDisciplinas,
        experienciasActivas,
        solicitudes,
        sociosReales
      ),
    [topDisciplinas, experienciasActivas, solicitudes, sociosReales]
  );

  const openRootModule = useCallback(
    (mod: AdminModuleType, walletFilter: WalletContextFilter = 'all') => {
      triggerHaptic('Selection');
      const normalizedMod: AdminModuleType = mod === 'TESORERIA' ? 'DIRECTORIO_ATLETAS' : mod;
      setModuleHistory([]);
      setWalletContextFilter(walletFilter);
      setActiveModule(normalizedMod);
    },
    []
  );

  const navigateToSubModule = useCallback(
    (targetMod: AdminModuleType, walletFilter: WalletContextFilter = 'all') => {
      triggerHaptic('Selection');
      const normalizedTarget: AdminModuleType =
        targetMod === 'TESORERIA' ? 'DIRECTORIO_ATLETAS' : targetMod;
      setWalletContextFilter(walletFilter);
      setActiveModule((currentMod) => {
        if (currentMod && currentMod !== normalizedTarget) {
          setModuleHistory((prev) => [...prev, currentMod]);
        }
        return normalizedTarget;
      });
    },
    []
  );

  const handleStepBackModule = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    setModuleHistory((prevStack) => {
      if (prevStack.length > 0) {
        const nextStack = [...prevStack];
        const prevMod = nextStack.pop() || null;
        setWalletContextFilter('all');
        setActiveModule(prevMod);
        return nextStack;
      }
      setWalletContextFilter('all');
      setActiveModule(null);
      return [];
    });
  }, []);

  const handleCloseAllModules = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    setModuleHistory([]);
    setWalletContextFilter('all');
    setActiveModule(null);
  }, []);

  const abrirCreadorNuevo = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    returnModuleAfterEditRef.current = null;
    setIsEditingEvent(false);
    setNuevoEvento(buildDefaultAdminEvent(tx));
    setIsCreateModalOpen(true);
  }, [tx]);

  const abrirEditorOnTheGo = useCallback(
    (item: any) => {
      if (!item) {
        const hadModule = Boolean(activeModule);
        setModuleHistory([]);
        setWalletContextFilter('all');
        setActiveModule(null);
        if (modalSwitchTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(modalSwitchTimerRef.current);
        if (hadModule) {
          modalSwitchTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => abrirCreadorNuevo(), 190);
        } else {
          abrirCreadorNuevo();
        }
        return;
      }
      triggerHaptic('Selection');
      returnModuleAfterEditRef.current = activeModule;
      setActiveModule(null);
      setIsEditingEvent(true);
      setNuevoEvento(buildEditableAdminEvent(item, tx));
      if (modalSwitchTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(modalSwitchTimerRef.current);
      modalSwitchTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => setIsCreateModalOpen(true), 190);
    },
    [activeModule, abrirCreadorNuevo, tx]
  );

  const handleCloseEditorModal = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    setIsCreateModalOpen(false);
    const prevMod = returnModuleAfterEditRef.current;
    if (prevMod) {
      returnModuleAfterEditRef.current = null;
      if (modalSwitchTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(modalSwitchTimerRef.current);
      modalSwitchTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => setActiveModule(prevMod), 190);
    }
  }, []);

  const tokenomicsReales = useMemo(() => {
    const sumaMembresia = Math.max(0, Number(kpis?.sumaMembresia) || 0);
    const sumaTopup = Math.max(0, Number(kpis?.sumaTopup) || 0);
    const saldoDisponibleUsuarios = Math.max(
      0,
      Number(kpis?.saldoDisponibleUsuarios) || sumaMembresia + sumaTopup
    );
    const escrowTotal = Math.max(0, Number(kpis?.escrowTotal) || 0);
    const masaMonetariaTotal = Math.max(
      0,
      Number(kpis?.masaMonetariaTotal) || saldoDisponibleUsuarios + escrowTotal
    );
    const liveTotalClubs =
      Array.isArray(sociosReales) && sociosReales.length > 0
        ? sociosReales.length
        : Math.max(0, Number(kpis?.clubesTotales) || 0);
    const liveActiveClubs =
      Array.isArray(sociosReales) && sociosReales.length > 0
        ? sociosReales.filter((s: any) => Boolean(s?.esClubActivo)).length
        : Math.max(0, Number(kpis?.clubesActivos) || 0);
    const liveProspectClubs = Math.max(0, liveTotalClubs - liveActiveClubs);
    return {
      sumaMembresia,
      sumaTopup,
      saldoDisponibleUsuarios,
      escrowTotal,
      masaMonetariaTotal,
      reservasActivasCount: Math.max(0, Number(kpis?.reservasActivasCount) || 0),
      atletasCount: Math.max(0, Number(kpis?.atletasCount) || 0),
      clubesTotales: liveTotalClubs,
      clubesActivos: liveActiveClubs,
      clubesProspectos: liveProspectClubs,
    };
  }, [kpis, sociosReales]);

  const netoSpeiSemanalFormatted = useMemo(() => {
    const val = Math.max(0, Number(reporteCorteSemanal?.total_neto_dispersar_spei_mxn) || 0);
    return val.toLocaleString(language === 'en' ? 'en-US' : 'es-MX', {
      minimumFractionDigits: 2,
      maximumFractionDigits: 2,
    });
  }, [reporteCorteSemanal?.total_neto_dispersar_spei_mxn, language]);

  if (!canAdminDelete) {
    return <View style={[globalStyles.mainContainer, { backgroundColor: themeColors.background }]} />;
  }

  return (
    <View style={[globalStyles.mainContainer, { backgroundColor: themeColors.background }]}>
      <StatusBar barStyle={themeColors.statusBarStyle} backgroundColor={themeColors.background} />
      <View
        style={[
          adm.header,
          {
            paddingTop: insets.top + 14,
            borderBottomWidth: hasScrolledHeader ? 1 : 0,
            borderBottomColor: isLightMode ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.16)',
          },
        ]}
      >
        <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 10 }}>
          <Text style={[adm.headerSub, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{t('admin.sub')}</Text>
          <Text style={[adm.headerTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{t('admin.title')}</Text>
        </View>
        <View style={{ alignItems: 'flex-end', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[
              adm.refreshBtn,
              isLightMode ? baseAdm.refreshBtnLight : baseAdm.refreshBtnDark,
              {
                elevation: 0,
                minWidth: 122,
                height: 32,
                flexDirection: 'row',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                gap: 6,
                paddingVertical: 0,
                paddingHorizontal: 10,
                borderRadius: 20,
              },
            ]}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
            onPress={handlePullToRefresh}
            disabled={refreshing}
          >
            {refreshing || isSyncing ? (
              <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            ) : (
              <Feather name="refresh-cw" size={13} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            )}
            <Text
              numberOfLines={1}
              style={[
                adm.refreshBtnText,
                { color: themeColors.cyanText, fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '800', fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] },
              ]}
            >
              {refreshing || isSyncing ? tx('Sincronizando...', 'Syncing...') : lastUpdatedLabel}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        ref={scrollViewRef}
        keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
        removeClippedSubviews={false}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        onScroll={(e) => {
          const y = e.nativeEvent.contentOffset.y;
          scrollYRef.current = y;
          const nextScrolled = y > 4;
          if (nextScrolled !== hasScrolledRef.current) {
            hasScrolledRef.current = nextScrolled;
            setHasScrolledHeader(nextScrolled);
          }
        }}
        scrollEventThrottle={16}
        onTouchStart={(e) => {
          if (Platform.OS === 'web' && scrollYRef.current <= 2) {
            pullStartYRef.current = e.nativeEvent.pageY ?? (e.nativeEvent as any).clientY ?? 0;
          } else {
            pullStartYRef.current = null;
          }
        }}
        onTouchEnd={(e) => {
          if (Platform.OS === 'web' && pullStartYRef.current !== null && !refreshing) {
            const currentY = e.nativeEvent.pageY ?? (e.nativeEvent as any).clientY ?? 0;
            const deltaY = currentY - pullStartYRef.current;
            pullStartYRef.current = null;
            if (deltaY > 68) {
              handlePullToRefresh();
            }
          }
        }}
        contentContainerStyle={{ paddingHorizontal: SPACING.lg, paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 120 }}
        refreshControl={
          <RefreshControl
            refreshing={refreshing}
            onRefresh={handlePullToRefresh}
            tintColor="#00F0FF"
            colors={['#00F0FF']}
          />
        }
      >
        <AdminHubDashboardCards
          hasHydratedAdminKpis={hasHydratedAdminKpis}
          tokenomicsReales={tokenomicsReales}
          netoSpeiSemanalFormatted={netoSpeiSemanalFormatted}
          solicitudesCount={solicitudes.length}
          preRegistrosPendientesCount={preRegistrosPendientesCount}
          ugcPendingCount={ugcPendingCount}
          experienciasCount={experienciasActivas.length}
          isExportingPDF={isExportingPDF}
          openRootModule={openRootModule}
          abrirCreadorNuevo={abrirCreadorNuevo}
          exportarReporteMaestroPDF={exportarReporteMaestroPDF}
        />
      </ScrollView>

      <AdminModuleModal
        activeModule={activeModule}
        hasPreviousModule={moduleHistory.length > 0}
        walletContextFilter={walletContextFilter}
        onStepBack={handleStepBackModule}
        onCloseAll={handleCloseAllModules}
        onNavigateModule={navigateToSubModule}
        tokenomicsReales={tokenomicsReales}
        solicitudes={solicitudes} preRegistrosWeb={preRegistrosWeb} ugcPendingCount={ugcPendingCount} experienciasActivas={experienciasActivas}
        sociosReales={sociosReales} atletasList={atletasList} reservasEscrowActivas={reservasEscrowActivas}
        reservasHistoricas={reservasHistoricas} auditLogs={auditLogs} distribucionNiveles={distribucionNiveles}
        topDisciplinas={normalizedTopDisciplinas} reporteCorteSemanal={reporteCorteSemanal}
        cambiarEstadoAfiliado={cambiarEstadoAfiliado} crearAfiliadoManual={crearAfiliadoManual}
        eliminarSocioB2B={eliminarSocioB2B} canAdminDelete={canAdminDelete}
        togglingAffiliateName={togglingAffiliateName} isSavingManualAffiliate={isSavingManualAffiliate}
        isSubmitting={isSubmitting} cambiarEstadoServicio={cambiarEstadoServicio}
        gestionarPreRegistroWeb={gestionarPreRegistroWeb} guardarEvento={guardarEvento}
        eliminarEvento={eliminarEvento} eliminarCuentaAtleta={eliminarCuentaAtleta} user={user}
        abrirEditorOnTheGo={abrirEditorOnTheGo} isInjectingKC={isInjectingKC}
        inyectarKCAtleta={(atletaId: string, cantidad: string, billetera: 'membresia' | 'topup', nuevoNivel?: string | null, modo?: 'inyectar' | 'retirar' | 'reset', cb?: () => void) => inyectarKCAtleta(atletaId, cantidad, billetera, nuevoNivel, modo, user?.id, cb)}
        actualizarFiscalSocio={actualizarFiscalSocio} isUpdatingFiscal={isUpdatingFiscal}
        exportarReporteMaestroPDF={exportarReporteMaestroPDF} exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF={exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF}
        isExportingPDF={isExportingPDF} copiarDatoBancario={copiarDatoBancario}
        ejecutarBarridoNoShow40={ejecutarBarridoNoShow40} isSweepingNoShow={isSweepingNoShow}
      />

      <EventDetailsModal
        visible={isCreateModalOpen}
        isCreateMode={!isEditingEvent}
        defaultClubName={nuevoEvento?.negocio_nombre || 'Kinetic Oficial'}
        event={nuevoEvento}
        onClose={handleCloseEditorModal}
        onRefreshEvents={() => cargarDatos(true, true)}
      />

      <TacticalAlertModal
        alertConfig={alertConfig}
        setAlertConfig={setAlertConfig}
        triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic}
      />
    </View>
  );
}

    FILE: src/components/admin/AdminAffiliateDetailModal.tsx

    // === src/components/admin/AdminAffiliateDetailModal.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  Keyboard,
  Linking,
  Modal,
  Platform,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import {
  extractInstagramFromAffiliate,
  formatMxn,
  stripInstagramMetaFromHorarios,
} from '../../services/adminService';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

export interface AdminAffiliateDetailModalProps {
  visible: boolean;
  affiliate: any | null;
  canDeleteAsAdmin: boolean;
  isBusy?: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onToggleStatus: (af: any, nextStatus: 'activo' | 'inactivo') => Promise<boolean | void>;
  onSaveEdit: (payload: any, onDone?: () => void) => Promise<boolean | void>;
  onDeleteAffiliate: (af: any) => Promise<boolean | void>;
  onOpenPublicPreview?: (af: any) => void;
}

export const AdminAffiliateDetailModal: React.FC<AdminAffiliateDetailModalProps> = ({
  visible,
  affiliate,
  canDeleteAsAdmin,
  isBusy = false,
  onClose,
  onToggleStatus,
  onSaveEdit,
  onDeleteAffiliate,
  onOpenPublicPreview,
}) => {
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const styles = React.useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(rawStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
  const phColor = !isLightMode ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.38)' : '#7D8A99';

  const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);
  const [confirmDeleteStep, setConfirmDeleteStep] = useState(false);
  const [savingLocal, setSavingLocal] = useState(false);

  const [nombre, setNombre] = useState('');
  const [esEspecialista, setEsEspecialista] = useState(false);
  const [disciplina, setDisciplina] = useState('');
  const [ubicacion, setUbicacion] = useState('');
  const [horarios, setHorarios] = useState('');
  const [whatsapp, setWhatsapp] = useState('');
  const [instagram, setInstagram] = useState('');
  const [descripcion, setDescripcion] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    if (!affiliate || !visible) {
      setIsEditing(false);
      setConfirmDeleteStep(false);
      return;
    }
    setNombre(String(affiliate.nombre || '').trim());
    setEsEspecialista(Boolean(affiliate.es_especialista || String(affiliate.categoria || '').toLowerCase() === 'especialista'));
    setDisciplina(String(affiliate.disciplina || affiliate.categoria || 'Fuerza').trim());
    setUbicacion(String(affiliate.ubicacion || '').trim());
    setHorarios(stripInstagramMetaFromHorarios(affiliate.horarios) || 'Lunes a Sábado • 06:00 a 21:00');
    setWhatsapp(String(affiliate.whatsapp || affiliate.telefono || '').trim());
    setInstagram(extractInstagramFromAffiliate(affiliate));
    setDescripcion(String(affiliate.descripcion || '').trim());
    setIsEditing(false);
    setConfirmDeleteStep(false);
  }, [affiliate, visible]);

  if (!visible || !affiliate) return null;

  const handleClose = () => {
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    onClose();
  };

  const isActive = Boolean(
    affiliate.esClubActivo ?? (String(affiliate.estado || '').toLowerCase() === 'activo' || affiliate.verificado)
  );

  const handleToggle = async () => {
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    triggerHaptic('Medium');
    setSavingLocal(true);
    try {
      await onToggleStatus(
        { ...affiliate, whatsapp, instagram, horarios },
        isActive ? 'inactivo' : 'activo'
      );
    } finally {
      setSavingLocal(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSave = async () => {
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    if (!nombre.trim()) return;
    triggerHaptic('Medium');
    setSavingLocal(true);
    try {
      const ok = await onSaveEdit(
        {
          ...affiliate,
          id: affiliate.id,
          nombre: nombre.trim(),
          es_especialista: esEspecialista,
          disciplina: disciplina.trim() || 'Fuerza',
          ubicacion: ubicacion.trim() || 'Ubicación Verificada',
          horarios: horarios.trim() || 'Lunes a Sábado • 06:00 a 21:00',
          whatsapp: whatsapp.trim(),
          instagram: instagram.trim(),
          descripcion: descripcion.trim(),
          activo: isActive,
        },
        () => setIsEditing(false)
      );
      if (ok) setIsEditing(false);
    } finally {
      setSavingLocal(false);
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmFinalDelete = async () => {
    if (!canDeleteAsAdmin) return;
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    triggerHaptic('Warning');
    setSavingLocal(true);
    try {
      await onDeleteAffiliate(affiliate);
      setConfirmDeleteStep(false);
      handleClose();
    } finally {
      setSavingLocal(false);
    }
  };

  const openWhatsappDirect = () => {
    const digits = whatsapp.replace(/\D/g, '');
    if (!digits) return;
    const normalized = digits.length === 10 ? `52${digits}` : digits;
    Linking.openURL(`https://wa.me/${normalized}`).catch(() => {});
  };

  const working = isBusy || savingLocal;

  return (
    <Modal visible={visible} transparent animationType="fade" onRequestClose={handleClose}>
      <View style={styles.overlay}>
        <View style={styles.sheet}>
          <View style={styles.headerRow}>
            <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
              <Text style={styles.kicker}>
                {esEspecialista ? tx('EXPEDIENTE ESPECIALISTA B2B', 'B2B SPECIALIST RECORD') : tx('EXPEDIENTE CLUB AFILIADO', 'AFFILIATED CLUB RECORD')}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.title} numberOfLines={1}>{affiliate.nombre}</Text>
            </View>
            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.closeBtn} onPress={handleClose} activeOpacity={0.8}>
              <Feather name="x" size={18} color={COLORS.textPrimary} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled" contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 18 }}>
            <View style={styles.statusBanner}>
              <View style={[styles.statusDot, { backgroundColor: isActive ? '#00FF66' : '#FFCC00' }]} />
              <Text style={[styles.statusText, { color: isActive ? '#00FF66' : '#FFCC00' }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                {isActive ? tx('ACTIVO Y VISIBLE EN RADAR GPS', 'ACTIVE & LIVE ON GPS RADAR') : tx('OCULTO / PROSPECTO EN ESPERA', 'HIDDEN / STANDBY PROSPECT')}
              </Text>
              <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.quickToggleBtn, isActive && styles.quickToggleHide]} onPress={handleToggle} disabled={working}>
                {working ? (
                  <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={isActive ? '#FFAA00' : '#05080D'} />
                ) : (
                  <Text style={[styles.quickToggleText, isActive && { color: '#FFAA00' }]}>
                    {isActive ? tx('OCULTAR', 'HIDE') : tx('ACTIVAR', 'ACTIVATE')}
                  </Text>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.kpiRow}>
              <View style={styles.kpiBox}>
                <Text style={styles.kpiLabel}>{tx('CARTELERA', 'SESSIONS')}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.kpiVal}>{affiliate.experienciasCount || 0}</Text>
              </View>
              <View style={styles.kpiBox}>
                <Text style={styles.kpiLabel}>{tx('VOLUMEN QR', 'QR VOLUME')}</Text>
                <Text style={[styles.kpiVal, { color: '#00FF66' }]}>{formatMxn(affiliate.ingresosGenerados || 0)}</Text>
              </View>
              <View style={styles.kpiBox}>
                <Text style={styles.kpiLabel}>{tx('ESCROW 40%', '40% ESCROW')}</Text>
                <Text style={[styles.kpiVal, { color: '#CE93D8' }]}>{affiliate.enCustodia || 0} KC</Text>
              </View>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.modeTabs}>
              <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.modeTab, !isEditing && styles.modeTabActive]} onPress={() => { Keyboard.dismiss(); setIsEditing(false); setConfirmDeleteStep(false); }}>
                <Feather name="info" size={13} color={!isEditing ? '#00F0FF' : COLORS.textMuted} />
                <Text style={[styles.modeTabText, !isEditing && styles.modeTabTextActive]}>{tx('FICHA Y ACCIONES', 'SUMMARY & ACTIONS')}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.modeTab, isEditing && styles.modeTabActive]} onPress={() => { setIsEditing(true); setConfirmDeleteStep(false); }}>
                <Feather name="edit-2" size={13} color={isEditing ? '#00F0FF' : COLORS.textMuted} />
                <Text style={[styles.modeTabText, isEditing && styles.modeTabTextActive]}>{tx('EDITAR DATOS', 'EDIT RECORD')}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            {!isEditing ? (
              <View style={styles.infoSection}>
                <View style={styles.infoItem}>
                  <Feather name="award" size={14} color="#00F0FF" />
                  <Text style={styles.infoText} numberOfLines={2}>{tx('Disciplina:', 'Discipline:')} {disciplina}</Text>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.infoItem}>
                  <Feather name="map-pin" size={14} color="#00F0FF" />
                  <Text style={styles.infoText} numberOfLines={2}>{ubicacion || tx('Ubicación Verificada', 'Verified Location')}</Text>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.infoItem}>
                  <Feather name="clock" size={14} color="#00F0FF" />
                  <Text style={styles.infoText} numberOfLines={2}>{horarios}</Text>
                </View>
                {Boolean(whatsapp) && (
                  <TouchableOpacity style={styles.infoItem} onPress={openWhatsappDirect}>
                    <Feather name="phone" size={14} color="#00FF66" />
                    <Text style={[styles.infoText, { color: '#00FF66' }]} numberOfLines={1}>WhatsApp: {whatsapp}</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                )}
                {Boolean(instagram) && (
                  <View style={styles.infoItem}>
                    <Feather name="instagram" size={14} color="#E040FB" />
                    <Text style={styles.infoText} numberOfLines={1}>{instagram}</Text>
                  </View>
                )}

                <View style={styles.actionStack}>
                  <TouchableOpacity style={styles.primaryEditBtn} onPress={() => setIsEditing(true)}>
                    <Feather name="edit-3" size={15} color="#05080D" />
                    <Text style={styles.primaryEditBtnText}>{tx('EDITAR REGISTRO EN SUPABASE', 'EDIT RECORD IN SUPABASE')}</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>

                  {Boolean(onOpenPublicPreview) && (
                    <TouchableOpacity style={styles.previewBtn} onPress={() => { handleClose(); onOpenPublicPreview?.(affiliate); }}>
                      <Feather name="eye" size={14} color="#00F0FF" />
                      <Text style={styles.previewBtnText}>{tx('VER ESCAPARATE PÚBLICO RADAR', 'VIEW PUBLIC RADAR SHOWCASE')}</Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  )}

                  {canDeleteAsAdmin && !confirmDeleteStep && (
                    <TouchableOpacity style={styles.dangerTriggerBtn} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Warning'); setConfirmDeleteStep(true); }}>
                      <Feather name="trash-2" size={14} color="#FF4D4D" />
                      <Text style={styles.dangerTriggerText}>{tx('ELIMINAR REGISTRO B2B (SOLO ADMIN)', 'DELETE B2B RECORD (ADMIN ONLY)')}</Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  )}

                  {canDeleteAsAdmin && confirmDeleteStep && (
                    <View style={styles.confirmDangerBox}>
                      <Text style={styles.confirmDangerTitle}>{tx('CONFIRMACIÓN EXPRESA REQUERIDA', 'EXPRESS CONFIRMATION REQUIRED')}</Text>
                      <Text style={styles.confirmDangerSub}>
                        {tx(
                          `¿Confirmas eliminar permanentemente a "${affiliate.nombre}" de public.afiliados y retirarlo del Radar GPS?`,
                          `Do you confirm permanently deleting "${affiliate.nombre}" from public.afiliados and removing it from GPS Radar?`
                        )}
                      </Text>
                      <View style={styles.confirmBtnRow}>
                        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.cancelDeleteBtn} onPress={() => setConfirmDeleteStep(false)}>
                          <Text style={styles.cancelDeleteText}>{tx('CANCELAR', 'CANCEL')}</Text>
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.confirmDeleteBtn} onPress={handleConfirmFinalDelete} disabled={working}>
                          {working ? <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#FFF" /> : <Text style={styles.confirmDeleteText}>{tx('SÍ, ELIMINAR', 'YES, DELETE')}</Text>}
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                      </View>
                    </View>
                  )}
                </View>
              </View>
            ) : (
              <View style={styles.formSection}>
                <View style={styles.typeToggleRow}>
                  <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.typePill, !esEspecialista && styles.typePillActive]} onPress={() => setEsEspecialista(false)}>
                    <Text style={[styles.typePillText, !esEspecialista && styles.typePillTextActive]}>{tx('CLUB / SEDE', 'CLUB / VENUE')}</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                  <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.typePill, esEspecialista && styles.typePillActive]} onPress={() => setEsEspecialista(true)}>
                    <Text style={[styles.typePillText, esEspecialista && styles.typePillTextActive]}>{tx('ESPECIALISTA', 'SPECIALIST')}</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>

                <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>{tx('NOMBRE COMERCIAL', 'COMMERCIAL NAME')}</Text>
                <TextInput style={styles.input} value={nombre} onChangeText={setNombre} placeholderTextColor={phColor} placeholder={tx('Nombre del Club o Especialista', 'Club or Specialist Name')} />

                <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>{tx('DISCIPLINA PRINCIPAL', 'PRIMARY DISCIPLINE')}</Text>
                <TextInput style={styles.input} value={disciplina} onChangeText={setDisciplina} placeholderTextColor={phColor} placeholder={tx('Pilates Reformer, Boxeo, Fisioterapia...', 'Pilates Reformer, Boxing, Physical Therapy...')} />

                <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>{tx('UBICACIÓN / DIRECCIÓN', 'LOCATION / ADDRESS')}</Text>
                <TextInput style={styles.input} value={ubicacion} onChangeText={setUbicacion} placeholderTextColor={phColor} placeholder={tx('Dirección o link de Google Maps', 'Address or Google Maps link')} />

                <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>{tx('HORARIOS DE ATENCIÓN', 'OPERATING HOURS')}</Text>
                <TextInput style={styles.input} value={horarios} onChangeText={setHorarios} placeholderTextColor={phColor} placeholder={tx('Lunes a Sábado • 06:00 a 21:00', 'Mon to Sat • 06:00 to 21:00')} />

                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                    <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>WHATSAPP</Text>
                    <TextInput style={styles.input} value={whatsapp} onChangeText={setWhatsapp} keyboardType="phone-pad" placeholderTextColor={phColor} placeholder="6241234567" />
                  </View>
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                    <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>INSTAGRAM</Text>
                    <TextInput style={styles.input} value={instagram} onChangeText={setInstagram} autoCapitalize="none" placeholderTextColor={phColor} placeholder={tx('@estudio', '@studio')} />
                  </View>
                </View>

                <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>{tx('DESCRIPCIÓN', 'DESCRIPTION')}</Text>
                <TextInput style={[styles.input, { minHeight: 68, textAlignVertical: 'top' }]} multiline value={descripcion} onChangeText={setDescripcion} placeholderTextColor={phColor} placeholder={tx('Descripción breve de la sede...', 'Short venue description...')} />

                <TouchableOpacity style={styles.primaryEditBtn} onPress={handleSave} disabled={working}>
                  {working ? (
                    <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#05080D" />
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Feather name="save" size={15} color="#05080D" />
                      <Text style={styles.primaryEditBtnText}>{tx('GUARDAR CAMBIOS EN SUPABASE', 'SAVE CHANGES IN SUPABASE')}</Text>
                    </>
                  )}
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            )}
          </ScrollView>
        </View>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
};

const rawStyles = StyleSheet.create({
  overlay: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: 'rgba(4, 7, 13, 0.86)', justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', padding: 16 },
  sheet: { width: '100%', maxWidth: 480, maxHeight: Platform.OS === 'web' ? '88%' : '90%', backgroundColor: '#101622', borderRadius: 20, padding: 18, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(0,240,255,0.28)', borderTopColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.16)' },
  headerRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: 10, marginBottom: 12 },
  kicker: { color: '#00F0FF', fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 1 },
  title: { color: COLORS.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: '900', marginTop: 2 },
  closeBtn: { width: 34, height: 34, borderRadius: 10, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)' },
  statusBanner: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, padding: 10, borderRadius: 12, backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.09)', marginBottom: 10 },
  statusDot: { width: 8, height: 8, borderRadius: 4 },
  statusText: { flex: 1, minWidth: 0, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900' },
  quickToggleBtn: { paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 7, borderRadius: 8, backgroundColor: '#00FF66' },
  quickToggleHide: { backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,170,0,0.14)', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(255,170,0,0.45)' },
  quickToggleText: { color: '#05080D', fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.4 },
  kpiRow: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, marginBottom: 12 },
  kpiBox: { flex: 1, minWidth: 0, backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.03)', borderRadius: 10, padding: 9, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.07)' },
  kpiLabel: { color: COLORS.textMuted, fontSize: 9, fontWeight: '800' },
  kpiVal: { color: COLORS.textPrimary, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '900', marginTop: 2 },

interface AdminAffiliateDirectoryCardProps {
  af: any;
  styles: any;
  isTogglingThis: boolean;
  isAdminOperator: boolean;
  canAdminDelete: boolean;
  onSelectAffiliate: (af: any, editMode: boolean) => void;
  onOpenMaps: (af: any) => void;
  onPromptStrike: (af: any) => void;

    return (

                    {isSpec ? tx('ESPECIALISTA', 'SPECIALIST') : 'CLUB'} • {String(tDisc(af.disciplina || 'Fuerza')).toUpperCase()}

                  </Text>

                  {displaySchedule ? `• ${displaySchedule}` : ''}

                {af.ingresosGenerados ?? 0} KC
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.statLabel} numberOfLines={1}>{tx('Generado', 'Generated')}</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={[styles.statCell, { minWidth: 0 }]}>
              <Text style={[styles.statValue, { color: themeColors.green }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                {af.enCustodia ?? 0} KC
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.statLabel} numberOfLines={1}>{tx('Apartado 40%', '40% Escrow')}</Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', marginTop: 10 }}>

            style={[styles.radarSwitchBtn, { flex: 1, minWidth: 0, marginTop: 0 }, isActive ? styles.radarSwitchBtnOn : styles.radarSwitchBtnOff]}
