    FILE: src/components/admin/AdminAthletesDirectoryView.tsx

    // === src/components/admin/AdminAthletesDirectoryView.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { FlatList, Platform, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { isMasterAdminAccount } from '../../services/adminService';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { AdminAthleteTreasuryPanel, localizeVipTierLabel } from './AdminAthleteTreasuryPanel';
import { KineticListFilterSortBar } from '../shared/KineticListFilterSortBar';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

const resolveAthleteCode = (atl: any): string =>
  String(
    atl?.codigo_referido ||
      atl?.codigo_amigo_squad ||
      `#${String(atl?.id || '').slice(0, 6).toUpperCase()}`
  ).trim();

export const AdminAthletesDirectoryView = memo(
  ({
    atletasList = [],
    walletContextFilter = 'all',
    styles,
    inyectarKCAtleta,
    isInjectingKC = false,
    eliminarCuentaAtleta,
    canAdminDelete = false,
  }: {
    atletasList: any[];
    walletContextFilter?: 'all' | 'membresia' | 'topup';
    styles: any;
    onNavigateModule?: (mod: any, filter?: any) => void;
    onOpenInTreasury?: (athleteId: string) => void;
    inyectarKCAtleta?: any;
    isInjectingKC?: boolean;
    eliminarCuentaAtleta?: (atleta: any) => void;
    canAdminDelete?: boolean;
  }) => {
    const { isLightMode, themeColors, language, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isEn = language === 'en';
    const [dirSearch, setDirSearch] = useState('');
    const [filterTab, setFilterTab] = useState<string>(
      walletContextFilter === 'membresia'
        ? 'membresia'
        : walletContextFilter === 'topup'
        ? 'topup'
        : 'todos'
    );
    const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState<string>('kc_desc');
    const [selectedAthleteId, setSelectedAthleteId] = useState<string | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (walletContextFilter === 'membresia') setFilterTab('membresia');
      else if (walletContextFilter === 'topup') setFilterTab('topup');
      else setFilterTab('todos');
    }, [walletContextFilter]);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (selectedAthleteId && !atletasList.some((a) => String(a.id) === String(selectedAthleteId))) {
        setSelectedAthleteId(null);
      }
    }, [atletasList, selectedAthleteId]);

    const filteredDirAthletes = useMemo(() => {
      const q = dirSearch.trim().toLowerCase();
      const base = (atletasList || []).filter((a: any) => {
        if (q) {
          const code = resolveAthleteCode(a);
          const blob = `${a?.nombre || ''} ${a?.codigo_referido || ''} ${a?.codigo_amigo_squad || ''} ${code} ${a?.email || ''} ${a?.id || ''}`.toLowerCase();
          if (!blob.includes(q)) return false;
        }
        if (filterTab === 'con_saldo') return (a.saldo_total_kc || 0) > 0;
        if (filterTab === 'membresia') return (a.kc_membresia || 0) > 0;
        if (filterTab === 'topup') return (a.kc_topup || 0) > 0;
        return true;
      });
      return [...base].sort((a, b) => {
        if (sortBy === 'streak_desc') return (Number(b.racha_dias) || 0) - (Number(a.racha_dias) || 0);
        if (sortBy === 'name_az') return String(a.nombre || '').localeCompare(String(b.nombre || ''));
        return (Number(b.saldo_total_kc) || 0) - (Number(a.saldo_total_kc) || 0);
      });
    }, [atletasList, dirSearch, filterTab, sortBy]);

    const dirFilterChips = useMemo(
      () => [
        { key: 'todos', label: tx('Todos', 'All'), count: atletasList.length },
        { key: 'con_saldo', label: tx('Con Saldo KC', 'With KC Balance') },
        { key: 'membresia', label: tx('Membresía', 'Membership') },
        ...(walletContextFilter === 'topup' || filterTab === 'topup'
          ? [{ key: 'topup', label: 'Top-Up' }]
          : []),
      ],
      [atletasList.length, walletContextFilter, filterTab, tx]
    );

    const listHeader = useMemo(
      () => (
        <View style={{ paddingTop: 4 }}>
          <Text style={[styles.moduleTitle, { lineHeight: 27, paddingTop: 2, marginBottom: 4 }]}>
            {tx('Directorio y Tesorería KC', 'Athletes & KC Treasury')}
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.moduleSub}>
            {tx(
              `${atletasList.length} atletas registrados. Toca un perfil para gestionar sus Kineticoins o rango VIP in-situ.`,
              `${atletasList.length} registered athletes. Tap a profile to manage Kineticoins or VIP tier inline.`
            )}
          </Text>
          <KineticListFilterSortBar
            searchQuery={dirSearch}
            onChangeSearch={setDirSearch}
            searchPlaceholder={tx('Buscar atleta por nombre o código...', 'Search athlete by name or code...')}
            filterChips={dirFilterChips}
            activeFilter={filterTab}
            onSelectFilter={setFilterTab}
            sortOptions={[
              { key: 'kc_desc', label: tx('Mayor KC', 'Top KC') },
              { key: 'streak_desc', label: tx('Racha 🔥', 'Streak 🔥') },
              { key: 'name_az', label: 'A-Z' },
            ]}
            activeSort={sortBy}
            onSelectSort={setSortBy}
          />
        </View>
      ),
      [atletasList.length, dirFilterChips, dirSearch, filterTab, sortBy, styles, tx]
    );

    const renderAthleteItem = useCallback(
      ({ item: atl }: { item: any }) => {
        const isExpanded = String(selectedAthleteId) === String(atl.id);
        const codeStr = resolveAthleteCode(atl);
        return (
          <View style={{ marginBottom: 10 }}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[
                styles.athleteDirCard,
                isExpanded && { borderColor: isLightMode ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan },
              ]}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
              onPress={() => {
                triggerHaptic('Selection');
                setSelectedAthleteId(isExpanded ? null : String(atl.id));
              }}
            >
              <View style={styles.avatarCircle}>
                <Feather name="user" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              </View>
              <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingHorizontal: 10 }}>
                <Text style={styles.whiteBold}>{atl.nombre}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.simSub}>
                  {localizeVipTierLabel(atl.nivel, isEn).toUpperCase()} • 🔥 {atl.racha_dias || 0}D • {codeStr}
                </Text>
              </View>
              <View style={{ alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>
                <Text style={[styles.whiteBold, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                  {atl.saldo_total_kc || 0} KC
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.simSub}>
                  M: {atl.kc_membresia || 0} • T: {atl.kc_topup || 0}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            {isExpanded && (
              <View style={styles.dirInfoStrip3D}>
                <AdminAthleteTreasuryPanel
                  athlete={atl}
                  inyectarKCAtleta={inyectarKCAtleta}
                  isInjectingKC={Boolean(isInjectingKC)}
                  onClosePanel={() => setSelectedAthleteId(null)}
                />

                {canAdminDelete && !isMasterAdminAccount(atl.email) && (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    activeOpacity={0.85}
                    style={{
                      flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                      gap: 6, marginTop: 8, paddingVertical: 7, paddingHorizontal: 12,
                      borderRadius: 10, backgroundColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1)',
                      borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.35)', elevation: 0,
                    }}
                    onPress={() => eliminarCuentaAtleta?.(atl)}
                  >
                    <Feather name="trash-2" size={12} color={COLORS.red} />
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '800', color: COLORS.red, letterSpacing: 0.8 }}>
                      {tx('ELIMINAR CUENTA DE ATLETA', 'DELETE ATHLETE ACCOUNT')}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                )}
              </View>
            )}
          </View>
        );
      },
      [canAdminDelete, eliminarCuentaAtleta, inyectarKCAtleta, isEn, isInjectingKC, isLightMode, selectedAthleteId, styles, themeColors.cyanText, tx]
    );

    return (
      <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        <FlatList
          data={filteredDirAthletes}
          keyExtractor={(item, idx) => String(item?.id || idx)}
          renderItem={renderAthleteItem}
          ListHeaderComponent={listHeader}
          contentContainerStyle={{ paddingHorizontal: 4, paddingTop: 10, paddingBottom: 84 }}
          showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
          keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
          initialNumToRender={8}
          maxToRenderPerBatch={8}
          windowSize={5}
          removeClippedSubviews={false}
        />
      </View>
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/admin/AdminAthleteTreasuryPanel.tsx

    // === src/components/admin/AdminAthleteTreasuryPanel.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Animated,
  Keyboard,
  Platform,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { VIP_GEM_CONFIGS, baseStyles, localizeVipTierLabel } from '../AdminTreasuryViews.styles';
import { KineticLoader, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

export { VIP_GEM_CONFIGS, localizeVipTierLabel };

const QUICK_AMOUNTS = ['50', '100', '250', '500', '1000'] as const;
type QuickAmountPreset = (typeof QUICK_AMOUNTS)[number];

const AnimatedScaleTouchable = memo(
  ({
    onPress,
    disabled,
    wrapperStyle,
    style,
    children,
  }: {
    onPress: () => void;
    disabled?: boolean;
    wrapperStyle?: any;
    style?: any;
    children: React.ReactNode;
  }) => {
    const scaleAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(1)).current;
    const animateTo = (val: number, bounciness: number) =>
      Animated.spring(scaleAnim, {
        toValue: val,
        speed: 30,
        bounciness,
        useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web',
      }).start();
    return (
      <Animated.View style={[wrapperStyle, { transform: [{ scale: scaleAnim }] }]}>
        <TouchableOpacity
          activeOpacity={0.9}
          disabled={disabled}
          onPressIn={() => animateTo(0.94, 8)}
          onPressOut={() => animateTo(1, 12)}
          onPress={onPress}
          style={style}
        >
          {children}
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </Animated.View>
    );
  }
);

export const AdminAthleteTreasuryPanel = memo(
  ({
    athlete,
    inyectarKCAtleta,
    isInjectingKC,
    onClosePanel,
  }: {
    athlete: any;
    inyectarKCAtleta: any;
    isInjectingKC: boolean;
    onClosePanel?: () => void;
  }) => {
    const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, language, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isEn = language === 'en';
    const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
    const [subTab, setSubTab] = useState<'fondos' | 'rango'>('fondos');
    const [opMode, setOpMode] = useState<'inyectar' | 'retirar' | 'reset'>('inyectar');
    const [targetWallet, setTargetWallet] = useState<'membresia' | 'topup'>('topup');
    const [amountStr, setAmountStr] = useState<QuickAmountPreset>('100');
    const [selectedRank, setSelectedRank] = useState<string>(athlete?.nivel || 'Acceso Base');
    const [liveFeedback, setLiveFeedback] = useState<string | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
      setSelectedRank(athlete?.nivel || 'Acceso Base');
      setLiveFeedback(null);
    }, [athlete?.id, athlete?.nivel]);

    const safeAmountStr: QuickAmountPreset = (QUICK_AMOUNTS as readonly string[]).includes(amountStr)
      ? amountStr
      : '100';
    const parsedInput = parseInt(safeAmountStr, 10);

    const currentMem = Math.max(0, Number(athlete?.kc_membresia) || 0);
    const currentTop = Math.max(0, Number(athlete?.kc_topup) || 0);
    const currentTotal = currentMem + currentTop;
    const activeWalletBalance = targetWallet === 'membresia' ? currentMem : currentTop;
    const isWalletZero = activeWalletBalance <= 0;

    useEffect(() => {
      if (isWalletZero && (opMode === 'retirar' || opMode === 'reset')) {
        setOpMode('inyectar');
      }
    }, [isWalletZero, opMode]);

    if (!athlete) return null;
    const simulatedMem =
      opMode === 'reset'
        ? targetWallet === 'membresia' ? 0 : currentMem
        : targetWallet === 'membresia'
        ? opMode === 'retirar' ? Math.max(0, currentMem - parsedInput) : currentMem + parsedInput
        : currentMem;
    const simulatedTop =
      opMode === 'reset'
        ? targetWallet === 'topup' ? 0 : currentTop
        : targetWallet === 'topup'
        ? opMode === 'retirar' ? Math.max(0, currentTop - parsedInput) : currentTop + parsedInput
        : currentTop;
    const simulatedTotal = simulatedMem + simulatedTop;

    const selectedGem =
      VIP_GEM_CONFIGS.find((g) => g.dbValue.toLowerCase() === String(selectedRank).toLowerCase()) ||
      VIP_GEM_CONFIGS[0];
    const walletLabelUpper = targetWallet === 'membresia' ? tx('MEMBRESÍA', 'MEMBERSHIP') : 'TOP-UP';
    const currentRankDisplay = localizeVipTierLabel(athlete.nivel, isEn).toUpperCase();
    const selectedRankDisplay = localizeVipTierLabel(selectedRank, isEn).toUpperCase();
    const athleteUpper = String(athlete.nombre || '').toUpperCase();
    const withdrawAccent = isLightMode ? '#B91C1C' : '#FF5A52';

    const handleExecuteFunds = () => {
      Keyboard.dismiss();
      setLiveFeedback(null);
      inyectarKCAtleta?.(
        athlete.id,
        opMode === 'reset' ? '0' : String(parsedInput),
        targetWallet,
        null,
        opMode,
        () => {
          setLiveFeedback(
            opMode === 'reset'
              ? tx(`✓ Billetera ${walletLabelUpper} vaciada a 0 KC en Supabase`, `✓ ${walletLabelUpper} wallet reset to 0 KC in Supabase`)
              : opMode === 'retirar'
              ? tx(`✓ Se retiraron -${parsedInput} KC (${walletLabelUpper}) a ${athlete.nombre}`, `✓ Withdrew -${parsedInput} KC (${walletLabelUpper}) from ${athlete.nombre}`)
              : tx(`✓ Se inyectaron +${parsedInput} KC (${walletLabelUpper}) a ${athlete.nombre}`, `✓ Injected +${parsedInput} KC (${walletLabelUpper}) to ${athlete.nombre}`)
          );
        }
      );
    };

    const handleExecuteRankChange = () => {
      Keyboard.dismiss();
      setLiveFeedback(null);
      inyectarKCAtleta?.(athlete.id, '0', 'membresia', selectedRank, 'inyectar', () => {
        setLiveFeedback(
          tx(
            `✓ Rango de ${athlete.nombre} actualizado a ${selectedRankDisplay} en Supabase`,
            `✓ ${athlete.nombre}’s VIP tier updated to ${selectedRankDisplay} in Supabase`
          )
        );
      });
    };

    return (
      <View style={styles.treasuryEditorBox}>
        {onClosePanel && (
          <View style={styles.rowBetween}>
            <Text style={styles.cyanMicroTitle}>
              {tx('GESTIONAR BÓVEDA:', 'MANAGE VAULT:')} {athleteUpper}
            </Text>
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={onClosePanel}>
              <Text style={styles.closeMiniLink}>{tx('CERRAR ✕', 'CLOSE ✕')}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        )}

        {Boolean(liveFeedback) && (
          <View style={styles.liveSuccessBanner}>
            <Feather name="check-circle" size={14} color={themeColors.green} />
            <Text style={styles.liveSuccessText}>{liveFeedback}</Text>
          </View>
        )}

        <View style={[styles.rowGap8, { marginTop: 8, marginBottom: 12 }]}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            style={[styles.modeSwitchTab, subTab === 'fondos' && styles.modeSwitchTabCyan]}
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setSubTab('fondos'); }}
          >
            <Feather name="sliders" size={13} color={subTab === 'fondos' ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textMuted} />
            <Text style={[styles.modeSwitchText, subTab === 'fondos' && { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
              {tx('MOVER FONDOS (KC)', 'MOVE FUNDS (KC)')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <TouchableOpacity
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            style={[styles.modeSwitchTab, subTab === 'rango' && styles.modeSwitchTabPurple]}
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setSubTab('rango'); }}
          >
            <Feather name="award" size={13} color={subTab === 'rango' ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.textMuted} />
            <Text style={[styles.modeSwitchText, subTab === 'rango' && { color: themeColors.purpleText }]} numberOfLines={1}>
              {tx('RANGO VIP', 'VIP TIER')} ({currentRankDisplay})
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        {subTab === 'fondos' ? (
          <>
            <Text style={styles.stepLabel}>
              {tx('1. OPERACIÓN EN BÓVEDA (SIN ALTERAR RANGO VIP)', '1. VAULT OPERATION (VIP TIER UNCHANGED)')}
            </Text>
            <View style={styles.rowGap8}>
              {[
                { key: 'inyectar', label: tx('+ INYECTAR', '+ INJECT') },
                { key: 'retirar', label: tx('− RETIRAR', '− WITHDRAW') },
                { key: 'reset', label: tx('Ø VACIAR A 0', 'Ø RESET TO 0') },
              ].map((op: any) => {
                const active = opMode === op.key;
                const isOpDisabled = (op.key === 'retirar' || op.key === 'reset') && isWalletZero;
                return (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={op.key}
                    activeOpacity={0.85}
                    disabled={isOpDisabled}
                    style={[
                      styles.pillOptionBtn,
                      active && (op.key === 'reset' ? styles.quickAmtChipActiveRed : styles.pillOptionBtnActiveCyan),
                      isOpDisabled && { opacity: 0.4 },
                    ]}
                    onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setOpMode(op.key); }}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.pillOptionText,
                        active && { color: op.key === 'reset' ? withdrawAccent : themeColors.cyanText, fontWeight: '900' },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {op.label}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                );
              })}
            </View>

            <Text style={styles.stepLabel}>
              {tx('2. SELECCIONA LA BILLETERA OBJETIVO', '2. SELECT TARGET WALLET')}
            </Text>
            <View style={styles.rowGap8}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                activeOpacity={0.85}
                style={[styles.pillOptionBtn, targetWallet === 'membresia' && styles.pillOptionBtnActiveCyan]}
                onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setTargetWallet('membresia'); }}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.pillOptionText, targetWallet === 'membresia' && { color: themeColors.cyanText, fontWeight: '900' }]}>
                  {tx('Membresía', 'Membership')} ({currentMem} KC)
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <TouchableOpacity
                activeOpacity={0.85}
                style={[styles.pillOptionBtn, targetWallet === 'topup' && styles.pillOptionBtnActiveCyan]}
                onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setTargetWallet('topup'); }}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.pillOptionText, targetWallet === 'topup' && { color: themeColors.cyanText, fontWeight: '900' }]}>
                  Top-Up ({currentTop} KC)
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            {opMode !== 'reset' && (
              <>
                <Text style={styles.stepLabel}>
                  {opMode === 'retirar'
                    ? tx('3. CANTIDAD DE KINETICOINS A RETIRAR', '3. KINETICOINS AMOUNT TO WITHDRAW')
                    : tx('3. CANTIDAD DE KINETICOINS A INYECTAR', '3. KINETICOINS AMOUNT TO INJECT')}
                </Text>
                <View style={styles.rowGap6}>
                  {QUICK_AMOUNTS.map((amt) => {
                    const active = safeAmountStr === amt;
                    return (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        key={amt}
                        activeOpacity={0.85}
                        style={[
                          styles.quickAmtChip,
                          active && (opMode === 'retirar' ? styles.quickAmtChipActiveRed : styles.quickAmtChipActive),
                        ]}
                        onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setAmountStr(amt); }}
                      >
                        <Text
                          style={[
                            styles.quickAmtText,
                            active && { color: opMode === 'retirar' ? withdrawAccent : themeColors.cyanText },
                          ]}
                        >
                          {opMode === 'retirar' ? `-${amt}` : `+${amt}`} KC
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    );
                  })}
                </View>

                <View style={[styles.amountInputShell, { elevation: 0 }]} pointerEvents="none">
                  <TextInput
                    style={[
                      styles.amountInputField,
                      { fontSize: 16, elevation: 0, color: opMode === 'retirar' ? withdrawAccent : themeColors.textPrimary },
                    ]}
                    editable={false}
                    selectTextOnFocus={false}
                    caretHidden={true}
                    showSoftInputOnFocus={false}
                    value={opMode === 'retirar' ? `-${safeAmountStr}` : `+${safeAmountStr}`}
                  />
                  <View style={styles.lockedPresetBadge}>
                    <Feather name="lock" size={10} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                    <Text style={styles.lockedPresetText}>{tx('SOLO PRESETS', 'PRESETS ONLY')}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <Text style={{ color: themeColors.cyanText, fontWeight: '900', fontSize: 13 }}>KC</Text>
                </View>
              </>
            )}

            <View style={styles.simulationBox}>
              <Text style={styles.simHeader}>
                {tx(`✓ SIMULACIÓN CONTABLE EN VIVO (RANGO ${currentRankDisplay} INTACTO)`, `✓ LIVE ACCOUNTING PREVIEW (${currentRankDisplay} TIER INTACT)`)}
              </Text>
              <View style={styles.rowBetween}>
                <Text style={styles.simLabel}>{tx('Saldo Total Bóveda:', 'Total Vault Balance:')}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.simVal}>
                  <Text style={{ color: opMode === 'inyectar' ? themeColors.textSecondary : COLORS.red }}>{currentTotal} KC</Text> →{' '}
                  <Text style={{ color: opMode === 'inyectar' ? themeColors.green : '#F59E0B' }}>{simulatedTotal} KC</Text>
                </Text>
              </View>
              <Text style={styles.simSub}>
                {tx('Membresía', 'Membership')}: {currentMem} → {simulatedMem} KC • Top-Up: {currentTop} → {simulatedTop} KC
              </Text>
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity
              activeOpacity={0.88}
              style={[
                styles.confirmActionBtn,
                opMode === 'reset' && {
                  backgroundColor: COLORS.red,
                  borderColor: '#FF8A80',
                  borderTopColor: '#FFCDD2',
                  borderBottomColor: '#8E0000',
                  shadowColor: COLORS.red,
                },
              ]}
              disabled={isInjectingKC}
              onPress={handleExecuteFunds}
            >
              {isInjectingKC ? (
                <KineticLoader size={16} color={opMode === 'reset' ? '#FFFFFF' : '#050505'} />
              ) : (
                <Text style={[styles.confirmActionText, opMode === 'reset' && { color: '#FFFFFF' }]}>
                  {opMode === 'reset'
                    ? tx(`VACIAR BILLETERA ${walletLabelUpper} A 0 KC`, `RESET ${walletLabelUpper} WALLET TO 0 KC`)
                    : opMode === 'retirar'
                    ? tx(`RETIRAR ${parsedInput} KC A ${athleteUpper}`, `WITHDRAW ${parsedInput} KC FROM ${athleteUpper}`)
                    : tx(`INYECTAR ${parsedInput} KC A ${athleteUpper}`, `INJECT ${parsedInput} KC TO ${athleteUpper}`)}
                </Text>
              )}
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            <Text style={styles.stepLabel}>
              {tx('SELECCIONA EL NUEVO RANGO VIP (SIN MOVER SUS KINETICOINS)', 'SELECT NEW VIP TIER (KINETICOINS BALANCE UNCHANGED)')}
            </Text>

            <View style={styles.vipGrid}>
              {VIP_GEM_CONFIGS.map((gem) => {
                const active = gem.dbValue.toLowerCase() === String(selectedRank).toLowerCase();
                const bg = isLightMode
                  ? active ? gem.lightBgActive : gem.lightBg
                  : active ? gem.darkBgActive : gem.darkBg;
                const txtColor = isLightMode ? gem.lightText : gem.darkText;
                return (
                  <AnimatedScaleTouchable
                    key={gem.dbValue}
                    wrapperStyle={{ width: '48.4%' }}
                    onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setSelectedRank(gem.dbValue); }}
                    style={[
                      styles.gemRankBtn,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: bg,
                        borderColor: active ? gem.border : `${gem.border}88`,
                        borderTopColor: active ? gem.topBevel : `${gem.topBevel}AA`,
                        borderBottomColor: gem.bottomLip,
                        shadowColor: gem.shadow,
                        shadowOpacity: active ? 0.52 : 0.24,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <View style={styles.gemRankLeft}>
                      <Feather name={gem.icon} size={15} color={txtColor} />
                      <Text style={[styles.gemRankText, { color: txtColor, fontWeight: active ? '900' : '800' }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                        {isEn ? gem.labelEn : gem.labelEs}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                    {active && <Feather name="check-circle" size={14} color={txtColor} />}
                  </AnimatedScaleTouchable>
                );
              })}
            </View>

            <AnimatedScaleTouchable
              wrapperStyle={{ marginTop: 14 }}
              disabled={isInjectingKC}
              onPress={handleExecuteRankChange}
              style={[
                styles.confirmActionBtn,
                {
                  backgroundColor: selectedGem.ctaBg,
                  borderColor: selectedGem.border,
                  borderTopColor: selectedGem.ctaTop,
                  borderBottomColor: selectedGem.ctaBottom,
                  shadowColor: selectedGem.shadow,
                },
              ]}
            >
              {isInjectingKC ? (
                <KineticLoader size={16} color={selectedGem.ctaText} />
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Feather name={selectedGem.icon} size={15} color={selectedGem.ctaText} />
                  <Text style={[styles.confirmActionText, { color: selectedGem.ctaText }]}>
                    {tx(`ACTUALIZAR RANGO A ${selectedRankDisplay}`, `UPDATE VIP TIER TO ${selectedRankDisplay}`)}
                  </Text>
                </>
              )}
            </AnimatedScaleTouchable>
          </>
        )}
      </View>
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/admin/AdminAuditLogsView.tsx

    // === src/components/admin/AdminAuditLogsView.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useCallback, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { FlatList, Platform, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS, RADIUS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { localizeVipTierLabel } from '../AdminTreasuryViews.styles';
import { KineticListFilterSortBar } from '../shared/KineticListFilterSortBar';

type AuditCategoryKey = 'inyeccion' | 'retiro_reset' | 'rango_vip' | 'red_b2b' | 'disputas_reembolsos';

const classifyAuditRow = (row: any): AuditCategoryKey => {
  const acc = String(row?.accion || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  const kc = Number(row?.cantidad_kc) || 0;
  if (acc.includes('REEMBOLSO') || acc.includes('CANCELACION') || acc.includes('DISPUTA')) {
    return 'disputas_reembolsos';
  }
  if (acc.includes('RANGO') || acc.includes('NIVEL') || acc.includes('TIER') || acc.includes('VIP')) {
    return 'rango_vip';
  }
  if (
    acc.includes('AFILIAD') ||
    acc.includes('SOCIO') ||
    acc.includes('CLUB') ||
    acc.includes('B2B') ||
    acc.includes('FISCAL') ||
    acc.includes('SAT') ||
    acc.includes('SPEI') ||
    acc.includes('SERVICIO') ||
    acc.includes('RADAR') ||
    acc.includes('STRIKE')
  ) {
    return 'red_b2b';
  }
  if (acc.includes('RETIR') || acc.includes('RESET') || acc.includes('VACIAR') || kc < 0) {
    return 'retiro_reset';
  }
  return 'inyeccion';
};

export const AdminAuditLogsView = memo(({ auditLogs = [] }: { auditLogs?: any[] }) => {
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, language, tx, fmtDateTime } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isEn = language === 'en';
  const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);

  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [activeFilter, setActiveFilter] = useState('all');
  const [activeSort, setActiveSort] = useState('recent');

  const safeLogs = useMemo(
    () =>
      (Array.isArray(auditLogs) ? auditLogs : []).filter(
        (r: any) => !String(r?.accion || '').trim().toUpperCase().startsWith('__KINETIC_')
      ),
    [auditLogs]
  );

  const categoryCounts = useMemo(() => {
    const counts = { inyeccion: 0, retiro_reset: 0, rango_vip: 0, red_b2b: 0, disputas_reembolsos: 0 };
    safeLogs.forEach((r: any) => {
      counts[classifyAuditRow(r)] += 1;
    });
    return counts;
  }, [safeLogs]);

  const formatLogPresentation = (row: any) => {
    const cat = classifyAuditRow(row);
    const rawAcc = String(row?.accion || 'OPERACION').trim();
    const accUpper = rawAcc.toUpperCase();
    const kc = Number(row?.cantidad_kc) || 0;
    const walletLabel = accUpper.includes('MEMBRESIA')
      ? tx('Membresía', 'Membership')
      : accUpper.includes('TOPUP')
      ? 'Top-Up'
      : tx('Bóveda KC', 'KC Vault');

    if (cat === 'disputas_reembolsos') {
      const isReembolso = accUpper.includes('REEMBOLSO');
      return {
        cat,
        icon: (isReembolso ? 'rotate-ccw' : 'alert-circle') as any,
        title: isReembolso
          ? tx(`Reembolso Ejecutado • ${walletLabel}`, `Refund Executed • ${walletLabel}`)
          : tx(`Disputa / Cancelación • ${rawAcc.replace(/_/g, ' ')}`, `Dispute / Cancellation • ${rawAcc.replace(/_/g, ' ')}`),
        badgeText: tx('DISPUTA / REEMBOLSO', 'DISPUTE / REFUND'),
        accentColor: isLightMode ? '#D97706' : '#FBBF24',
        badgeStyle: isLightMode ? styles.badgeAmberLight : styles.badgeAmberDark,
      };
    }

    if (cat === 'rango_vip') {
      const extractedRank = rawAcc.replace(/^CAMBIO_RANGO_/i, '').replace(/_/g, ' ').trim();
      const rankDisplay = localizeVipTierLabel(extractedRank, isEn);
      return {
        cat,
        icon: 'award' as const,
        title: tx(`Cambio Rango VIP → ${rankDisplay}`, `VIP Tier Change → ${rankDisplay}`),
        badgeText: rankDisplay.toUpperCase(),
        accentColor: isLightMode ? '#6D28D9' : '#E0AAFF',
        badgeStyle: isLightMode ? styles.badgePurpleLight : styles.badgePurpleDark,
      };
    }

    if (cat === 'red_b2b') {
      const cleanB2B = rawAcc.replace(/_/g, ' ');
      const isStrike = accUpper.includes('STRIKE');
      return {
        cat,
        icon: (isStrike ? 'alert-triangle' : 'briefcase') as any,
        title: isStrike
          ? tx(`Sanción Calidad • ${cleanB2B}`, `Quality Sanction • ${cleanB2B}`)
          : tx(`Operación Red B2B • ${cleanB2B}`, `B2B Network Op • ${cleanB2B}`),
        badgeText: isStrike ? tx('STRIKE B2B', 'B2B STRIKE') : tx('RED B2B', 'B2B NET'),
        accentColor: isStrike ? (isLightMode ? '#DC2626' : '#FF5A52') : (isLightMode ? '#006B82' : COLORS.cyan),
        badgeStyle: isStrike
          ? (isLightMode ? styles.badgeRedLight : styles.badgeRedDark)
          : (isLightMode ? styles.badgeCyanLight : styles.badgeCyanDark),
      };
    }

    if (cat === 'retiro_reset') {
      const isReset = accUpper.includes('RESET') || accUpper.includes('VACIAR');
      const absKc = Math.abs(kc);
      return {
        cat,
        icon: isReset ? ('slash' as const) : ('arrow-down-right' as const),
        title: isReset
          ? tx(`Reset a 0 KC • Billetera ${walletLabel}`, `Reset to 0 KC • ${walletLabel} Wallet`)
          : tx(`Retiro de Fondos • ${walletLabel}`, `KC Withdrawal • ${walletLabel}`),
        badgeText: isReset ? (absKc > 0 ? `Ø -${absKc} KC` : 'Ø 0 KC') : `-${absKc} KC`,
        accentColor: isLightMode ? '#B91C1C' : '#FF5A52',
        badgeStyle: isLightMode ? styles.badgeRedLight : styles.badgeRedDark,
      };
    }

    return {
      cat,
      icon: 'arrow-up-right' as const,
      title: tx(`Inyección de Fondos • ${walletLabel}`, `KC Injection • ${walletLabel}`),
      badgeText: `+${Math.abs(kc)} KC`,
      accentColor: isLightMode ? '#047857' : COLORS.green,
      badgeStyle: isLightMode ? styles.badgeGreenLight : styles.badgeGreenDark,
    };
  };

  const filteredLogs = useMemo(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    const base = safeLogs.filter((row: any) => {
      const cat = classifyAuditRow(row);
      if (activeFilter !== 'all' && cat !== activeFilter) return false;
      if (!q) return true;
      const pres = formatLogPresentation(row);
      const blob = `${row?.atleta_nombre || ''} ${row?.atleta_codigo || ''} ${row?.admin_nombre || ''} ${row?.accion || ''} ${pres.title} ${row?.id || ''}`.toLowerCase();
      return blob.includes(q);
    });

    return [...base].sort((a, b) => {
      if (activeSort === 'kc_desc') {
        return Math.abs(Number(b?.cantidad_kc) || 0) - Math.abs(Number(a?.cantidad_kc) || 0);
      }
      if (activeSort === 'az') {
        return String(a?.atleta_nombre || '').localeCompare(String(b?.atleta_nombre || ''));
      }
      return (new Date(b?.created_at || 0).getTime() || 0) - (new Date(a?.created_at || 0).getTime() || 0);
    });
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [safeLogs, searchQuery, activeFilter, activeSort, isEn, isLightMode]);

  const listHeader = useMemo(
    () => (
      <View>
        <View style={styles.headerBanner}>
          <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 10 }}>
            <Text style={[styles.sectionTag, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
              {tx('TRAZABILIDAD EJECUTIVA • SUPABASE', 'EXECUTIVE AUDIT TRAIL • SUPABASE')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>{tx('Bitácora de Auditoría', 'Audit Logs')}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.sectionSub}>
              {tx(
                'Historial inmutable de movimientos KC, cambios de Rango VIP y red B2B.',
                'Immutable ledger of KC movements, VIP Tier changes, and B2B network ops.'
              )}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.totalCountBadge}>
            <Text style={[styles.totalCountValue, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{safeLogs.length}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.totalCountLabel}>{tx('REGISTROS', 'RECORDS')}</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <KineticListFilterSortBar
          searchQuery={searchQuery}
          onChangeSearch={setSearchQuery}
          searchPlaceholder={tx('Buscar por atleta, admin o acción...', 'Search by athlete, admin or action...')}
          filterChips={[
            { key: 'all', label: tx('Todos', 'All'), count: safeLogs.length },
            { key: 'inyeccion', label: tx('Inyección KC', 'KC Injection'), count: categoryCounts.inyeccion },
            { key: 'retiro_reset', label: tx('Retiro / Reset KC', 'KC Withdrawal / Reset'), count: categoryCounts.retiro_reset },
            { key: 'disputas_reembolsos', label: tx('Disputas / Reembolsos', 'Disputes / Refunds'), count: categoryCounts.disputas_reembolsos },
            { key: 'rango_vip', label: tx('Cambio Rango VIP', 'VIP Tier Change'), count: categoryCounts.rango_vip },
            { key: 'red_b2b', label: tx('Red B2B', 'B2B Network'), count: categoryCounts.red_b2b },
          ]}
          activeFilter={activeFilter}
          onSelectFilter={setActiveFilter}
          sortOptions={[
            { key: 'recent', label: tx('Más Recientes', 'Most Recent') },
            { key: 'kc_desc', label: tx('Mayor KC', 'Top KC') },
            { key: 'az', label: 'A-Z' },
          ]}
          activeSort={activeSort}
          onSelectSort={setActiveSort}
        />
      </View>
    ),
    [activeFilter, activeSort, categoryCounts, safeLogs.length, searchQuery, styles, themeColors.cyanText, tx]
  );

  const renderLogItem = useCallback(
    ({ item: row }: { item: any }) => {
      const pres = formatLogPresentation(row);
      const dateStr = row?.created_at
        ? fmtDateTime(row.created_at) || String(row.created_at)
        : tx('Reciente', 'Just now');
      const codeSuffix = row?.atleta_codigo ? ` (${row.atleta_codigo})` : '';

      return (
        <View style={styles.logCard3D}>
          <View style={styles.rowBetween}>
            <View style={styles.leftTitleRow}>
              <View style={[styles.iconCircle, { borderColor: `${pres.accentColor}55` }]}>
                <Feather name={pres.icon} size={14} color={pres.accentColor} />
              </View>
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <Text style={styles.logTitle} numberOfLines={1}>
                  {pres.title}
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.logTimestamp}>{dateStr}</Text>
              </View>
            </View>

            <View style={[styles.deltaBadge3D, pres.badgeStyle]}>
              <Text style={[styles.deltaBadgeText, { color: pres.accentColor }]}>{pres.badgeText}</Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.metaWell}>
            <View style={styles.metaItem}>
              <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>{tx('OBJETIVO / ATLETA', 'TARGET / ATHLETE')}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaValue} numberOfLines={1}>
                {row?.atleta_nombre || tx('Atleta Kinetic', 'Kinetic Athlete')}
                {codeSuffix}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.metaDivider} />
            <View style={styles.metaItem}>
              <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>{tx('AUTORIZADO POR', 'AUTHORIZED BY')}</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.metaValue, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                {row?.admin_nombre || 'Kinetic Admin'}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
      );
    },
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    [fmtDateTime, isEn, isLightMode, styles, themeColors.cyanText, tx]
  );

  const emptyComponent = useMemo(
    () => (
      <View style={styles.emptyCard}>
        <Feather name="shield" size={26} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
        <Text style={styles.emptyTitle}>
          {safeLogs.length === 0
            ? tx('SIN MOVIMIENTOS REGISTRADOS AÚN', 'NO AUDIT MOVEMENTS RECORDED YET')
            : tx('SIN COINCIDENCIAS EN BITÁCORA', 'NO MATCHING AUDIT RECORDS')}
        </Text>
        <Text style={styles.emptyDesc}>
          {safeLogs.length === 0
            ? tx(
                'Cada inyección, retiro, vaciado o cambio de Rango VIP ejecutado desde Tesorería KC quedará sellado aquí.',
                'Every KC injection, withdrawal, reset, or VIP Tier update executed from KC Treasury will be sealed here.'
              )
            : tx(
                'Prueba cambiando la búsqueda o seleccionando otro filtro en la barra superior.',
                'Try adjusting your search query or selecting another filter above.'
              )}
        </Text>
      </View>
    ),
    [safeLogs.length, styles, themeColors.cyanText, tx]
  );

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
      <FlatList
        data={filteredLogs}
        keyExtractor={(item, idx) => String(item?.id || `${item?.created_at || 'log'}_${idx}`)}
        renderItem={renderLogItem}
        ListHeaderComponent={listHeader}
        ListEmptyComponent={emptyComponent}
        contentContainerStyle={[styles.container, { paddingBottom: 24 }]}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
        initialNumToRender={8}
        maxToRenderPerBatch={8}
        windowSize={5}
        removeClippedSubviews={Platform.OS === 'android'}
      />
    </View>
  );
});

    FILE: src/components/admin/AdminCatalogAndMetricsView.tsx

    // === src/components/admin/AdminCatalogAndMetricsView.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { isSpecialistEventOrCategory } from '../../services/admin/adminMetricsCalculator';
import { adminService } from '../../services/adminService';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { styles as baseStyles } from '../AdminOperationsViews.styles';
import { KineticListFilterSortBar } from '../shared/KineticListFilterSortBar';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { AdminVipAndDisciplinesMetricsView } from './AdminVipAndDisciplinesMetricsView';
import { AdminWaitlistPreRegistrosSection } from './AdminWaitlistPreRegistrosSection';

const isEventExpired = (ev: any): boolean => {
  const freq = String(ev?.frecuencia || '').toLowerCase();
  if (freq && freq !== 'única vez' && freq !== 'unica vez' && freq !== 'one-time') return false;
  const dateStr = ev?.fecha || ev?.fecha_evento;
  if (!dateStr) return false;
  const t = new Date(`${String(dateStr).slice(0, 10)}T${String(ev?.hora_inicio || ev?.hora || '23:59').slice(0, 5)}:00`).getTime();
  return !isNaN(t) && t < Date.now();
};

export const AdminCatalogAndMetricsView = memo(
  ({
    mode,
    solicitudes = [],
    preRegistrosWeb = [],
    experienciasActivas = [],
    distribucionNiveles = [],
    topDisciplinas = [],
    cambiarEstadoServicio,
    gestionarPreRegistroWeb,
    copiarDatoBancario,
    guardarEvento,
    eliminarEvento,
    user,
    isSubmitting = false,
    abrirEditorOnTheGo,
  }: any) => {
    const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx, td, fmtDate } = useKineticPreferences();
    const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);

    const [eventSearch, setEventSearch] = useState('');
    const [eventFilter, setEventFilter] = useState('all');
    const [eventSort, setEventSort] = useState('revenue_desc');
    const [deletedEventIds, setDeletedEventIds] = useState<string[]>([]);

    const handleDeleteEvent = async (id: string) => {
      triggerHaptic('Warning');
      try {
        setDeletedEventIds((prev) => [...prev, String(id)]);
        if (eliminarEvento) await eliminarEvento(String(id));
        else await adminService.deleteEvent(String(id));
      } catch {
        setDeletedEventIds((prev) => prev.filter((x) => x !== String(id)));
      }
    };

    const formatEventDate = (raw?: string | null) => {
      if (!raw) return tx('Sin fecha', 'No date');
      return fmtDate(raw) || String(raw).slice(0, 10);
    };

    const visibleBaseEvents = useMemo(
      () => (experienciasActivas || []).filter((ev: any) => !deletedEventIds.includes(String(ev.id))),
      [experienciasActivas, deletedEventIds]
    );

    const eventCounts = useMemo(() => {
      let activos = 0; let inactivos = 0; let especialistas = 0;
      visibleBaseEvents.forEach((ev: any) => {
        const isInactive = ev?.estado === 'inactivo' || isEventExpired(ev);
        if (isInactive) inactivos += 1; else activos += 1;
        if (isSpecialistEventOrCategory(ev?.categoria, ev?.negocio_nombre, Boolean(ev?.es_especialista))) especialistas += 1;
      });
      return { total: visibleBaseEvents.length, activos, inactivos, especialistas };
    }, [visibleBaseEvents]);

    const filteredEvents = useMemo(() => {
      const q = eventSearch.trim().toLowerCase();
      const base = visibleBaseEvents.filter((ev: any) => {
        const isExp = isEventExpired(ev);
        const isInactive = ev?.estado === 'inactivo' || isExp;
        const isSpec = isSpecialistEventOrCategory(ev?.categoria, ev?.negocio_nombre, Boolean(ev?.es_especialista));
        const clubLower = String(ev?.negocio_nombre || '').toLowerCase();
        const isOfficial = clubLower.includes('kinetic oficial') || clubLower.includes('kinetic sports club');

        if (eventFilter === 'activos' && isInactive) return false;
        if (eventFilter === 'inactivos' && !isInactive) return false;
        if (eventFilter === 'especialistas' && !isSpec) return false;
        if (eventFilter === 'con_reservas' && (Number(ev?.reservadosCount) || 0) <= 0) return false;
        if (eventFilter === 'oficial' && !isOfficial) return false;
        if (eventFilter === 'afiliados' && isOfficial) return false;
        if (!q) return true;
        return `${ev?.nombre_servicio || ''} ${ev?.titulo || ''} ${ev?.negocio_nombre || ''} ${ev?.categoria || ''} ${ev?.instructor_nombre || ''}`
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(q);
      });

      return [...base].sort((a, b) => {
        if (eventSort === 'occ_desc') return (Number(b?.ocupacionPct) || 0) - (Number(a?.ocupacionPct) || 0);
        if (eventSort === 'price_desc') return (Number(b?.precio_kc) || 0) - (Number(a?.precio_kc) || 0);
        if (eventSort === 'date_asc') return String(a?.fecha || '').localeCompare(String(b?.fecha || ''));
        return (Number(b?.ingresosGenerados) || 0) - (Number(a?.ingresosGenerados) || 0);
      });
    }, [visibleBaseEvents, eventSearch, eventFilter, eventSort]);

    if (mode === 'SOLICITUDES') {
      return (
        <View style={styles.container}>
          <AdminWaitlistPreRegistrosSection
            preRegistrosWeb={preRegistrosWeb}
            isSubmitting={Boolean(isSubmitting)}
            onGestionarPreRegistro={gestionarPreRegistroWeb}
            onCopyText={copiarDatoBancario}
          />
          <Text style={styles.sectionTag}>
            {tx('MODERACIÓN DE CONTENIDO B2B', 'B2B CONTENT MODERATION')}
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>
            {tx('Eventos por Aprobar', 'Events Pending Approval')} ({solicitudes.length})
          </Text>

          {solicitudes.length === 0 ? (
            <View style={styles.emptyCard}>
              <Feather name="check-circle" size={26} color={COLORS.green} />
              <Text style={styles.emptyTitle}>{tx('BANDEJA AL DÍA', 'INBOX ALL CLEAR')}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.emptyDesc}>
                {tx(
                  'No hay eventos pendientes de aprobación en este momento.',
                  'There are no pending events awaiting approval right now.'
                )}
              </Text>
            </View>
          ) : (
            solicitudes.map((s: any) => (
              <View key={String(s.id)} style={styles.affiliateCard}>
                <Text style={styles.affiliateName}>
                  {s.nombre_servicio || s.titulo || tx('Evento Pendiente', 'Pending Event')}
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.contactMetaText}>
                  {s.negocio_nombre} • {td(s.categoria)} • {formatEventDate(s.fecha)} (
                  {String(s.hora_inicio || '08:00').slice(0, 5)})
                </Text>
                <View style={[styles.twoColRow, { marginTop: 12 }]}>
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    disabled={Boolean(isSubmitting)}
                    style={[styles.saveNewAffiliateBtn, { flex: 1, marginTop: 0, opacity: isSubmitting ? 0.6 : 1 }]}
                    onPress={() => cambiarEstadoServicio?.(s.id, 'aprobado')}
                  >
                    <Text style={styles.saveNewAffiliateBtnText}>{tx('APROBAR EN RADAR', 'APPROVE ON RADAR')}</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    disabled={Boolean(isSubmitting)}
                    style={[styles.rejectBtn, isSubmitting && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
                    onPress={() => cambiarEstadoServicio?.(s.id, 'rechazado')}
                  >
                    <Text style={styles.rejectBtnText}>{tx('DESCARTAR', 'REJECT')}</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
              </View>
            ))
          )}
        </View>
      );
    }

    if (mode === 'EVENTOS') {
      return (
        <View style={styles.container}>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 4 }}>
            <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 }}>
              <Text style={styles.sectionTag}>{tx('CARTELERA ACTIVA EN VIVO', 'LIVE ACTIVE SCHEDULE')}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>{tx('Eventos Publicados', 'Published Events')} ({eventCounts.total})</Text>
            </View>
            {abrirEditorOnTheGo ? (
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.pdfMiniBtn}
                onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Medium'); abrirEditorOnTheGo(null); }}
              >
                <Feather name="plus-circle" size={13} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                <Text style={styles.pdfMiniBtnText}>{tx('+ NUEVO', '+ NEW')}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ) : null}
          </View>

          <KineticListFilterSortBar
            searchQuery={eventSearch}
            onChangeSearch={setEventSearch}
            searchPlaceholder={tx('Buscar evento, club o disciplina...', 'Search event, club, or discipline...')}
            filterChips={[
              { key: 'all', label: tx('Todos', 'All'), count: eventCounts.total },
              { key: 'activos', label: tx('Activos', 'Active'), count: eventCounts.activos },
              { key: 'inactivos', label: tx('Inactivos', 'Inactive'), count: eventCounts.inactivos },
              { key: 'especialistas', label: tx('Especialistas', 'Specialists'), count: eventCounts.especialistas },
              { key: 'con_reservas', label: tx('Con Reservas', 'With Bookings') },
              { key: 'oficial', label: tx('Oficiales', 'Official') },
              { key: 'afiliados', label: tx('Clubes B2B', 'B2B Clubs') },
            ]}
            activeFilter={eventFilter}
            onSelectFilter={setEventFilter}
            sortOptions={[
              { key: 'revenue_desc', label: tx('Mayor Ingreso', 'Top Revenue') }, { key: 'occ_desc', label: tx('Ocupación ↓', 'Occupancy ↓') },
              { key: 'price_desc', label: tx('Mayor KC', 'Top KC') }, { key: 'date_asc', label: tx('Próximos', 'Upcoming') },
            ]}
            activeSort={eventSort}
            onSelectSort={setEventSort}
          />

          {filteredEvents.length === 0 ? (
            <View style={styles.emptyCard}>
              <Feather name="calendar" size={26} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
              <Text style={styles.emptyTitle}>{tx('SIN EVENTOS COINCIDENTES', 'NO MATCHING EVENTS')}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.emptyDesc}>{tx('Ajusta tu búsqueda o filtro para explorar otros eventos publicados.', 'Adjust your search or filter to browse other published events.')}</Text>
            </View>
          ) : (
            filteredEvents.map((ev: any) => {
              const isExp = isEventExpired(ev);
              const isHidden = ev?.estado === 'inactivo';
              const isInactive = isExp || isHidden;
              return (
                <View key={String(ev.id)} style={styles.affiliateCard}>
                  <View style={styles.rowBetween}>
                    <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0, paddingRight: 8 }}>
                      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, marginBottom: 4 }}>
                        <View style={isInactive ? styles.statusBadgeExpired : styles.statusBadgeActive}>
                          <Text style={{ fontSize: 9, fontWeight: '900', color: isInactive ? '#FF5A52' : COLORS.green }}>
                            {isExp ? tx('EXPIRADO', 'EXPIRED') : isHidden ? tx('INACTIVO', 'INACTIVE') : tx('ACTIVO', 'ACTIVE')}
                          </Text>
                        </View>
                        <Text style={[styles.affiliateName, { flex: 1, minWidth: 0, marginBottom: 0 }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                          {ev.nombre_servicio || ev.titulo}
                        </Text>
                      </View>
                      <Text style={styles.contactMetaText} numberOfLines={1}>
                        {ev.negocio_nombre} • {td(ev.categoria)} • {formatEventDate(ev.fecha)} • {ev.precio_kc} KC
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        style={styles.pdfMiniBtn}
                        onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Light'); if (abrirEditorOnTheGo) abrirEditorOnTheGo(ev); }}
                      >
                        <Feather name="edit-2" size={12} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                        <Text style={styles.pdfMiniBtnText}>{isInactive ? tx('REACTIVAR', 'REACTIVATE') : tx('EDITAR', 'EDIT')}</Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                      <TouchableOpacity style={styles.deleteMiniBtn} onPress={() => handleDeleteEvent(ev.id)}>
                        <Feather name="trash-2" size={12} color={isLightMode ? '#DC2626' : '#FF5A52'} />
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    </View>
                  </View>

                  <View style={styles.affiliateStatsGrid}>
                    <View style={styles.statCell}>
                      <Text style={styles.statValue}>{ev.reservadosCount ?? 0}/{ev.cupo_maximo ?? 12}</Text>
                      <Text style={styles.statLabel}>{tx('Ocupación', 'Occupancy')}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={styles.statCell}>
                      <Text style={[styles.statValue, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{ev.ocupacionPct ?? 0}%</Text>
                      <Text style={styles.statLabel}>{tx('Demanda', 'Demand')}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={styles.statCell}>
                      <Text style={[styles.statValue, { color: themeColors.green }]}>{ev.ingresosGenerados ?? 0} KC</Text>
                      <Text style={styles.statLabel}>{tx('Ingresos', 'Revenue')}</Text>
                    </View>
                  </View>
                </View>
              );
            })
          )}
        </View>
      );
    }

    return (
      <AdminVipAndDisciplinesMetricsView
        styles={styles}
        distribucionNiveles={distribucionNiveles}
        topDisciplinas={topDisciplinas}
        experienciasActivas={experienciasActivas}
      />
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/admin/AdminCreateAffiliateForm.tsx

    // === src/components/admin/AdminCreateAffiliateForm.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Linking, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { SORTED_CATEGORIES } from '../../constants/exploreData';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { normalizeInstagramHandle } from '../../services/adminService';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { extractGoogleMapsUrlAndCoords } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { styles as baseStyles } from '../AdminOperationsViews.styles';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import {
  AffiliateDisciplinePickerModal,
  AffiliateOperatingHoursModal,
  resolveCanonicalDaysEs,
  resolveShortDaysDisplay,
} from './AdminAffiliateSubModals';

const openGoogleMapsSearch = (nombre: string) => {
  triggerHaptic('Light');
  const trimmed = nombre.trim();
  const targetUrl = trimmed
    ? `https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=${encodeURIComponent(trimmed)}`
    : 'https://www.google.com/maps';
  Linking.openURL(targetUrl).catch(() => {});
};

export const AdminCreateAffiliateForm = memo(
  ({
    isSavingManualAffiliate = false,
    onSubmitAffiliate,
    onValidationError,
  }: {
    isSavingManualAffiliate?: boolean;
    onSubmitAffiliate: (payload: any) => Promise<boolean | void>;
    onValidationError: (msg: string, title?: string) => void;
  }) => {
    const { resolvedTheme, language, themeColors, tx, tDisc } = useKineticPreferences();
    const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
    const isEn = language === 'en';

    const [newNombre, setNewNombre] = useState('');
    const [newIsSpec, setNewIsSpec] = useState(false);
    const [newDisciplina, setNewDisciplina] = useState('Fuerza');
    const [newUbicacion, setNewUbicacion] = useState('');
    const [parsedCoords, setParsedCoords] = useState<{ lat: number | null; lng: number | null }>({ lat: null, lng: null });
    const [newWhatsapp, setNewWhatsapp] = useState('');
    const [newInstagram, setNewInstagram] = useState('');
    const [daysKey, setDaysKey] = useState('mon_sat');
    const [customDaysEs, setCustomDaysEs] = useState<string[]>(['Lunes', 'Miércoles', 'Viernes']);
    const [openHour, setOpenHour] = useState('06:00');
    const [closeHour, setCloseHour] = useState('21:00');
    const [newActivoInmediato, setNewActivoInmediato] = useState(true);
    const [showDiscModal, setShowDiscModal] = useState(false);
    const [hoursModalMode, setHoursModalMode] = useState<'days' | 'hours' | null>(null);
    const [localSaving, setLocalSaving] = useState(false);

    const handleUbicacionChange = (raw: string) => {
      const parsed = extractGoogleMapsUrlAndCoords(raw);
      if (parsed.url) {
        setNewUbicacion(parsed.cleanValue);
        setParsedCoords({ lat: parsed.lat, lng: parsed.lng });
      } else {
        setNewUbicacion(raw);
        if (!raw.trim()) setParsedCoords({ lat: null, lng: null });
      }
    };

    const handleSave = async () => {
      const cleanName = newNombre.trim();
      if (!cleanName) {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        onValidationError(
          tx('Escribe el nombre comercial del Club o Especialista.', 'Enter the commercial name of the Club or Specialist.'),
          tx('NOMBRE COMERCIAL REQUERIDO', 'COMMERCIAL NAME REQUIRED')
        );
        return;
      }

      const waDigits = newWhatsapp.replace(/\D/g, '');
      const cleanIg = normalizeInstagramHandle(newInstagram);
      const igCore = cleanIg.replace(/^@+/, '').trim();

      if (newActivoInmediato && (waDigits.length < 10 || igCore.length < 2)) {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        onValidationError(
          tx(
            'Para publicar un operador como ACTIVO Y VISIBLE EN RADAR GPS es obligatorio capturar su WhatsApp de 10 dígitos y su usuario oficial de Instagram (@cluboficial). Si aún no tienes ambos datos, cambia el estado inicial a PROSPECTO OCULTO.',
            'To publish an operator as ACTIVE & VISIBLE ON GPS RADAR, both a valid 10-digit WhatsApp number and an official Instagram handle (@officialclub) are mandatory. Switch initial status to HIDDEN PROSPECT if you do not have both yet.'
          ),
          tx('WHATSAPP E INSTAGRAM OBLIGATORIOS', 'WHATSAPP & INSTAGRAM MANDATORY')
        );
        return;
      }

      const canonicalHorarios = `${resolveCanonicalDaysEs(daysKey, customDaysEs)} • ${openHour} a ${closeHour}`;
      setLocalSaving(true);
      try {
        const result = await onSubmitAffiliate({
          nombre: cleanName,
          es_especialista: newIsSpec,
          disciplina: newDisciplina,
          ubicacion: newUbicacion.trim() || 'Ubicación Verificada',
          latitud: parsedCoords.lat,
          longitud: parsedCoords.lng,
          whatsapp: waDigits || newWhatsapp.trim(),
          instagram: cleanIg,
          horarios: canonicalHorarios,
          activo: newActivoInmediato,
        });
        if (result !== false) {
          setNewNombre('');
          setNewUbicacion('');
          setParsedCoords({ lat: null, lng: null });
          setNewWhatsapp('');
          setNewInstagram('');
        }
      } finally {
        setLocalSaving(false);
      }
    };

    const busy = localSaving || isSavingManualAffiliate;

    return (
      <View style={styles.createFormCard}>
        <Text style={styles.createFormTitle}>{tx('REGISTRAR NUEVO OPERADOR', 'REGISTER NEW OPERATOR')}</Text>
        <Text style={styles.createFormSub}>
          {tx(
            'Agrega cualquier gimnasio, estudio, cancha o especialista que no esté en el directorio maestro.',
            'Add any gym, studio, court, or specialist not yet listed in the master directory.'
          )}
        </Text>

        <View style={styles.typeToggleRow}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.typeToggleBtn, !newIsSpec && styles.typeToggleBtnCyan]}
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setNewIsSpec(false); }}
          >
            <Feather name="home" size={13} color={!newIsSpec ? '#050505' : themeColors.textMuted} />
            <Text style={[styles.typeToggleBtnText, !newIsSpec && { color: '#050505' }]}>
              {tx('CLUB / ESTUDIO', 'CLUB / STUDIO')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.typeToggleBtn, newIsSpec && styles.typeToggleBtnPurple]}
            onPress={() => {
              triggerHaptic('Selection');
              setNewIsSpec(true);
              if (newDisciplina === 'Fuerza') setNewDisciplina('Fisioterapia');
            }}
          >
            <Feather name="user-check" size={13} color={newIsSpec ? '#FFFFFF' : themeColors.textMuted} />
            <Text style={[styles.typeToggleBtnText, newIsSpec && { color: '#FFFFFF' }]}>
              {tx('ESPECIALISTA', 'SPECIALIST')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>{tx('NOMBRE COMERCIAL *', 'COMMERCIAL NAME *')}</Text>
        <TextInput
          style={[styles.textInput, { fontSize: 16, elevation: 0, textAlign: 'left' }]}
          placeholder={tx('Ej. Elite Padel & Recovery Studio', 'E.g. Elite Padel & Recovery Studio')}
          placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
          value={newNombre}
          onChangeText={setNewNombre}
        />

        <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>
          {tx(`DISCIPLINA PRINCIPAL (${SORTED_CATEGORIES.length})`, `PRIMARY DISCIPLINE (${SORTED_CATEGORIES.length})`)}
        </Text>
        <TouchableOpacity
          activeOpacity={0.85}
          style={styles.selectorBtn3D}
          onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Light'); setShowDiscModal(true); }}
        >
          <Feather name="layers" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <Text style={styles.selectorBtnText} numberOfLines={1}>{tDisc(newDisciplina)}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.selectorChevronText}>▼</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>{tx('DIRECCIÓN / ENLACE DE UBICACIÓN', 'ADDRESS / LOCATION LINK')}</Text>
        <TouchableOpacity
          activeOpacity={0.85}
          style={styles.mapsSearchBtn3D}
          onPress={() => openGoogleMapsSearch(newNombre)}
        >
          <Text style={[styles.mapsSearchBtnText, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
            {tx(
              '📍 Toca aquí para buscar en Google Maps y copiar el enlace',
              '📍 Tap here to search on Google Maps and copy the link'
            )}
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TextInput
          style={[styles.textInput, { fontSize: 16, elevation: 0 }]}
          placeholder={tx('Pega aquí el link de Google Maps...', 'Paste the Google Maps link here...')}
          placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
          autoCapitalize="none"
          autoCorrect={false}
          value={newUbicacion}
          onChangeText={handleUbicacionChange}
        />
        {parsedCoords.lat !== null && parsedCoords.lng !== null && (
          <Text style={{ color: themeColors.green, fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '800', marginBottom: 4 }}>
            {tx(
              `✓ Coordenadas GPS extraídas (${parsedCoords.lat.toFixed(4)}, ${parsedCoords.lng.toFixed(4)})`,
              `✓ GPS coordinates extracted (${parsedCoords.lat.toFixed(4)}, ${parsedCoords.lng.toFixed(4)})`
            )}
          </Text>
        )}

        <View style={styles.twoColRow}>
          <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
            <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>
              {tx(`WHATSAPP (10 DÍGITOS)${newActivoInmediato ? ' *' : ''}`, `WHATSAPP (10 DIGITS)${newActivoInmediato ? ' *' : ''}`)}
            </Text>
            <TextInput
              style={[styles.textInput, { fontSize: 16, elevation: 0 }]}
              placeholder="000 000 0000"
              placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
              keyboardType="phone-pad"
              maxLength={15}
              value={newWhatsapp}
              onChangeText={setNewWhatsapp}
            />
          </View>
          <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
            <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>{`INSTAGRAM${newActivoInmediato ? ' *' : ''}`}</Text>
            <TextInput
              style={[styles.textInput, { fontSize: 16, elevation: 0 }]}
              placeholder={tx('@cluboficial', '@officialclub')}
              placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
              autoCapitalize="none"
              autoCorrect={false}
              value={newInstagram}
              onChangeText={setNewInstagram}
            />
          </View>
        </View>

        <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>{tx('HORARIOS DE ATENCIÓN', 'OPERATING HOURS')}</Text>
        <View style={styles.twoColRow}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            style={[styles.selectorBtn3D, { flex: 1.1 }]}
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Light'); setHoursModalMode('days'); }}
          >
            <Feather name="calendar" size={13} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text style={styles.selectorBtnText} numberOfLines={1}>
              {resolveShortDaysDisplay(daysKey, customDaysEs, isEn)}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.selectorChevronText}>▼</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            style={[styles.selectorBtn3D, styles.selectorBtnPurple3D, { flex: 0.9 }]}
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Light'); setHoursModalMode('hours'); }}
          >
            <Feather name="clock" size={13} color={themeColors.purpleText} />
            <Text style={styles.selectorBtnText} numberOfLines={1}>
              {`${openHour} ${tx('a', 'to')} ${closeHour}`}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.selectorChevronText, { color: themeColors.purpleText }]}>▼</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.initialStatusRow, newActivoInmediato ? styles.initialStatusActive : styles.initialStatusPending]}
          onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setNewActivoInmediato((prev) => !prev); }}
        >
          <Feather name={newActivoInmediato ? 'eye' : 'eye-off'} size={15} color={newActivoInmediato ? themeColors.green : '#FFAB00'} />
          <Text style={styles.initialStatusText}>
            {newActivoInmediato
              ? tx('Estado Inicial: ACTIVO Y VISIBLE EN RADAR GPS', 'Initial Status: ACTIVE & VISIBLE ON GPS RADAR')
              : tx('Estado Inicial: GUARDAR COMO PROSPECTO OCULTO', 'Initial Status: SAVE AS HIDDEN PROSPECT')}
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.saveNewAffiliateBtn} activeOpacity={0.85} disabled={busy} onPress={handleSave}>
          <Feather name="check-circle" size={16} color="#050505" />
          <Text style={styles.saveNewAffiliateBtnText}>
            {busy ? tx('GUARDANDO EN SUPABASE...', 'SAVING TO SUPABASE...') : tx('GUARDAR Y SINCRONIZAR AFILIADO', 'SAVE & SYNC AFFILIATE')}
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <AffiliateDisciplinePickerModal
          visible={showDiscModal}
          selectedDiscipline={newDisciplina}
          onSelectDiscipline={setNewDisciplina}
          onClose={() => setShowDiscModal(false)}
        />

        <AffiliateOperatingHoursModal
          mode={hoursModalMode}
          selectedDaysKey={daysKey}
          customDaysEs={customDaysEs}
          openHour={openHour}
          closeHour={closeHour}
          onApplyDays={(nextKey, nextCustom) => {
            setDaysKey(nextKey);
            setCustomDaysEs(nextCustom);
          }}
          onApplyHours={(nextOpen, nextClose) => {
            setOpenHour(nextOpen);
            setCloseHour(nextClose);
          }}
          onClose={() => setHoursModalMode(null)}
        />
      </View>
    );
  }
);
