                {tx(
                  'Toca aquí para revisar y aceptar solicitudes desde kineticapp.com.mx',
                  'Tap here to review and accept leads from kineticapp.com.mx'
                )}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <Feather name="chevron-right" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>
        )}

        <Text style={[adm.sectionHeader, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
          {t('admin.ops_audit_modules')}
        </Text>

        <View style={adm.modulesGrid}>
          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="cardFloat"
            style={[
              adm.moduleCard,
              totalPendingReview > 0 && { borderColor: 'rgba(0, 255, 102, 0.45)' },
            ]}

          >

              <View style={[adm.kpiIconBox, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.12)' }]}>
                <Feather name="inbox" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              </View>
              {totalPendingReview > 0 && <PulseBadge color={COLORS.green} size={8} />}
            </View>
            <Text style={[adm.moduleTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
              {tx('Revisión', 'Review')}
            </Text>
            <Text
              style={[
                adm.moduleSub,
                totalPendingReview > 0 && { color: themeColors.green, fontWeight: '800' },
              ]}
            >
              {totalPendingReview} {tx('Pendientes', 'Pending')}
              {preRegistrosPendientesCount > 0
                ? tx(` • ${preRegistrosPendientesCount} Web`, ` • ${preRegistrosPendientesCount} Web`)
                : ''}
            </Text>

                <Feather name="calendar" size={16} color={themeColors.green} />
              </View>
            </View>
            <Text style={[adm.moduleTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
              {tx('Cartelera', 'Schedule')}
            </Text>

              {experienciasCount}{' '}

                ? tx('evento • Ocupación', 'event • Occupancy')
                : tx('eventos • Ocupación', 'events • Occupancy')}
            </Text>
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>

          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="cardFloat"
            style={adm.moduleCard}

          >

          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="cardFloat"
            style={adm.moduleCard}
            onPress={() => openRootModule('AUDIT_LOGS')}

            <View style={adm.kpiTopRow}>

              {tx('Bitácora de Auditoría', 'Audit Logs')}

            <Text style={[adm.moduleSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
              {tx('Movimientos KC y Rango VIP', 'KC & VIP Tier History')}
            </Text>
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>

          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="cardFloat"
            style={[
              adm.moduleCard,
              ugcPendingCount > 0 && { borderColor: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.55)' },

              {ugcPendingCount > 0 && (
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5 }}>
                  <PulseBadge color="#F59E0B" size={8} />
                  <View
                    style={{
                      backgroundColor: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.18)',
                      borderRadius: 8,
                      paddingHorizontal: 6,
                      paddingVertical: 2,
                      borderWidth: 1,
                      borderColor: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.45)',
                    }}
                  >
                    <Text style={{ color: '#F59E0B', fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900' }}>
                      {ugcPendingCount}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
              )}
            </View>
            <Text style={[adm.moduleTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
              {tx('KinetiClub', 'KinetiClub')}
            </Text>
            <Text
              style={[
                adm.moduleSub,
                {
                  color: ugcPendingCount > 0 ? '#F59E0B' : themeColors.textMuted,
                  fontWeight: ugcPendingCount > 0 ? '800' : '600',
                },
              ]}
            >
              {ugcPendingCount > 0
                ? tx(`${ugcPendingCount} reporte(s) UGC pendiente(s)`, `${ugcPendingCount} pending UGC report(s)`)
                : tx('Moderación de Muro, Historias y Equipos', 'Feed, Stories & Teams Moderation')}
            </Text>
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>

          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="cardFloat"
            onPress={() => {
              triggerHaptic('Selection');
              abrirCreadorNuevo();
            }}
            style={[
              adm.moduleCard,
              {
                borderColor: isLightMode ? '#0284C7' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.42)',
              },
            ]}
          >
            <View style={adm.kpiTopRow}>
              <View
                style={[
                  adm.kpiIconBox,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.14)',
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Feather name="plus-circle" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              </View>
              <Feather name="arrow-up-right" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            </View>
            <Text
              style={[
                adm.moduleTitle,
                {
                  color: themeColors.cyanText,
                },
              ]}
            >
              {tx('Crear Evento', 'Create Event')}
            </Text>
            <Text
              style={[
                adm.moduleSub,
                {
                  color: themeColors.textMuted,
                },
              ]}
            >
              {tx('Publicar en cartelera oficial', 'Publish to official lineup')}
            </Text>
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>
        </View>
      </>
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/admin/AdminKinetiClubModerationView.tsx

    // === src/components/admin/AdminKinetiClubModerationView.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import {
  getInitialKinetiClubModerationSnapshot,
  updateKinetiClubUgcReportStatus,
} from '../../services/admin/adminOpsHelpers';
import { adminService } from '../../services/adminService';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { baseStyles } from '../../styles/adminKinetiClubModeration.styles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { KineticPhysicsPressable } from '../../utils/kineticPhysicsEngine';
import { TacticalAlertModal } from '../BovedaPassAndReceiptModals';
import { SmartImage, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

export const AdminKinetiClubModerationView = memo(({
  ugcPendingCount = 0,
  onRequestAlert,
}: {
  ugcPendingCount?: number;
  onRequestAlert?: (cfg: any) => void;
}) => {
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx, fmtDate, tDisc } = useKineticPreferences();
  const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
  const initialSnap = useMemo(() => getInitialKinetiClubModerationSnapshot(), []);

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'muro' | 'historias' | 'equipos' | 'reportes'>(
    ugcPendingCount > 0 ? 'reportes' : 'muro'
  );
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [isRefreshing, setIsRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [posts, setPosts] = useState<any[]>(() => initialSnap.posts);
  const [stories, setStories] = useState<any[]>(() => initialSnap.stories);
  const [teams, setTeams] = useState<any[]>(() => initialSnap.teams);
  const [reports, setReports] = useState<any[]>(() => initialSnap.reports);
  const [alertConfig, setAlertConfig] = useState<any>({ visible: false });
  const openAlert = onRequestAlert || setAlertConfig;
  const loadingRef = useRef(false);
  const resolvingIdsRef = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const deleteBusyIdsRef = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const isMountedRef = useRef(true);
  const autoOpenedPendingRef = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => () => { isMountedRef.current = false; }, []);

  const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
    if (loadingRef.current) return;
    loadingRef.current = true;
    setIsRefreshing(true);
    try {
      const data = await adminService.fetchKinetiClubModerationData();
      setPosts(data.posts || []);
      setStories(data.stories || []);
      setTeams(data.teams || []);
      const fetchedReports = (data as any).reports || [];
      setReports(fetchedReports);
      if (
        !autoOpenedPendingRef.current &&
        fetchedReports.some((r: any) => String(r?.estado || 'pendiente').toLowerCase().trim() === 'pendiente')
      ) {
        autoOpenedPendingRef.current = true;
        setActiveTab('reportes');
      }
    } catch {
      // Conservar snapshot SWR previo ante error de red
    } finally {
      loadingRef.current = false;
      setIsRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, [loadData]);

  const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
  const filteredPosts = useMemo(
    () => (!q ? posts : posts.filter((p) => `${p.autor_nombre || ''} ${p.contenido || ''} ${p.disciplina || ''}`.toLowerCase().includes(q))),
    [posts, q]
  );
  const filteredStories = useMemo(
    () => (!q ? stories : stories.filter((s) => `${s.autor_nombre || ''} ${s.contenido || ''} ${s.disciplina || ''}`.toLowerCase().includes(q))),
    [stories, q]
  );
  const filteredTeams = useMemo(
    () => (!q ? teams : teams.filter((t) => `${t.nombre || ''} ${t.descripcion || ''} ${t.creador_nombre || ''}`.toLowerCase().includes(q))),
    [teams, q]
  );
  const filteredReports = useMemo(
    () =>
      !q
        ? reports
        : reports.filter((r) =>
            `${r.tipo_contenido || ''} ${r.categoria_motivo || ''} ${r.detalle || ''} ${r.estado || ''} ${r.reporter_nombre || ''} ${r.target_nombre || ''}`
              .toLowerCase()
              .includes(q)
          ),
    [reports, q]
  );

  const pendingReportsCount = useMemo(
    () => reports.filter((r) => String(r?.estado || 'pendiente').toLowerCase().trim() === 'pendiente').length,
    [reports]
  );

  const handleResolveReport = async (reportId: string, estado: 'revisado' | 'accion_tomada' | 'descartado') => {
    const rid = String(reportId || '').trim();
    if (!rid || resolvingIdsRef.current.has(rid)) return;
    resolvingIdsRef.current.add(rid);
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    setReports((prev) => prev.map((r) => (String(r.id) === rid ? { ...r, estado } : r)));
    try {
      await updateKinetiClubUgcReportStatus(rid, estado);
    } finally {
      resolvingIdsRef.current.delete(rid);
    }
  };

  const handleDeletePost = (id: string, autor: string) => {
    triggerHaptic('Warning');
    openAlert({
      visible: true,
      title: tx('¿MODERAR PUBLICACIÓN?', 'MODERATE POST?'),
      message: tx(`¿Eliminar permanentemente la publicación de ${autor} en el Muro Social?`, `Permanently delete post by ${autor} from the Social Feed?`),
      type: 'warning',
      confirmText: tx('SÍ, ELIMINAR', 'YES, DELETE'),
      cancelText: tx('CANCELAR', 'CANCEL'),
      onConfirm: async () => {
        const key = `post:${id}`;
        if (deleteBusyIdsRef.current.has(key)) return;
        deleteBusyIdsRef.current.add(key);
        let rollbackList: any[] = [];
        try {
          triggerHaptic('Heavy');
          setPosts((prev) => { rollbackList = prev; return prev.filter((p) => String(p.id) !== String(id)); });
          await adminService.deleteKinetiClubPost(String(id));
        } catch {
          if (isMountedRef.current && rollbackList.length > 0) setPosts(rollbackList);
        } finally {
          deleteBusyIdsRef.current.delete(key);
        }
      },
    });
  };

  const handleDeleteStory = (story: any) => {
    triggerHaptic('Warning');
    const autor = story.autor_nombre || 'Atleta Kinetic';
    openAlert({
      visible: true,
      title: tx('¿RETIRAR HISTORIA 24H?', 'REMOVE 24H STORY?'),
      message: tx(`¿Retirar inmediatamente la historia 24h de ${autor}?`, `Immediately remove 24h story by ${autor}?`),
      type: 'warning',
      confirmText: tx('SÍ, RETIRAR', 'YES, REMOVE'),
      cancelText: tx('CANCELAR', 'CANCEL'),
      onConfirm: async () => {
        const key = `story:${story.id}`;
        if (deleteBusyIdsRef.current.has(key)) return;
        deleteBusyIdsRef.current.add(key);
        let rollbackList: any[] = [];
        try {
          triggerHaptic('Heavy');
          setStories((prev) => { rollbackList = prev; return prev.filter((s) => String(s.id) !== String(story.id)); });
          await adminService.deleteKinetiClubStory(String(story.id), story.ownerKey, autor);
        } catch {
          if (isMountedRef.current && rollbackList.length > 0) setStories(rollbackList);
        } finally {
          deleteBusyIdsRef.current.delete(key);
        }
      },
    });
  };

  const handleDeleteTeam = (id: string, nombre: string) => {
    triggerHaptic('Warning');
    openAlert({
      visible: true,
      title: tx('¿DESMANTELAR EQUIPO?', 'DISBAND TEAM?'),
      message: tx(`¿Eliminar el equipo deportivo "${nombre}" de KinetiClub?`, `Disband and delete sports team "${nombre}" from KinetiClub?`),
      type: 'warning',
      confirmText: tx('SÍ, ELIMINAR', 'YES, DELETE'),
      cancelText: tx('CANCELAR', 'CANCEL'),
      onConfirm: async () => {
        const key = `team:${id}`;
        if (deleteBusyIdsRef.current.has(key)) return;
        deleteBusyIdsRef.current.add(key);
        let rollbackList: any[] = [];
        try {
          triggerHaptic('Heavy');
          setTeams((prev) => { rollbackList = prev; return prev.filter((t) => String(t.id) !== String(id)); });
          await adminService.deleteKinetiClubTeam(String(id));
        } catch {
          if (isMountedRef.current && rollbackList.length > 0) setTeams(rollbackList);
        } finally {
          deleteBusyIdsRef.current.delete(key);
        }
      },
    });
  };

  const formatReportStatusLabel = (rawState: string): string => {
    const low = String(rawState || 'pendiente').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (low === 'accion_tomada') return tx('ACCIÓN TOMADA', 'ACTION TAKEN');
    if (low === 'revisado') return tx('REVISADO', 'REVIEWED');
    if (low === 'descartado') return tx('DESCARTADO', 'DISMISSED');
    return tx('PENDIENTE', 'PENDING');
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <View style={styles.topHeaderRow}>
        <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTag}>{tx('MODERACIÓN DE COMUNIDAD', 'COMMUNITY MODERATION')}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>KinetiClub Hub</Text>
          <Text style={styles.sectionSub}>
            {tx('Supervisión en tiempo real de muro social, historias 24h y equipos.', 'Real-time supervision of social feed, 24h stories, and teams.')}
          </Text>
        </View>
        <KineticPhysicsPressable preset="tactileChip" style={[styles.syncMiniBtn, isRefreshing && { opacity: 0.65 }]} disabled={isRefreshing} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Light'); loadData(); }}>
          <Feather name="refresh-cw" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
        </KineticPhysicsPressable>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.e2eePrivacyBanner}>
        <Feather name="lock" size={13} color={COLORS.green} />
        <Text style={[styles.e2eeDescription, { color: themeColors.textSecondary, flex: 1 }]}>
          {tx(
            'Candado E2EE: los chats 1 a 1 son privados • Solo moderas muro, historias, equipos y reportes.',
            'E2EE Lock: 1-on-1 chats are private • Moderating feed, stories, teams & reports only.'
          )}
        </Text>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.searchBox}>
        <Feather name="search" size={15} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
        <TextInput
          style={[styles.searchInput, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}
          value={searchQuery}
          onChangeText={setSearchQuery}
          placeholder={tx('Buscar por autor, contenido o equipo...', 'Search by author, content, or team...')}
          placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
        />
        {Boolean(searchQuery) && (
          <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setSearchQuery('')}>
            <Feather name="x-circle" size={15} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </View>

      <View style={styles.tabBarRow}>
        <KineticPhysicsPressable preset="tactileChip" style={[styles.tabBtn3D, activeTab === 'muro' && styles.tabBtn3DActive]} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setActiveTab('muro'); }}>
          <Feather name="message-square" size={13} color={activeTab === 'muro' ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textMuted} />
          <Text style={[styles.tabBtnText, activeTab === 'muro' && { color: themeColors.cyanText }]} numberOfLines={1}>
            {tx('Muro', 'Feed')} ({posts.length})
          </Text>
        </KineticPhysicsPressable>

        <KineticPhysicsPressable preset="tactileChip" style={[styles.tabBtn3D, activeTab === 'historias' && styles.tabBtn3DActive]} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setActiveTab('historias'); }}>
          <Feather name="clock" size={13} color={activeTab === 'historias' ? '#FF007F' : themeColors.textMuted} />
          <Text style={[styles.tabBtnText, activeTab === 'historias' && { color: '#FF007F' }]} numberOfLines={1}>
            {tx('Historias', 'Stories')} ({stories.length})
          </Text>
        </KineticPhysicsPressable>

        <KineticPhysicsPressable preset="tactileChip" style={[styles.tabBtn3D, activeTab === 'equipos' && styles.tabBtn3DActive]} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setActiveTab('equipos'); }}>
          <Feather name="users" size={13} color={activeTab === 'equipos' ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.textMuted} />
          <Text style={[styles.tabBtnText, activeTab === 'equipos' && { color: themeColors.purpleText }]} numberOfLines={1}>
            {tx('Equipos', 'Teams')} ({teams.length})
          </Text>
        </KineticPhysicsPressable>

        <KineticPhysicsPressable preset="tactileChip" style={[styles.tabBtn3D, activeTab === 'reportes' && styles.tabBtn3DActive]} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setActiveTab('reportes'); }}>
          <Feather name="flag" size={13} color={activeTab === 'reportes' ? '#F59E0B' : themeColors.textMuted} />
          <Text style={[styles.tabBtnText, activeTab === 'reportes' && { color: '#F59E0B' }]} numberOfLines={1}>
            {tx('Reportes', 'Reports')} ({pendingReportsCount})
          </Text>
        </KineticPhysicsPressable>
      </View>

      {activeTab === 'muro' ? (
        filteredPosts.length === 0 ? (
          <View style={styles.emptyBox}>
            <Feather name="check-circle" size={20} color={COLORS.green} />
            <Text style={[styles.emptyText, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>
              {isRefreshing
                ? tx('Verificando nuevas publicaciones...', 'Checking for new feed posts...')
                : tx('Sin publicaciones pendientes o activas en el muro.', 'No active or matching posts in the social feed.')}
            </Text>
          </View>
        ) : (
          <View style={{ gap: 10 }}>
            {filteredPosts.map((post) => (
              <View key={String(post.id)} style={styles.card3D}>
                <View style={styles.cardHeaderRow}>
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                    <Text style={[styles.authorName, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>{post.autor_nombre}</Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.cardDateMeta, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                      {fmtDate(post.created_at) || tx('Reciente', 'Recent')} • {tDisc(post.disciplina || 'Comunidad')}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                  <TouchableOpacity style={styles.deleteMiniBtn} onPress={() => handleDeletePost(post.id, post.autor_nombre)}>
                    <Feather name="trash-2" size={13} color={isLightMode ? '#B91C1C' : '#FF5A52'} />
                    <Text style={[styles.deleteMiniBtnText, { color: isLightMode ? '#B91C1C' : '#FF5A52' }]}>{tx('ELIMINAR', 'DELETE')}</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
                  {Boolean(post.imagen_url) && (
                    <SmartImage
                      source={{ uri: String(post.imagen_url) }}
                      style={styles.postThumbImg}
                      contentFit="cover"
                      fallbackCategory={post.disciplina || 'Fuerza'}
                    />
                  )}
                  <Text style={[styles.cardBodyText, { color: themeColors.textSecondary, flex: 1 }]}>{post.contenido}</Text>
                </View>
              </View>
            ))}
          </View>
        )
      ) : activeTab === 'historias' ? (
        filteredStories.length === 0 ? (
          <View style={styles.emptyBox}>
            <Feather name="clock" size={20} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
            <Text style={[styles.emptyText, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{tx('No hay historias efímeras 24h activas en este momento.', 'No active 24h stories right now.')}</Text>
          </View>
        ) : (
          <View style={{ gap: 10 }}>
            {filteredStories.map((story) => (
              <View key={String(story.id)} style={styles.card3D}>
                <View style={styles.cardHeaderRow}>
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                    <Text style={[styles.authorName, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>{story.autor_nombre}</Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.cardDateMeta, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                      {story.disciplina || 'STORY 24H'} • {fmtDate(story.created_at) || tx('Activa', 'Active')}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                  <TouchableOpacity style={styles.deleteMiniBtn} onPress={() => handleDeleteStory(story)}>
                    <Feather name="trash-2" size={13} color={isLightMode ? '#B91C1C' : '#FF5A52'} />
                    <Text style={[styles.deleteMiniBtnText, { color: isLightMode ? '#B91C1C' : '#FF5A52' }]}>{tx('RETIRAR', 'REMOVE')}</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
                <Text style={[styles.cardBodyText, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{story.contenido}</Text>
              </View>
            ))}
          </View>
        )
      ) : activeTab === 'equipos' ? (
        filteredTeams.length === 0 ? (
          <View style={styles.emptyBox}>
            <Feather name="users" size={20} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
            <Text style={[styles.emptyText, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{tx('No hay equipos públicos registrados en KinetiClub.', 'No public teams registered in KinetiClub.')}</Text>
          </View>
        ) : (
          <View style={{ gap: 10 }}>
            {filteredTeams.map((team) => (
              <View key={String(team.id)} style={styles.card3D}>
                <View style={styles.cardHeaderRow}>
                  <View style={styles.teamEmblemBox}>
                    {team.avatar_url ? (
                      <SmartImage source={{ uri: String(team.avatar_url) }} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} contentFit="cover" fallbackCategory={team.disciplina || 'Fuerza'} />
                    ) : (
                      <Feather name="shield" size={18} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                    )}
                  </View>
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                    <Text style={[styles.authorName, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>{team.nombre}</Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.cardDateMeta, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                      {tx('Capitán:', 'Captain:')} {team.creador_nombre || 'Coach'}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                  <TouchableOpacity style={styles.deleteMiniBtn} onPress={() => handleDeleteTeam(team.id, team.nombre)}>
                    <Feather name="trash-2" size={13} color={isLightMode ? '#B91C1C' : '#FF5A52'} />
                    <Text style={[styles.deleteMiniBtnText, { color: isLightMode ? '#B91C1C' : '#FF5A52' }]}>{tx('ELIMINAR', 'DELETE')}</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 6, marginBottom: 7 }}>
                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, paddingHorizontal: 8, paddingVertical: 3, borderRadius: 8, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#E0F2FE' : 'rgba(0,240,255,0.12)', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: isLightMode ? '#7DD3FC' : 'rgba(0,240,255,0.35)', elevation: 0 }}>
                    <Feather name="activity" size={11} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.cyanText }}>{tDisc(team.disciplina || 'Fuerza')}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, paddingHorizontal: 8, paddingVertical: 3, borderRadius: 8, backgroundColor: isLightMode ? '#F3E8FF' : 'rgba(157,78,221,0.16)', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: isLightMode ? '#C4B5FD' : 'rgba(157,78,221,0.45)', elevation: 0 }}>
                    <Feather name="users" size={11} color={themeColors.purpleText} />
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.purpleText }}>
                      {team.miembros_count || 1} {(team.miembros_count || 1) === 1 ? tx('miembro', 'member') : tx('miembros', 'members')}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
                <Text style={[styles.cardBodyText, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{team.descripcion}</Text>
              </View>
            ))}
          </View>
        )
      ) : filteredReports.length === 0 ? (
        <View style={styles.emptyBox}>
          <Feather name="shield" size={20} color={COLORS.green} />
          <Text style={[styles.emptyText, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>
            {tx('Cero reportes UGC pendientes en la comunidad.', 'Zero pending UGC reports in the community.')}
          </Text>
        </View>
      ) : (
        <View style={{ gap: 10 }}>
          {filteredReports.map((rep) => {
            const st = String(rep.estado || 'pendiente').toLowerCase().trim();
            const stColor = st === 'accion_tomada' ? (isLightMode ? '#047857' : COLORS.green) : st === 'revisado' ? themeColors.cyanText : st === 'descartado' ? themeColors.textMuted : '#D97706';
            return (
              <View key={String(rep.id)} style={styles.card3D}>
                <View style={styles.cardHeaderRow}>
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                    <Text style={[styles.authorName, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                      {`${String(rep.tipo_contenido || 'post').toUpperCase()} • ${rep.categoria_motivo || 'inapropiado'}`}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.cardDateMeta, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                      {tx('Reportó:', 'Reporter:')} {rep.reporter_nombre || 'Atleta Kinetic'} • {tx('Reportado:', 'Reported:')} {rep.target_nombre || 'Atleta Kinetic'}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.cardDateMeta, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                      {fmtDate(rep.created_at) || tx('Reciente', 'Recent')}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                  <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', color: stColor, flexShrink: 0 }}>
                    {formatReportStatusLabel(st)}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                {Boolean(rep.detalle) && (
                  <Text style={[styles.cardBodyText, { color: themeColors.textSecondary, marginBottom: 8 }]}>
                    {rep.detalle}
                  </Text>
                )}
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                  {([
                    { k: 'revisado' as const, l: tx('Revisado', 'Reviewed'), c: themeColors.cyanText, activeBg: isLightMode ? '#E0F2FE' : 'rgba(0,240,255,0.18)' },
                    { k: 'accion_tomada' as const, l: tx('Acción Tomada', 'Action Taken'), c: isLightMode ? '#047857' : COLORS.green, activeBg: isLightMode ? '#DCFCE7' : 'rgba(0,255,102,0.18)' },
                    { k: 'descartado' as const, l: tx('Descartar', 'Dismiss'), c: isLightMode ? '#475569' : '#CBD5E1', activeBg: isLightMode ? '#E2E8F0' : 'rgba(148,163,184,0.22)' },
                  ]).map((act) => {
                    const isSelected = st === act.k;
                    return (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        key={act.k}
                        activeOpacity={0.82}
                        style={[
                          styles.reportActionBtn,
                          isSelected && {
                            backgroundColor: act.activeBg,
                            borderColor: act.c,
                            borderBottomColor: act.c,
                            borderWidth: 1.5,
                            borderBottomWidth: 3,
                          },
                        ]}
                        onPress={() => handleResolveReport(String(rep.id), act.k)}
                      >
                        {isSelected && <Feather name="check" size={11} color={act.c} />}
                        <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', color: isSelected ? act.c : themeColors.textMuted }}>
                          {act.l}
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    );
                  })}
                </View>
              </View>
            );
          })}
        </View>
      )}

      {!onRequestAlert && <TacticalAlertModal inlineOverlay alertConfig={alertConfig} setAlertConfig={setAlertConfig} triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic} />}
    </View>
  );
});

    // === src/components/admin/AdminVipAndDisciplinesMetricsView.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { formatMxn } from '../../services/adminService';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { GpuProgressBar, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

export const AdminVipAndDisciplinesMetricsView = memo(
  ({
    styles,
    distribucionNiveles = [],
    topDisciplinas = [],
    experienciasActivas = [],
  }: {
    styles: any;
    distribucionNiveles: any[];
    topDisciplinas: any[];
    experienciasActivas: any[];
  }) => {
    const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx, td } = useKineticPreferences();
    const mStyles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(metricStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
    const [expandedTierKey, setExpandedTierKey] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [expandedDiscKey, setExpandedDiscKey] = useState<string | null>(null);

    const resolveLocalizedTierMeta = (tier: any) => {
      const low = String(tier?.tierKey || tier?.label || '').toLowerCase();
      const feeMxn = formatMxn(Number(tier?.precioMensual) || 0);
      if (low.includes('platinum') || low.includes('platino') || low.includes('leyenda') || low.includes('legend')) {
        return {
          label: tx('Platino', 'Platinum'),
          summary: tx(
            'Acceso total 1 a 5 Estrellas, Ventas Flash -20%, 3 Perdones <72h y 0% cuota app.',
            'Full 1–5 Star access, Flash Sales -20%, 3 Grace Waivers <72h & 0% app fee.'
          ),
          financialRule: tx(
            `Cuota fija ${feeMxn} MXN/mes • Suscripciones pagadas activas: ${Number(tier?.paidCount ?? tier?.count ?? 0)}.`,
            `Fixed fee ${feeMxn} MXN/mo • Active paid subscriptions: ${Number(tier?.paidCount ?? tier?.count ?? 0)}.`
          ),
        };
      }
      if (low.includes('gold') || low === 'oro' || low.includes('oro')) {
        return {
          label: tx('Oro', 'Gold'),
          summary: tx(
            'Acceso 1 a 5 Estrellas, Ventas Flash -15%, 2 Perdones <72h y paridad VIP 1:1.',
            '1–5 Star access, Flash Sales -15%, 2 Grace Waivers <72h & 1:1 VIP parity.'
          ),
          financialRule: tx(
            `Cuota fija ${feeMxn} MXN/mes • Suscripciones pagadas activas: ${Number(tier?.paidCount ?? tier?.count ?? 0)}.`,
            `Fixed fee ${feeMxn} MXN/mo • Active paid subscriptions: ${Number(tier?.paidCount ?? tier?.count ?? 0)}.`
          ),
        };
      }
      if (low.includes('silver') || low.includes('plata')) {
        return {
          label: tx('Plata', 'Silver'),
          summary: tx(
            'Acceso 1 a 4 Estrellas, Ventas Flash -10%, 1 Perdón <72h y recargas sin comisión.',
            '1–4 Star access, Flash Sales -10%, 1 Grace Waiver <72h & zero-fee top-ups.'
          ),
          financialRule: tx(
            `Cuota fija ${feeMxn} MXN/mes • Suscripciones pagadas activas: ${Number(tier?.paidCount ?? tier?.count ?? 0)}.`,
            `Fixed fee ${feeMxn} MXN/mo • Active paid subscriptions: ${Number(tier?.paidCount ?? tier?.count ?? 0)}.`
          ),
        };
      }
      if (low.includes('bronze') || low.includes('bronce')) {
        return {
          label: tx('Bronce', 'Bronze'),
          summary: tx(
            'Acceso 1 a 3 Estrellas, Ventas Flash -5% y exención del +20% en recargas Top-Up.',
            '1–3 Star access, Flash Sales -5% & +20% surcharge waived on Top-Ups.'
          ),
          financialRule: tx(
            `Cuota fija ${feeMxn} MXN/mes • Suscripciones pagadas activas: ${Number(tier?.paidCount ?? tier?.count ?? 0)}.`,
            `Fixed fee ${feeMxn} MXN/mo • Active paid subscriptions: ${Number(tier?.paidCount ?? tier?.count ?? 0)}.`
          ),
        };
      }
      return {
        label: tx('Acceso Base', 'Base Access'),
        summary: tx(
          'Modo escaparate gratuito ($0 MXN/mes). Permite reservar con recargas Top-Up (+20% cargo app).',
          'Free showcase tier ($0 MXN/mo). Book experiences via Top-Up wallet (+20% app fee).'
        ),
        financialRule: tx(
          'Suscripción $0.00 MXN/mes • Opera exclusivamente con Billetera Top-Up Rollover (+20% comisión app).',
          '$0.00 MXN/mo subscription • Operates exclusively via Rollover Top-Up Wallet (+20% app fee).'
        ),
      };
    };

    return (
      <View style={[styles.container, { paddingBottom: 44 }]}>
        <Text style={styles.sectionTag}>{tx('INTELIGENCIA DE NEGOCIO B2C', 'B2C BUSINESS INTELLIGENCE')}</Text>
        <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>{tx('Distribución de Membresías VIP', 'VIP Membership Distribution')}</Text>
        <Text style={[styles.sectionSub, { marginBottom: 10 }]}>
          {tx(
            'Toca cualquier rango VIP o disciplina para desplegar su radiografía financiera.',
            'Tap any VIP tier or discipline to expand its financial breakdown.'
          )}
        </Text>

        {(distribucionNiveles || []).map((tier: any) => {
          const tKey = String(tier.tierKey || tier.label);
          const isOpen = expandedTierKey === tKey;
          const meta = resolveLocalizedTierMeta(tier);
          const rawTierColor = String(tier.color || '').trim().toUpperCase();
          const tierTextColor =
            resolvedTheme !== 'light'
              ? tier.color || COLORS.cyan
              : rawTierColor === '#00F0FF' || rawTierColor === '#00E5FF'
              ? '#0369A1'
              : rawTierColor === '#FFCC00' || rawTierColor === '#F59E0B'
              ? '#B45309'
              : rawTierColor === '#A8B2C1' || rawTierColor === '#94A3B8' || rawTierColor === '#CBD5E1'
              ? '#334155'
              : rawTierColor === '#CD7F32'
              ? '#9A3412'
              : rawTierColor === '#9D4EDD' || rawTierColor === '#E0AAFF'
              ? '#6D28D9'
              : '#047857';
          const countVal = Number(tier.count) || 0;
          const tierAthletes = Array.isArray(tier.atletas) ? tier.atletas : [];
          const countBadgeText = `${countVal} ${countVal === 1 ? tx('atleta', 'athlete') : tx('atletas', 'athletes')} (${Number(tier.pct) || 0}%)`;

          return (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={tKey}
              activeOpacity={0.86}
              onPress={() => {
                triggerHaptic('Selection');
                setExpandedTierKey(isOpen ? null : tKey);
              }}
              style={[
                styles.tierMetricCard,
                mStyles.interactiveCard3D,
                isOpen && { borderColor: tierTextColor, borderTopColor: isLightMode ? '#FFFFFF' : tierTextColor },
              ]}
            >
              <View style={styles.rowBetween}>
                <Text style={[styles.tierLabel, { color: tierTextColor, flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                  {meta.label}
                </Text>
                <View style={[mStyles.rightBadgeRow, { flexShrink: 0 }]}>
                  <Text style={styles.tierCountText} numberOfLines={1}>
                    {countBadgeText}
                  </Text>
                  <Feather name={isOpen ? 'chevron-up' : 'chevron-down'} size={14} color={tierTextColor} />
                </View>
              </View>
              <Text style={styles.contactMetaText}>{meta.summary}</Text>
              <View style={[styles.rowBetween, { marginTop: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 4 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.statLabel}>
                  {tx('Ingreso Mensual Recurrente:', 'Monthly Recurring Revenue:')}{' '}
                  <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textPrimary, fontWeight: '900' }}>
                    {formatMxn(tier.ingresoRecurrenteMxn || 0)} MXN
                  </Text>
                </Text>
                <Text style={[styles.statLabel, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                  {tx('Saldo en Bóvedas:', 'Vault Balance:')} {tier.saldoAcumuladoKc || 0} KC
                </Text>
              </View>

              {isOpen && (
                <View style={mStyles.accordionWell}>
                  <Text style={[mStyles.accordionRuleText, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{meta.financialRule}</Text>
                  <View style={mStyles.accordionMetricsGrid}>
                    <View style={mStyles.accMetricCell}>
                      <Text style={mStyles.accMetricLabel}>{tx('CUOTA / MES', 'MONTHLY FEE')}</Text>
                      <Text style={mStyles.accMetricVal}>{formatMxn(tier.precioMensual || 0)}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={mStyles.accMetricCell}>
                      <Text style={mStyles.accMetricLabel}>{tx('APORTE MENSUAL', 'MONTHLY REV.')}</Text>
                      <Text style={[mStyles.accMetricVal, { color: themeColors.green }]}>
                        {formatMxn(tier.ingresoRecurrenteMxn || 0)}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={mStyles.accMetricCell}>
                      <Text style={mStyles.accMetricLabel}>{tx('KC EN BÓVEDAS', 'VAULT KC HELD')}</Text>
                      <Text style={[mStyles.accMetricVal, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                        {tier.saldoAcumuladoKc || 0} KC
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                  </View>

                  <Text style={mStyles.accSubHeader}>
                    {tx('ATLETAS ACTIVOS EN ESTE RANGO', 'ACTIVE ATHLETES IN THIS TIER')} ({tierAthletes.length})
                  </Text>
                  {tierAthletes.length === 0 ? (
                    <Text style={styles.contactMetaText}>
                      {tx('Sin atletas activos asignados a este rango actualmente.', 'No active athletes assigned to this tier right now.')}
                    </Text>
                  ) : (
                    tierAthletes.map((a: any) => (
                      <View key={String(a.id)} style={mStyles.athleteMiniRow}>
                        <Text style={mStyles.athleteMiniName} numberOfLines={1}>• {a.nombre}</Text>
                        <Text style={[mStyles.athleteMiniKc, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                          {a.saldo_total_kc || 0} KC
                        </Text>
                      </View>
                    ))
                  )}
                </View>
              )}
            </TouchableOpacity>
          );
        })}

        <Text style={[styles.sectionTitle, { marginTop: 18 }]}>
          {tx('Disciplinas Más Reservadas', 'Most Booked Disciplines')}
        </Text>

        {(topDisciplinas || []).map((d: any, idx: number) => {
          const discName = String(d.disciplina || d.cat || 'Fuerza');
          const dKey = `${discName}_${idx}`;
          const isOpen = expandedDiscKey === dKey;
          const pctVal = d.percentage ?? d.pct ?? 0;
          const confirmedBookings = Number(d.count ?? 0);
          const completedQr = Number(d.completadas ?? 0);
          const volKc = Number(d.volumenKc || 0);
          const activeSessionsCount = (experienciasActivas || []).filter((ev: any) => {
            const evCat = String(ev?.categoria || ev?.disciplina || '').trim().toLowerCase();
            return evCat === discName.trim().toLowerCase();
          }).length;

          return (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={dKey}
              activeOpacity={0.86}
              onPress={() => {
                triggerHaptic('Selection');
                setExpandedDiscKey(isOpen ? null : dKey);
              }}
              style={[styles.tierMetricCard, mStyles.interactiveCard3D, isOpen && { borderColor: isLightMode ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan }]}
            >
              <View style={styles.rowBetween}>
                <Text style={[styles.affiliateName, { flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 }]} numberOfLines={1}>{td(discName)}</Text>
                <View style={[mStyles.rightBadgeRow, { flexShrink: 0 }]}>
                  <Text style={{ color: themeColors.cyanText, fontWeight: '900', fontSize: 12 }} numberOfLines={1}>
                    {pctVal}% • {confirmedBookings} {confirmedBookings === 1 ? tx('reserva', 'booking') : tx('reservas', 'bookings')}
                  </Text>
                  <Feather name={isOpen ? 'chevron-up' : 'chevron-down'} size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                </View>
              </View>
              <View style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
                <GpuProgressBar progress={Math.min(100, Math.max(6, pctVal))} color={COLORS.cyan} height={7} />
              </View>
              {isOpen && (
                <View style={mStyles.accordionWell}>
                  <View style={mStyles.accordionMetricsGrid}>
                    <View style={mStyles.accMetricCell}><Text style={mStyles.accMetricLabel}>{tx('SESIONES ACTIVAS', 'ACTIVE SESSIONS')}</Text><Text style={mStyles.accMetricVal}>{activeSessionsCount}</Text></View>
                    <View style={mStyles.accMetricCell}><Text style={mStyles.accMetricLabel}>{tx('RESERVAS CONF.', 'BOOKINGS')}</Text><Text style={[mStyles.accMetricVal, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{confirmedBookings}</Text></View>
                    <View style={mStyles.accMetricCell}><Text style={mStyles.accMetricLabel}>{tx('CHECK-INS QR', 'QR CHECK-INS')}</Text><Text style={[mStyles.accMetricVal, { color: themeColors.green }]}>{completedQr}</Text></View>
                    <View style={mStyles.accMetricCell}><Text style={mStyles.accMetricLabel}>{tx('VOLUMEN KC', 'KC VOLUME')}</Text><Text style={[mStyles.accMetricVal, { color: themeColors.purpleText }]}>{volKc} KC</Text></View>
                  </View>
                </View>
              )}
            </TouchableOpacity>
          );
        })}
      </View>
    );
  }
);

const metricStyles = StyleSheet.create({
  interactiveCard3D: { borderWidth: 1.2, borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderBottomWidth: 4.5, elevation: 9 },
  rightBadgeRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, flexShrink: 0 },
  accordionWell: { backgroundColor: '#05070C', borderRadius: 11, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)', padding: 11, marginTop: 10 },
  accordionRuleText: { fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '700', lineHeight: 16, marginBottom: 9 },
  accordionMetricsGrid: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: 6, paddingVertical: 6, borderTopWidth: 1, borderTopColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)' },
  accMetricCell: { flex: 1, alignItems: 'center' },
  accMetricLabel: { color: COLORS.textMuted, fontSize: 8, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.4, textAlign: 'center' },
  accMetricVal: { color: COLORS.textPrimary, fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '900', marginTop: 2, fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] },
  accSubHeader: { color: COLORS.cyan, fontSize: 9, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.7, marginTop: 8, marginBottom: 5 },
  athleteMiniRow: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', paddingVertical: 4 },
  athleteMiniName: { color: COLORS.textPrimary, fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '700', flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 },
  athleteMiniKc: { fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '900', fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] },
});

    FILE: src/components/admin/AdminWaitlistPreRegistrosSection.tsx

    // === src/components/admin/AdminWaitlistPreRegistrosSection.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Linking, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase';
import { escapeLikePattern, UUID_ANY_RE } from '../../services/admin/adminAffiliateCrudHelper';
import { sanitizePendingB2BChanges } from '../../services/admin/adminSecurityAndSpeiHelpers';
import type { IWaitlistPreRegistro } from '../../services/admin/adminWaitlistService';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { emitKineticRadarUpdated } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { baseWaitlistStyles } from './AdminWaitlistPreRegistrosSection.styles';

interface AdminWaitlistPreRegistrosProps {
  preRegistrosWeb?: IWaitlistPreRegistro[];
  isSubmitting?: boolean;
  onGestionarPreRegistro?: (registro: IWaitlistPreRegistro, accion: 'aceptar' | 'descartar') => void;
  onCopyText?: (value: string, label: string) => void;
}

export const AdminWaitlistPreRegistrosSection = memo(
  ({ preRegistrosWeb = [], isSubmitting = false, onGestionarPreRegistro, onCopyText }: AdminWaitlistPreRegistrosProps) => {
    const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx, fmtDate } = useKineticPreferences();
    const s = useMemo(
      () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseWaitlistStyles, resolvedTheme, ['acceptBtn3D', 'acceptBtnText']),
      [resolvedTheme]
    );
    const [tab, setTab] = useState<'todos' | 'pendientes' | 'aceptados'>('todos');
    const [clearedPendingIds, setClearedPendingIds] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
    const [busyLeadId, setBusyLeadId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [feedbackMsg, setFeedbackMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const mountedRef = useRef(true);
    const busyLeadRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    const feedbackTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
      mountedRef.current = true;
      return () => {
        mountedRef.current = false;
        if (feedbackTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(feedbackTimerRef.current);
      };
    }, []);

    const showFeedback = useCallback((msg: string) => {
      if (!mountedRef.current) return;
      setFeedbackMsg(msg);
      if (feedbackTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(feedbackTimerRef.current);
      feedbackTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => { if (mountedRef.current) setFeedbackMsg(null); }, 3600);
    }, []);

    const { pendientes, aceptados } = useMemo(() => {
      const pend: IWaitlistPreRegistro[] = [];
      const acep: IWaitlistPreRegistro[] = [];
      (preRegistrosWeb || []).forEach((item) => {
        const st = String(item?.estado || item?.estadoRevision || 'pendiente').toLowerCase().trim();
        if (st === 'aceptado' || st === 'aprobado') acep.push(item);
        else if (st !== 'descartado' && st !== 'rechazado') pend.push(item);
      });
      return { pendientes: pend, aceptados: acep };
    }, [preRegistrosWeb]);

    const visibleList = useMemo(
      () => (tab === 'todos' ? preRegistrosWeb : tab === 'pendientes' ? pendientes : aceptados),
      [tab, preRegistrosWeb, pendientes, aceptados]
    );

    const handleOpenEmail = (email: string) => {
      triggerHaptic('Light');
      const clean = String(email || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!clean || !clean.includes('@')) return;
      Linking.openURL(`mailto:${clean}`).catch(() => onCopyText?.(clean, tx('Correo', 'Email')));
    };

    const handleOpenWhatsapp = (phone: string) => {
      triggerHaptic('Light');
      const digits = String(phone || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
      if (digits.length >= 10) {
        const waDigits = digits.length === 10 ? `52${digits}` : digits;
        Linking.openURL(`https://wa.me/${waDigits}`).catch(() => onCopyText?.(phone, tx('Teléfono', 'Phone')));
      } else {
        onCopyText?.(phone, tx('Teléfono', 'Phone'));
      }
    };

    const resolveLeadKey = (leadItem: any) =>
      String(leadItem?.id || leadItem?.email || leadItem?.nombre || '').trim();

    const handleApproveB2BChanges = useCallback(
      async (leadItem: any) => {
        const leadKey = resolveLeadKey(leadItem);
        const rawDbId = String(leadItem?.id || '').trim();
        if (!leadKey || busyLeadRef.current) return;
        busyLeadRef.current = leadKey;
        triggerHaptic('Heavy');
        setBusyLeadId(leadKey);
        try {
          const safeChanges = sanitizePendingB2BChanges(leadItem?.cambios_pendientes_b2b);
          const safeKeys = Object.keys(safeChanges);

          if (safeKeys.length > 0) {
            let updatedAny = false;
            const targetAfId = String(leadItem?.afiliado_id || rawDbId).trim();
            if (UUID_ANY_RE.test(targetAfId)) {
              const { data: byIdRows, error: byIdErr } = await supabase
                .from('afiliados').update(safeChanges).eq('id', targetAfId).select('id');
              if (!byIdErr && Array.isArray(byIdRows) && byIdRows.length > 0) updatedAny = true;
            }
            const cleanLeadName = String(leadItem?.nombre || '').trim();
            if (!updatedAny && cleanLeadName.length >= 3 && !/[%_]/.test(cleanLeadName) && cleanLeadName.toLowerCase() !== 'kinetic oficial') {
              const { error: byNameErr } = await supabase
                .from('afiliados').update(safeChanges).ilike('nombre', escapeLikePattern(cleanLeadName));
              if (byNameErr) throw byNameErr;
            }
          }

          if (UUID_ANY_RE.test(rawDbId)) {
            const { error: wlErr } = await supabase
              .from('fundadores_waitlist').update({ cambios_pendientes_b2b: null }).eq('id', rawDbId);
            if (wlErr) throw wlErr;
          }
          if (mountedRef.current) setClearedPendingIds((prev) => ({ ...prev, [leadKey]: true }));

          if (safeKeys.length > 0) {
            emitKineticRadarUpdated();
            showFeedback(tx(`Escaparate B2B actualizado (${safeKeys.join(', ')}).`, `B2B storefront updated (${safeKeys.join(', ')}).`));
          } else {
            showFeedback(tx('Solicitud depurada: sin campos editables permitidos.', 'Request cleared: no allowed editable storefront fields.'));
          }
        } catch {
          showFeedback(tx('No se pudo autorizar el cambio B2B. Verifica permisos RLS.', 'Could not authorize B2B change. Verify RLS permissions.'));
        } finally {
          busyLeadRef.current = null;
          if (mountedRef.current) setBusyLeadId(null);
        }
      },
      [showFeedback, tx]
    );

    const handleRejectB2BChanges = useCallback(
      async (leadItem: any) => {
        const leadKey = resolveLeadKey(leadItem);
        const rawDbId = String(leadItem?.id || '').trim();
        if (!leadKey || busyLeadRef.current) return;
        busyLeadRef.current = leadKey;
        triggerHaptic('Medium');
        setBusyLeadId(leadKey);
        try {
          if (UUID_ANY_RE.test(rawDbId)) {
            const { error: wlErr } = await supabase
              .from('fundadores_waitlist').update({ cambios_pendientes_b2b: null }).eq('id', rawDbId);
            if (wlErr) throw wlErr;
          }
          if (mountedRef.current) setClearedPendingIds((prev) => ({ ...prev, [leadKey]: true }));
          showFeedback(tx('Cambios pendientes B2B descartados.', 'Pending B2B changes dismissed.'));
        } catch {
          showFeedback(tx('Error al descartar solicitud B2B.', 'Error dismissing B2B request.'));
        } finally {
          busyLeadRef.current = null;
          if (mountedRef.current) setBusyLeadId(null);
        }
      },
      [showFeedback, tx]
    );

    const formatLeadDate = (iso?: string | null) => {
      if (!iso) return tx('Reciente', 'Recent');
      try {
        return fmtDate(iso, { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' }) || String(iso).slice(0, 10);
      } catch {
        return String(iso).slice(0, 10);
      }
    };

    return (
      <View style={s.sectionWrap}>
        {pendientes.length > 0 && (
          <View
            style={[
              s.alertBanner3D,
              isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1' },
            ]}
          >
            <View style={s.alertIconCircle}>
              <Feather name="bell" size={15} color={isLightMode ? '#0369A1' : COLORS.cyan} />
            </View>
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <Text style={[s.alertTitle, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                {tx(`NUEVOS PRE-REGISTROS WEB (${pendientes.length})`, `NEW WEB PRE-REGISTRATIONS (${pendientes.length})`)}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[s.alertSub, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>
                {tx('Solicitudes recibidas desde kineticapp.com.mx listas para revisión y aceptación.', 'Leads received from kineticapp.com.mx ready for review and approval.')}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        )}

        {Boolean(feedbackMsg) && (
          <View style={s.feedbackBanner3D}>
            <Feather name="shield" size={13} color={themeColors.green} />
            <Text style={[s.feedbackBannerTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{feedbackMsg}</Text>
          </View>
        )}

        <View style={s.headerRow}>
          <View style={{ width: '100%' }}>
            <Text style={[s.subTag, { color: themeColors.purpleText }]}>
              {tx('LISTA DE ACCESO PRIORITARIO WEB', 'PRIORITY WEB WAITLIST')}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[s.subTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={2}>
              {tx('Pre-Registros kineticapp.com.mx', 'Web Pre-Registrations')} ({preRegistrosWeb.length})
            </Text>
          </View>
          <View style={s.filterPillsRow}>
            {[
              { k: 'todos', lbl: tx('Todos', 'All'), n: preRegistrosWeb.length },
              { k: 'pendientes', lbl: tx('Pendientes', 'Pending'), n: pendientes.length },
              { k: 'aceptados', lbl: tx('Aceptados', 'Accepted'), n: aceptados.length },
            ].map((p) => {
              const active = tab === p.k;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={p.k}
                  activeOpacity={0.85}
                  style={[s.filterPill, active && s.filterPillActive]}
                  onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setTab(p.k as any); }}
                >
                  <Text style={[s.filterPillTxt, active && { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{p.lbl} ({p.n})</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
          </View>
        </View>

        {visibleList.length === 0 ? (
          <View style={s.emptyBox}>
            <Feather name="inbox" size={18} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
            <Text style={[s.emptyTxt, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
              {tx('No hay pre-registros en esta categoría.', 'No pre-registrations in this category.')}
            </Text>
          </View>
        ) : (
          visibleList.map((lead, idx) => {
            const leadKey = resolveLeadKey(lead) || `lead_${idx}`;
            const isB2B = lead.esSocioB2B;
            const badgeColor = isB2B ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.cyanText;
            const hasRawPending = !clearedPendingIds[leadKey] && Boolean(lead.cambios_pendientes_b2b);
            const previewEntries = hasRawPending ? Object.entries(sanitizePendingB2BChanges(lead.cambios_pendientes_b2b)) : [];
            const isRowBusy = isSubmitting || busyLeadId === leadKey;

            return (
              <View key={leadKey} style={[s.leadCard3D, isB2B && { borderColor: 'rgba(157, 78, 221, 0.45)' }]}>
                <View style={s.topBadgeRow}>
                  <View style={[s.roleBadge, { borderColor: badgeColor, backgroundColor: isB2B ? 'rgba(157, 78, 221, 0.14)' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.12)' }]}>
                    <Text style={[s.roleBadgeTxt, { color: badgeColor }]}>{isB2B ? tx('SOCIO B2B', 'B2B PARTNER') : tx('ATLETA B2C', 'B2C ATHLETE')}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <Text style={[s.dateMetaTxt, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{lead.origen} • {formatLeadDate(lead.created_at)}</Text>
                </View>

                <Text style={[s.leadName, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{lead.nombre}</Text>
                <Text style={[s.profileDetailTxt, { color: badgeColor }]}>{lead.tipo_perfil}</Text>

                <View style={s.metaGrid}>
                  {Boolean(lead.email) && (
                    <TouchableOpacity activeOpacity={0.8} style={s.metaChip} onPress={() => handleOpenEmail(lead.email)}>
                      <Feather name="mail" size={11} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                      <Text numberOfLines={1} style={[s.metaChipTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{lead.email}</Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  )}
                  {Boolean(lead.telefono) && (
                    <TouchableOpacity activeOpacity={0.8} style={s.metaChip} onPress={() => handleOpenWhatsapp(lead.telefono)}>
                      <Feather name="phone" size={11} color={themeColors.green} />
                      <Text style={[s.metaChipTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{lead.telefono}</Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  )}
                  {Boolean(lead.ciudad_zona) && (
                    <View style={s.metaChip}>
                      <Feather name="map-pin" size={11} color={themeColors.purpleText} />
                      <Text style={[s.metaChipTxt, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{lead.ciudad_zona}</Text>
                    </View>
                  )}
                  {Boolean(lead.disciplina_interes) && (
                    <View style={s.metaChip}>
                      <Feather name="award" size={11} color={COLORS.yellow} />
                      <Text style={[s.metaChipTxt, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{lead.disciplina_interes}</Text>
                    </View>
                  )}
                </View>

                {hasRawPending && (
                  <View style={s.b2bPendingBanner}>
                    <View style={s.b2bPendingBadge}>
                      <Text style={s.b2bPendingBadgeTxt}>{tx('CAMBIOS PENDIENTES B2B', 'PENDING B2B CHANGES')}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <Text style={[s.b2bPendingDetails, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                      {previewEntries.length > 0
                        ? previewEntries.map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`).join(' • ')
                        : tx('Sin campos de escaparate válidos (se depurará al autorizar/rechazar).', 'No valid storefront fields (will clear on action).')}
                    </Text>
                    <View style={s.b2bActionsRow}>
                      <TouchableOpacity disabled={isRowBusy} activeOpacity={0.85} style={[s.acceptBtn3D, isRowBusy && { opacity: 0.6 }]} onPress={() => handleApproveB2BChanges(lead)}>
                        <Feather name="check" size={13} color="#050505" />
                        <Text style={s.acceptBtnText}>{tx('AUTORIZAR', 'AUTHORIZE')}</Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                      <TouchableOpacity disabled={isRowBusy} activeOpacity={0.85} style={[s.dismissBtn3D, isRowBusy && { opacity: 0.6 }]} onPress={() => handleRejectB2BChanges(lead)}>
                        <Text style={s.dismissBtnText}>{tx('RECHAZAR', 'REJECT')}</Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    </View>
                  </View>
                )}

                {lead.estadoRevision === 'pendiente' ? (
                  <View style={s.actionsRow}>
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      disabled={isRowBusy}
                      activeOpacity={0.86}
                      style={[s.acceptBtn3D, isRowBusy && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
                      onPress={() => onGestionarPreRegistro?.(lead, 'aceptar')}
                      {...({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } as any)}
                    >
                      <Feather name="check-circle" size={13} color="#050505" />
                      <Text style={s.acceptBtnText}>{isB2B ? tx('ACEPTAR SOCIO', 'ACCEPT PARTNER') : tx('ACEPTAR REGISTRO', 'ACCEPT SIGNUP')}</Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                    <TouchableOpacity disabled={isRowBusy} activeOpacity={0.86} style={[s.dismissBtn3D, isRowBusy && { opacity: 0.6 }]} onPress={() => onGestionarPreRegistro?.(lead, 'descartar')}>
                      <Text style={s.dismissBtnText}>{tx('DESCARTAR', 'DISMISS')}</Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  </View>
                ) : (
                  <View style={s.acceptedPill}>
                    <Feather name="check" size={12} color={themeColors.green} />
                    <Text style={[s.acceptedPillTxt, { color: themeColors.green }]}>{tx('PRE-REGISTRO REVISADO Y ACEPTADO', 'PRE-REGISTRATION ACCEPTED')}</Text>
                  </View>
                )}
              </View>
            );
          })
        )}
      </View>
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/admin/AffiliateQuickActivateModal.tsx

    // === src/components/admin/AffiliateQuickActivateModal.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Keyboard, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform, Pressable, ScrollView, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { extractInstagramFromAffiliate, normalizeInstagramHandle } from '../../services/adminService';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { renderInWebPortal } from '../../utils/kineticWebPortal';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from '../shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { baseSubStyles } from './AdminAffiliateSubModals';

export const AffiliateQuickActivateModal = memo(({
  visible, affiliate, isAdmin = true, isSubmitting = false, onConfirmActivate, onClose, inlineOverlay = false,
}: {
  visible: boolean;
  affiliate: any | null;
  isAdmin?: boolean;
  isSubmitting?: boolean;
  onConfirmActivate: (whatsapp: string, instagram: string) => Promise<void>;
  onClose: () => void;
  inlineOverlay?: boolean;
}) => {
  const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const s = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseSubStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
  const [waInput, setWaInput] = useState('');
  const [igInput, setIgInput] = useState('');
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const lastAffiliateRef = useRef<any>(null);
  if (affiliate) lastAffiliateRef.current = affiliate;
  const displayAffiliate = affiliate || lastAffiliateRef.current;

  useEffect(() => {
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    if (visible && affiliate) {
      setWaInput(String(affiliate.whatsapp || '').replace(/\D/g, ''));
      setIgInput(extractInstagramFromAffiliate(affiliate));
      setErrorMsg(null);
    }
  }, [visible, affiliate]);

  const handleClose = () => {
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    onClose();
  };

  const handleActivatePress = async () => {
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    const digits = waInput.replace(/\D/g, '');
    const cleanIg = igInput.trim() ? normalizeInstagramHandle(igInput) : '';
    const igCore = cleanIg.replace(/^@+/, '').trim();
    const invalidPartialWa = digits.length > 0 && digits.length < 10;
    if (isAdmin ? invalidPartialWa : (digits.length < 10 || igCore.length < 2)) {
      triggerHaptic('Error');
      setErrorMsg(isAdmin
        ? tx('Si capturas WhatsApp debe tener 10 dígitos, o déjalo vacío para activar directo.', 'If entering WhatsApp it must be 10 digits, or leave empty to activate directly.')
        : tx('Captura un WhatsApp válido de 10 dígitos y el usuario oficial de Instagram (@cluboficial) para activar en el Radar GPS.', 'Enter a valid 10-digit WhatsApp number and official Instagram handle (@officialclub) to activate on the GPS Radar.'));
      return;
    }
    setErrorMsg(null);
    await onConfirmActivate(digits, cleanIg);
  };

  const modalBody = (
    <KeyboardAvoidingView behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined} style={{ width: '100%', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
      <View style={[globalStyles.bottomSheet, KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, s.sheetSurface]}>
        <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={handleClose} />
        <ScrollView keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled" bounces={false} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
          <Text style={s.sheetTag}>{tx('PROTOCOLO DE ACTIVACIÓN EN RADAR GPS', 'GPS RADAR ACTIVATION PROTOCOL')}</Text>
          <Text style={s.sheetTitle}>{displayAffiliate?.nombre || tx('Operador B2B', 'B2B Operator')}</Text>

          <View style={s.reqNotice3D}>
            <Feather name="shield" size={16} color={isLightMode ? '#B45309' : '#FFAB00'} style={{ marginTop: 1 }} />
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <Text style={[s.reqNoticeTitle, { color: isLightMode ? '#B45309' : '#FFAB00' }]}>
                {isAdmin ? tx('ACTIVACIÓN DIRECTA DE ADMINISTRADOR', 'ADMIN 1-TAP ACTIVATION') : tx('REQUISITO OBLIGATORIO DE RADAR', 'MANDATORY RADAR REQUIREMENT')}
              </Text>
              <Text style={s.reqNoticeText}>
                {isAdmin
                  ? tx('Como Administrador puedes activar en 1 toque sin WhatsApp obligatorio, o registrarlo opcionalmente ahora.', 'As Admin you can activate in 1 tap without mandatory WhatsApp, or optionally save it now.')
                  : tx('Para publicar un prospecto oculto como ACTIVO EN RADAR GPS, registra su WhatsApp (10 dígitos) e Instagram oficial (@usuario).', 'To publish a hidden prospect as ACTIVE ON GPS RADAR, enter its 10-digit WhatsApp and official Instagram (@handle).')}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          <Text style={s.subLabel}>{isAdmin ? tx('WHATSAPP (10 DÍGITOS — OPCIONAL)', 'WHATSAPP (10 DIGITS — OPTIONAL)') : tx('WHATSAPP (10 DÍGITOS) *', 'WHATSAPP (10 DIGITS) *')}</Text>
          <TextInput style={[s.modalInput3D, { fontSize: 16, elevation: 0, color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} placeholder={tx('Opcional — 10 dígitos', 'Optional — 10 digits')} placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted} keyboardType="phone-pad" maxLength={15} value={waInput} onChangeText={setWaInput} />
          <Text style={s.subLabel}>{isAdmin ? tx('INSTAGRAM OFICIAL (OPCIONAL)', 'OFFICIAL INSTAGRAM (OPTIONAL)') : tx('INSTAGRAM OFICIAL *', 'OFFICIAL INSTAGRAM *')}</Text>
          <TextInput style={[s.modalInput3D, { fontSize: 16, elevation: 0, color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} placeholder={tx('@cluboficial', '@officialclub')} placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted} autoCapitalize="none" autoCorrect={false} value={igInput} onChangeText={setIgInput} />

          {Boolean(errorMsg) && (
            <View style={s.errorBox3D}>
              <Feather name="alert-triangle" size={14} color="#FF3B30" />
              <Text style={s.errorBoxText}>{errorMsg}</Text>
            </View>
          )}

          <TouchableOpacity style={[s.activateGreenCta3D, isSubmitting && { opacity: 0.65 }]} disabled={isSubmitting} onPress={handleActivatePress}>
            <Feather name="zap" size={16} color="#050505" />
            <Text style={s.primaryCtaText}>
              {isSubmitting ? tx('ACTIVANDO EN RADAR...', 'ACTIVATING ON RADAR...') : tx('GUARDAR Y ACTIVAR EN RADAR GPS', 'SAVE & ACTIVATE ON GPS RADAR')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </ScrollView>
      </View>
    </KeyboardAvoidingView>
  );

  if (inlineOverlay) {
    if (!visible) return null;
    const inlineWrap = (
      <View
        style={{
          position: (Platform.OS === 'web' ? 'fixed' : 'absolute') as any,
          top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0,
          ...(Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ width: '100vw', height: '100dvh' } as any) : {}),
          zIndex: 9940,
          backgroundColor: isLightMode ? 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.5)' : 'rgba(5, 7, 12, 0.92)',
          justifyContent: 'flex-end',
          elevation: 0,
        }}
      >
        <Pressable style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 }} onPress={handleClose} />
        {modalBody}
      </View>
    );
    return Platform.OS === 'web' ? renderInWebPortal(inlineWrap) : inlineWrap;
  }

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={visible} onBackdropPress={handleClose} onBackButtonPress={handleClose}
      style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : COLORS.background}
      backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.5 : 0.92} backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
      animationInTiming={200} animationOutTiming={180}
      useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'} useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      avoidKeyboard
    >
      {modalBody}
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
});

    borderTopWidth: 1.8,
    borderTopColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.28)',
    borderBottomWidth: 4.0,

  reqBtn3DActiveLight: { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1', shadowColor: '#0284C7' },

    width: 26,

    borderTopWidth: 1.8,

    elevation: 0,

  pickerOptionText: { color: COLORS.textLight, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: '700', flex: 1, paddingRight: 10 },
  checkboxBox: {
    width: 22,
