    FILE: src/components/AdminEventEditorModal.tsx

    // === src/components/AdminEventEditorModal.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  ActivityIndicator, Keyboard, KeyboardAvoidingView,
  Platform, ScrollView, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View,
} from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { normalizeAgePolicy, normalizeEnvironmentLabel } from '../constants/kineticEventCatalogs';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS } from '../styles/globalStyles';
import {
  buildCompressedFullGallery, loadEventAttrsDualCache, mergeAdminEventWithCachedAttrs,
  pickCompressedEventImages, resolveSplitEscrowConfig, saveEventAttrsDualCache, translateEventCatalogTerm,
} from '../utils/eventEditorHelpers';
import { openExternalLinkSafe } from '../utils/externalLinks';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { extractGoogleMapsUrlAndCoords } from '../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { styles as baseStyles } from './AdminEventEditorModal.styles';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from './shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { KineticLoader } from './SharedUI';
import {
  AdminEventPickerModal, AdminPickerType, buildAdminPickerConfig,
  extractCoordsFromText, parseRequirementsList,
} from './eventos/AdminEventPickerModal';
import {
  IStudioLayoutConfig, KineticStudioLayoutModal, MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS,
  formatStudioCapacityLabel, formatStudioLayoutButtonLabel, getDisciplineEventTitlePlaceholder,
  resolveDefaultStudioLayout, saveStudioLayoutLocal,
} from './eventos/KineticStudioLayoutModal';

const TEXT_SEL: any = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'text', WebkitUserSelect: 'text' } : null;

export const AdminEventEditorModal = ({
  isVisible, onClose, nuevoEvento, setNuevoEvento, onGuardarEvento,
  onDeleteEvento, isPublishing, editModeId, isCreateMode, triggerHaptic,
}: any) => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const [keyboardHeight, setKeyboardHeight] = useState(0);
  const [activePicker, setActivePicker] = useState<AdminPickerType>(null);
  const [isResolvingMapUrl, setIsResolvingMapUrl] = useState(false);
  const [detectedZone, setDetectedZone] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [selectedRequisitosDraft, setSelectedRequisitosDraft] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [isStudioModalOpen, setIsStudioModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isPickingImages, setIsPickingImages] = useState(false);

  const { resolvedTheme, fmtDate, language, tx, tDisc, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
  const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
  const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);

  const frozenEditModeRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const frozenCreateModeRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  if (isVisible) {
    frozenEditModeRef.current = editModeId || null;
    frozenCreateModeRef.current = typeof isCreateMode === 'boolean' ? isCreateMode : !editModeId;
  }
  const effectiveEditId = isVisible ? editModeId : frozenEditModeRef.current;
  const resolvedCreateMode = isVisible ? (typeof isCreateMode === 'boolean' ? isCreateMode : !effectiveEditId) : frozenCreateModeRef.current;
  const hydratedSessionRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const publishLockRef = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const showEv = Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'keyboardWillShow' : 'keyboardDidShow';
    const hideEv = Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'keyboardWillHide' : 'keyboardDidHide';
    const subShow = Keyboard.addListener(showEv, (e) => setKeyboardHeight(e.endCoordinates?.height || 260));
    const subHide = Keyboard.addListener(hideEv, () => setKeyboardHeight(0));
    return () => { subShow.remove(); subHide.remove(); };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isVisible) {
      hydratedSessionRef.current = null; setActivePicker(null); setIsStudioModalOpen(false); setKeyboardHeight(0); setDetectedZone(null);
      return;
    }
    const sig = `${effectiveEditId || 'create'}`;
    if (hydratedSessionRef.current === sig) return;
    hydratedSessionRef.current = sig;

    if (nuevoEvento?.ubicacion) {
      const parsed = extractGoogleMapsUrlAndCoords(String(nuevoEvento.ubicacion));
      setDetectedZone(parsed.detectedZone);
      if (parsed.lat !== null && parsed.lng !== null && (!nuevoEvento.latitud || !nuevoEvento.longitud)) {
        setNuevoEvento((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, latitud: parsed.lat, longitud: parsed.lng }));
      }
    }
    if (effectiveEditId) {
      loadEventAttrsDualCache(String(effectiveEditId), nuevoEvento?.nombre_servicio).then((cached) => {
        if (cached) setNuevoEvento((prev: any) => mergeAdminEventWithCachedAttrs(prev, cached));
      }).catch(() => {});
    }
  }, [isVisible, effectiveEditId, nuevoEvento?.nombre_servicio, nuevoEvento?.ubicacion, nuevoEvento?.latitud, nuevoEvento?.longitud, setNuevoEvento]);

  const dateOptions = useMemo(() =>
    Array.from({ length: 30 }).map((_, i) => {
      const d = new Date();
      d.setDate(d.getDate() + i);
      const val = `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
      return { val, label: fmtDate(d, { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' }).replace('.', '').toUpperCase() };
    }),
  [fmtDate]);

  const galleryList = useMemo<string[]>(() => {
    const raw = Array.isArray(nuevoEvento?.galeria) ? nuevoEvento.galeria : Array.isArray(nuevoEvento?.atributos?.galeria) ? nuevoEvento.atributos.galeria : [];
    const cover = String(nuevoEvento?.imagen_url || '').trim();
    return Array.from(new Set([cover, ...raw].filter(Boolean))).slice(0, 10);
  }, [nuevoEvento?.galeria, nuevoEvento?.atributos?.galeria, nuevoEvento?.imagen_url]);

  const handlePickGallery = async () => {
    if (isPickingImages) return;
    setIsPickingImages(true);
    try {
      const picked = await pickCompressedEventImages(Math.max(1, 10 - galleryList.length));
      if (picked.length > 0) {
        const merged = Array.from(new Set([...galleryList, ...picked].filter(Boolean))).slice(0, 10);
        setNuevoEvento((prev: any) => ({
          ...prev, imagen_url: merged[0] || prev?.imagen_url || '', galeria: merged,
          atributos: { ...(prev?.atributos || {}), galeria: merged },
        }));
        triggerHaptic?.('Success');
      }
    } catch {} finally { setIsPickingImages(false); }
  };

  const handleRemoveGalleryAt = (uri: string) => {
    triggerHaptic?.('Light');
    const next = galleryList.filter((u) => u !== uri);
    setNuevoEvento((prev: any) => ({
      ...prev, imagen_url: next[0] || '', galeria: next,
      atributos: { ...(prev?.atributos || {}), galeria: next },
    }));
  };

  const handleLocationChange = async (text: string) => {
    const parsed = extractGoogleMapsUrlAndCoords(text);
    setDetectedZone(parsed.detectedZone);
    setNuevoEvento((prev: any) => ({
      ...prev, ubicacion: parsed.cleanValue,
      ...(parsed.lat !== null && parsed.lng !== null ? { latitud: parsed.lat, longitud: parsed.lng } : {}),
    }));
    if (parsed.lat !== null && parsed.lng !== null) return;
    if (Platform.OS !== 'web' && (text.includes('maps.app.goo.gl/') || text.includes('goo.gl/maps/') || text.startsWith('https://maps.'))) {
      try {
        setIsResolvingMapUrl(true);
        const response = await fetch(text.trim(), { method: 'GET' });
        const resolved = extractCoordsFromText(response.url || '') || extractCoordsFromText(await response.text());
        if (resolved) {
          setNuevoEvento((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, latitud: resolved.lat, longitud: resolved.lng }));
          triggerHaptic?.('Success');
        }
      } catch {} finally { setIsResolvingMapUrl(false); }
    }
  };

  const openSelector = (type: AdminPickerType) => {
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    triggerHaptic?.('Light');
    if (type === 'requisitos') {
      const list = parseRequirementsList(nuevoEvento?.requisitos_traer);
      setSelectedRequisitosDraft(list.length > 0 ? list : ['Agua / Hidratación personal', 'Toalla de entrenamiento']);
    }
    setActivePicker(type);
  };

  const toggleRequirementItem = (itemLabel: string) => {
    triggerHaptic?.('Selection');
    setSelectedRequisitosDraft((prev) => {
      const exists = prev.some((r) => r.toLowerCase() === itemLabel.toLowerCase());
      return exists ? prev.filter((r) => r.toLowerCase() !== itemLabel.toLowerCase()) : [...prev, itemLabel].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'es'));
    });
  };

  const confirmMultiRequirements = () => {
    triggerHaptic?.('Success');
    const finalStr = selectedRequisitosDraft.length > 0 ? selectedRequisitosDraft.join(', ') : 'Agua / Hidratación personal';
    setNuevoEvento((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, requisitos_traer: finalStr }));
    setActivePicker(null);
  };

  const pickerConfig = useMemo(
    () => buildAdminPickerConfig(activePicker, nuevoEvento, setNuevoEvento, dateOptions, toggleRequirementItem, tx),
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    [activePicker, nuevoEvento, dateOptions, tx]
  );

  const currentPriceKc = Math.max(0, Number(nuevoEvento?.precio_kc ?? 200) || 0);
  const escrow40Kc = Math.round(currentPriceKc * 0.4);
  const puerta60Kc = Math.max(0, currentPriceKc - escrow40Kc);
  const currentDurationMin = Number(nuevoEvento?.duracion_minutos ?? nuevoEvento?.duracion_min ?? 60);
  const maxCupoNum = Math.min(
    MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS,
    Math.max(1, Number(nuevoEvento?.atributos?.studio_layout?.totalSpots || nuevoEvento?.studio_layout_json?.totalSpots || nuevoEvento?.cupo_maximo || 12))
  );

  const resolvedStudioLayout = useMemo<IStudioLayoutConfig>(
    () => resolveDefaultStudioLayout(nuevoEvento?.categoria, maxCupoNum, nuevoEvento?.atributos?.studio_layout || nuevoEvento?.studio_layout_json, nuevoEvento?.nombre_servicio),
    [nuevoEvento?.categoria, maxCupoNum, nuevoEvento?.atributos?.studio_layout, nuevoEvento?.studio_layout_json, nuevoEvento?.nombre_servicio]
  );
  const inputLight = isLight ? { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', color: '#0F172A', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' } : null;

  const attrs = nuevoEvento?.atributos || {};
  const splitCfg = resolveSplitEscrowConfig({ ...attrs, ...nuevoEvento });
  const safeAgeVal = normalizeAgePolicy(nuevoEvento?.edad_minima || attrs?.edad_minima);
  const attrRows: { key: AdminPickerType; label: string; value: string }[] = [
    { key: 'intensidad', label: tx('INTENSIDAD', 'INTENSITY'), value: attrs.intensidad || 'Intensidad Media' },
    { key: 'enfoque', label: tx('ENFOQUE', 'FOCUS'), value: attrs.enfoque || 'Técnica y Precisión' },
    { key: 'impacto', label: tx('IMPACTO', 'IMPACT'), value: attrs.impacto || 'Impacto Moderado' },
    { key: 'nivel_tec', label: tx('NIVEL', 'LEVEL'), value: attrs.nivel || 'Multi-Nivel (Todos)' },
    { key: 'entorno', label: tx('ENTORNO', 'ENVIRONMENT'), value: normalizeEnvironmentLabel(attrs.entorno) },
  ];

  const handlePublishPress = async () => {
    if (publishLockRef.current || Boolean(isPublishing)) return;
    publishLockRef.current = true;
    try {
      Keyboard.dismiss();
      const coverCandidate = galleryList[0] || String(nuevoEvento?.imagen_url || '');
      const { fullGallery } = await buildCompressedFullGallery(coverCandidate, galleryList);
      const atributosObj = {
        ...attrs,
        intensidad: attrs.intensidad || 'Intensidad Media', enfoque: attrs.enfoque || 'Técnica y Precisión',
        impacto: attrs.impacto || 'Impacto Moderado', nivel: attrs.nivel || 'Multi-Nivel (Todos)',
        entorno: normalizeEnvironmentLabel(attrs.entorno), frecuencia: nuevoEvento?.frecuencia || 'Única Vez',
        duracion_minutos: currentDurationMin, requisitos_traer: nuevoEvento?.requisitos_traer || 'Agua / Hidratación personal',
        edad_minima: safeAgeVal, precio_kc: currentPriceKc, galeria: fullGallery, studio_layout: resolvedStudioLayout,
        split_escrow_enabled: splitCfg.enabled, split_max_personas: splitCfg.maxPersonas,
        permite_dividir_cuenta: splitCfg.enabled, max_division_personas: splitCfg.maxPersonas,
      };
      const nextEvento = {
        ...(nuevoEvento && typeof nuevoEvento === 'object' ? nuevoEvento : {}),
        imagen_url: fullGallery[0] || nuevoEvento?.imagen_url || '',
        galeria: fullGallery,
        edad_minima: safeAgeVal,
        split_escrow_enabled: splitCfg.enabled,
        split_max_personas: splitCfg.maxPersonas,
        permite_dividir_cuenta: splitCfg.enabled,
        max_division_personas: splitCfg.maxPersonas,
        atributos: atributosObj,
      };
      setNuevoEvento(nextEvento);
      await saveEventAttrsDualCache([effectiveEditId, nextEvento.nombre_servicio], atributosObj, resolvedStudioLayout);
      await Promise.resolve(onGuardarEvento?.(nextEvento));
    } finally {
      publishLockRef.current = false;
    }
  };

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={Boolean(isVisible)} style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      backdropColor="#04060B" backdropOpacity={0.9} backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
      hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false} animationInTiming={200} animationOutTiming={180}
      onBackdropPress={() => (isStudioModalOpen ? setIsStudioModalOpen(false) : activePicker ? setActivePicker(null) : onClose())}
      onBackButtonPress={() => (isStudioModalOpen ? setIsStudioModalOpen(false) : activePicker ? setActivePicker(null) : onClose())}
      avoidKeyboard={true} useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'} useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
    >
      <KeyboardAvoidingView behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
        <View style={[styles.editorSheet, KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#0284C7' }, { paddingBottom: insets.bottom > 0 ? insets.bottom + 14 : 24 }]}>
          <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={onClose} />
          <ScrollView style={{ flex: 1 }} bounces={false} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled" contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: keyboardHeight > 0 ? keyboardHeight + 40 : 30 }}>
            <View style={styles.headerBlock}>
              {resolvedCreateMode && (
                <View style={{ alignSelf: 'flex-start', flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5, paddingHorizontal: 9, paddingVertical: 3, borderRadius: 999, borderWidth: 1, elevation: 0, marginBottom: 6, backgroundColor: isLight ? 'rgba(2, 132, 199, 0.1)' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.14)', borderColor: isLight ? 'rgba(2, 132, 199, 0.35)' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.4)' }}>
                  <Feather name="plus-circle" size={11} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                  <Text style={{ fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.7, color: themeColors.cyanText }}>{tx('CREACIÓN', 'CREATE MODE')}</Text>
                </View>
              )}
              <Text style={[styles.modalTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{effectiveEditId ? tx('Editar Experiencia', 'Edit Experience') : tx('Publicar Experiencia', 'Publish Experience')}</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.modalSubtitle, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{effectiveEditId ? tx('Actualiza horarios, galería, coach, mapa 3D y ubicación de esta sesión.', 'Update schedule, gallery, coach, 3D floor map, and location.') : tx('Configura horarios, galería, mapa 3D, coach, aforo y ubicación GPS.', 'Configure schedule, gallery, 3D floor map, coach, capacity, and GPS location.')}</Text>
            </View>

            <Text style={[styles.inputLabel, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{tx(`GALERÍA Y PORTADA (${galleryList.length}/10)`, `COVER & GALLERY (${galleryList.length}/10)`)}</Text>
            <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={styles.galleryStrip}>
              {galleryList.length < 10 && (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[styles.galleryAddBtn3D, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1' }]}
                  activeOpacity={0.85} onPress={handlePickGallery} disabled={isPickingImages}
                >
                  <Feather name="image" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                  <Text style={[styles.galleryAddTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{isPickingImages ? tx('COMPRIMIENDO...', 'COMPRESSING...') : tx('+ SUBIR FOTOS', '+ ADD PHOTOS')}</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              )}
              {galleryList.map((uri, idx) => (
                <View key={`${uri.slice(0, 32)}_${idx}`} style={styles.galleryThumbWrap}>
                  <Image source={{ uri }} style={styles.galleryThumbImg} contentFit="cover" cachePolicy="memory-disk" transition={0} />
                  <TouchableOpacity style={styles.galleryRemoveBadge} onPress={() => handleRemoveGalleryAt(uri)}>
                    <Feather name="x" size={11} color="#FFFFFF" />
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
              ))}
            </ScrollView>

            <Text style={[styles.inputLabel, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{tx('TÍTULO DEL EVENTO *', 'EVENT TITLE *')}</Text>
            <TextInput
              style={[styles.textInput, inputLight, TEXT_SEL, { fontSize: 16 }]}
              placeholder={getDisciplineEventTitlePlaceholder(nuevoEvento?.categoria, language === 'en')}
              placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
              value={nuevoEvento?.nombre_servicio || ''}
              onChangeText={(t) => setNuevoEvento({ ...nuevoEvento, nombre_servicio: t })}
            />

            {([
              [{ k: 'categoria' as AdminPickerType, l: tx('DISCIPLINA *', 'DISCIPLINE *'), v: tDisc(nuevoEvento?.categoria || 'Fuerza'), ic: 'chevron-down' as const }, { k: 'duracion' as AdminPickerType, l: tx('DURACIÓN *', 'DURATION *'), v: `${currentDurationMin} min`, ic: 'watch' as const }],
              [{ k: 'frecuencia' as AdminPickerType, l: tx('FRECUENCIA *', 'FREQUENCY *'), v: translateEventCatalogTerm(nuevoEvento?.frecuencia || 'Única Vez', language), ic: 'repeat' as const }, { k: 'nivel' as AdminPickerType, l: tx('NIVEL VIP *', 'VIP TIER *'), v: nuevoEvento?.nivel_requerido || 'Bronze', ic: 'star' as const }],
              [{ k: 'fecha' as AdminPickerType, l: tx('FECHA PROGRAMADA *', 'SCHEDULED DATE *'), v: nuevoEvento?.fecha || dateOptions[0]?.val, ic: 'calendar' as const }, { k: 'hora' as AdminPickerType, l: tx('HORA DE INICIO *', 'START TIME *'), v: `${String(nuevoEvento?.hora_inicio || '08:00').substring(0, 5)} HRS`, ic: 'clock' as const }],
              [{ k: 'cupo' as AdminPickerType, l: tx('CUPO MÁXIMO *', 'MAX CAPACITY *'), v: formatStudioCapacityLabel(maxCupoNum, resolvedStudioLayout, nuevoEvento?.categoria, nuevoEvento?.nombre_servicio, tx), ic: 'users' as const }, { k: 'precio' as AdminPickerType, l: tx('PRECIO (KC • MÚLT. 10) *', 'PRICE (KC • MULT. 10) *'), v: currentPriceKc === 0 ? tx('0 KC (Cortesía)', '0 KC (Courtesy)') : `${escrow40Kc + puerta60Kc} KC`, ic: 'zap' as const }],
            ]).map((pair, pIdx) => (
              <View key={`pair_${pIdx}`} style={styles.rowGrid}>
                {pair.map((cell) => (
                  <View key={String(cell.k)} style={styles.colGrid}>
                    <Text style={[styles.inputLabel, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{cell.l}</Text>
                    <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.selectorBtn, inputLight]} activeOpacity={0.8} onPress={() => openSelector(cell.k)}>
                      <Text style={[styles.selectorValue, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit minimumFontScale={0.78}>{cell.v}</Text>
                      <Feather name={cell.ic} size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  </View>
                ))}
              </View>
            ))}

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.studioMapBtn, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1' }]}
              activeOpacity={0.85} onPress={() => { Keyboard.dismiss(); triggerHaptic?.('Selection'); setIsStudioModalOpen(true); }}
            >
              <Feather name="grid" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              <Text style={[styles.selectorValue, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                {tx(
                  `DISEÑAR MAPA 3D DEL ESTUDIO (${formatStudioLayoutButtonLabel(resolvedStudioLayout, maxCupoNum, nuevoEvento?.categoria, nuevoEvento?.nombre_servicio, false)})`,
                  `CONFIGURE 3D STUDIO MAP (${formatStudioLayoutButtonLabel(resolvedStudioLayout, maxCupoNum, nuevoEvento?.categoria, nuevoEvento?.nombre_servicio, true)})`
                )}
              </Text>
              <Feather name="sliders" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <View style={[styles.escrowPreviewPill, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#7DD3FC', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' }]}>
              <Feather name="shield" size={13} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              <Text style={[styles.escrowPreviewText, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>
                {tx('Custodia 40% hoy:', '40% Escrow today:')} <Text style={{ color: themeColors.cyanText, fontWeight: '900' }}>{escrow40Kc} KC</Text> • {tx('Puerta QR 60%:', '60% QR Door:')} <Text style={{ color: themeColors.purpleText, fontWeight: '900' }}>{puerta60Kc} KC</Text>
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View style={{ borderRadius: 14, padding: 12, marginTop: 4, marginBottom: 12, borderWidth: 1.2, elevation: 0, backgroundColor: splitCfg.enabled ? (isLight ? '#FAF5FF' : 'rgba(157, 78, 221, 0.10)') : (isLight ? '#F8FAFC' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)'), borderColor: splitCfg.enabled ? (isLight ? '#C4B5FD' : 'rgba(224,170,255,0.45)') : (isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.14)') }}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                activeOpacity={0.85}
                style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: 8 }}
                onPress={() => {
                  triggerHaptic?.('Selection');
                  const next = !splitCfg.enabled;
                  setNuevoEvento({ ...nuevoEvento, split_escrow_enabled: next, permite_dividir_cuenta: next, split_max_personas: splitCfg.maxPersonas, max_division_personas: splitCfg.maxPersonas, atributos: { ...attrs, split_escrow_enabled: next, permite_dividir_cuenta: next, split_max_personas: splitCfg.maxPersonas } });
                }}
              >
                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                  <Text style={[styles.inputLabel, { color: splitCfg.enabled ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.textSecondary, marginBottom: 2 }]}>
                    {tx('Dividir Cuenta (Split-Escrow 2 a 4 personas)', 'Split Bill (Split-Escrow 2 to 4 people)')}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={{ fontSize: 10.5, color: themeColors.textMuted }}>
                    {splitCfg.enabled ? tx(`Equipo habilitado hasta ${splitCfg.maxPersonas} personas.`, `Team enabled up to ${splitCfg.maxPersonas} people.`) : tx('Oculto en la vista del atleta.', 'Hidden on athlete view.')}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <View style={[{ paddingHorizontal: 11, paddingVertical: 6, borderRadius: 9, borderWidth: 1.2, elevation: 0 }, splitCfg.enabled ? { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.15)', borderColor: COLORS.cyan } : { backgroundColor: isLight ? '#E2E8F0' : '#0B1320', borderColor: isLight ? '#94A3B8' : '#1E293B' }]}>
                  <Text style={{ fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '900', color: splitCfg.enabled ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textMuted }}>
                    {splitCfg.enabled ? tx('ACTIVO', 'ENABLED') : tx('DESACTIVADO', 'DISABLED')}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              {splitCfg.enabled && (
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, marginTop: 10 }}>
                  {[2, 3, 4].map((n) => {
                    const active = splitCfg.maxPersonas === n;
                    return (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        key={n}
                        activeOpacity={0.85}
                        onPress={() => { triggerHaptic?.('Selection'); setNuevoEvento({ ...nuevoEvento, split_max_personas: n, max_division_personas: n, atributos: { ...attrs, split_max_personas: n, max_division_personas: n } }); }}
                        style={{ flex: 1, height: 36, borderRadius: 10, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', borderWidth: 1.2, elevation: 0, backgroundColor: active ? COLORS.cyan : (isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#0B111E'), borderColor: active ? '#B8FFFF' : (isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : '#1E293B') }}
                      >
                        <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', color: active ? '#050505' : themeColors.textPrimary }}>{tx(`Máx. ${n} Pers.`, `Max ${n} Pax`)}</Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    );
                  })}
                </View>
              )}
            </View>

            <Text style={[styles.inputLabel, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{tx('ATRIBUTOS TÉCNICOS Y ENTORNO (3D)', 'TECHNICAL ATTRIBUTES & ENVIRONMENT (3D)')}</Text>
            <View style={styles.attrSectionBox}>
              {attrRows.map((row) => (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={String(row.key)} activeOpacity={0.85}
                  style={[styles.attrBtn3D, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#7DD3FC', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' }]}
                  onPress={() => openSelector(row.key)}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.attrBtnLbl, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{row.label}</Text>
                  <View style={[styles.attrValPill3D, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#06B6D4', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' }]}>
                    <Text style={[styles.attrValTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]} numberOfLines={1}>{translateEventCatalogTerm(row.value, language)}</Text>
                    <Feather name="chevron-down" size={13} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                  </View>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              ))}
            </View>

            <View style={styles.rowGrid}>
              <View style={styles.colGrid}>
                <Text style={[styles.inputLabel, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{tx('COACH / ESPECIALISTA', 'COACH / SPECIALIST')}</Text>
                <TextInput
                  style={[styles.textInput, inputLight, TEXT_SEL, { fontSize: 16 }]}
                  placeholder={tx('Ej. Coach Hermes', 'e.g., Coach Hermes')}
                  placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
                  value={nuevoEvento?.instructor_nombre ?? ''}
                  onChangeText={(t) => setNuevoEvento({ ...nuevoEvento, instructor_nombre: t })}
                />
              </View>
              <View style={styles.colGrid}>
                <Text style={[styles.inputLabel, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{tx('EDAD PERMITIDA *', 'AGE POLICY *')}</Text>
                <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.selectorBtn, inputLight]} activeOpacity={0.8} onPress={() => openSelector('edad')}>
                  <Text style={[styles.selectorValue, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit minimumFontScale={0.72}>{translateEventCatalogTerm(safeAgeVal, language)}</Text>
                  <Feather name="shield" size={14} color={themeColors.purpleText} />
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            </View>

            <Text style={[styles.inputLabel, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{tx('QUÉ LLEVAR / REQUISITOS (MULTI-SELECCIÓN)', 'WHAT TO BRING / REQUIREMENTS (MULTI-SELECT)')}</Text>
            <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.selectorBtn, inputLight]} activeOpacity={0.8} onPress={() => openSelector('requisitos')}>
              <Text style={[styles.selectorValue, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit minimumFontScale={0.78}>
                {parseRequirementsList(nuevoEvento?.requisitos_traer || 'Agua / Hidratación personal').map((r) => translateEventCatalogTerm(r, language)).join(', ')}
              </Text>
              <Feather name="check-square" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <View style={styles.labelRow}>
              <Text style={[styles.inputLabel, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{tx('UBICACIÓN / LINK DE GOOGLE MAPS *', 'LOCATION / GOOGLE MAPS LINK *')}</Text>
              {isResolvingMapUrl ? (
                <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={COLORS.cyan} style={{ marginBottom: 6 }} />
              ) : nuevoEvento?.latitud && nuevoEvento?.longitud ? (
                <Text style={[styles.coordsBadgeText, { color: themeColors.green }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                  ✓ {detectedZone ? `${detectedZone} • ` : 'GPS: '}{Number(nuevoEvento.latitud).toFixed(4)}, {Number(nuevoEvento.longitud).toFixed(4)}
                </Text>
              ) : detectedZone ? (
                <Text style={[styles.coordsBadgeText, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]} numberOfLines={1}>✓ {detectedZone}</Text>
              ) : null}
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity activeOpacity={0.85} style={[styles.mapsLinkBtn3D, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1' }]} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic?.('Light'); openExternalLinkSafe('https://www.google.com/maps'); }}>
              <Text style={[styles.mapsLinkBtnTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{tx('📍 Toca para obtener link en Google Maps', '📍 Tap to get link on Google Maps')}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
            <TextInput style={[styles.textInput, inputLight, TEXT_SEL, { fontSize: 16 }]} placeholder={tx('Pega aquí el link de Google Maps...', 'Paste the Google Maps link here...')} placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted} value={nuevoEvento?.ubicacion || ''} onChangeText={handleLocationChange} autoCapitalize="none" autoCorrect={false} />
            <Text style={[styles.inputLabel, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{tx('DESCRIPCIÓN DEL EVENTO *', 'EVENT DESCRIPTION *')}</Text>
            <TextInput style={[styles.textInput, styles.textArea, inputLight, TEXT_SEL, { fontSize: 16 }]} placeholder={tx('Describe la dinámica de la clase, beneficios y experiencia...', 'Describe class dynamics, benefits, and experience...')} placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted} multiline textAlignVertical="top" value={nuevoEvento?.descripcion || ''} onChangeText={(t) => setNuevoEvento({ ...nuevoEvento, descripcion: t })} />

            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.publishBtn} activeOpacity={0.85} onPress={handlePublishPress} disabled={isPublishing}>
              {isPublishing ? (
                <><KineticLoader size={18} color="#050505" /><Text style={styles.publishBtnText}>{tx('⚡ PUBLICANDO EVENTO...', '⚡ PUBLISHING EVENT...')}</Text></>
              ) : (
                <><Feather name={effectiveEditId ? 'save' : 'upload-cloud'} size={18} color="#050505" /><Text style={styles.publishBtnText}>{effectiveEditId ? tx('GUARDAR CAMBIOS', 'SAVE CHANGES') : tx('PUBLICAR EN EL RADAR', 'PUBLISH TO RADAR')}</Text></>
              )}
            </TouchableOpacity>
            {effectiveEditId && onDeleteEvento && (
              <TouchableOpacity style={styles.deleteBtn} activeOpacity={0.8} onPress={() => { Keyboard.dismiss(); onDeleteEvento(effectiveEditId); }} disabled={isPublishing}>
                <Feather name="trash-2" size={16} color={COLORS.red} style={{ marginRight: 8 }} />
                <Text style={styles.deleteBtnText}>{tx('ELIMINAR EXPERIENCIA', 'DELETE EXPERIENCE')}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            )}
            <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.backBtnStandard, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' }]} onPress={() => { Keyboard.dismiss(); onClose(); }} activeOpacity={0.8}>
              <Feather name="arrow-left" size={15} color={themeColors.textPrimary} />
              <Text style={[styles.backBtnStandardText, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('ATRÁS', 'BACK')}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </ScrollView>

          <AdminEventPickerModal
            inlineOverlay={true}
            activePicker={activePicker} onClose={() => setActivePicker(null)} pickerConfig={pickerConfig}
            selectedRequisitosDraft={selectedRequisitosDraft} toggleRequirementItem={toggleRequirementItem}
            confirmMultiRequirements={confirmMultiRequirements} triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic}
          />

          <KineticStudioLayoutModal
            inlineOverlay={true}
            visible={isStudioModalOpen} onClose={() => setIsStudioModalOpen(false)}
            eventKey={effectiveEditId ? String(effectiveEditId) : (nuevoEvento?.nombre_servicio ? String(nuevoEvento.nombre_servicio).trim() : 'draft_admin_clean')}
            eventTitle={nuevoEvento?.nombre_servicio || tx('Nuevo Evento', 'New Event')}
            discipline={nuevoEvento?.categoria || 'Fuerza'} maxCupo={maxCupoNum} occupiedCount={0}
            initialLayout={resolvedStudioLayout} canConfigure={true} initialConfigMode={true}
            onSaveLayout={(layout) => {
              const safeSpots = Math.min(MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS, layout.totalSpots);
              const key = String(effectiveEditId || nuevoEvento?.nombre_servicio || '').trim();
              if (key) saveStudioLayoutLocal(key, layout);
              setNuevoEvento((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, cupo_maximo: String(safeSpots), studio_layout_json: layout, atributos: { ...(prev?.atributos || {}), studio_layout: layout } }));
            }}
          />
        </View>
      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
};

export default AdminEventEditorModal;

    FILE: src/components/AdminModuleModal.tsx

    // === src/components/AdminModuleModal.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  InteractionManager,
  Keyboard,
  KeyboardAvoidingView,
  Platform,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { AdminAffiliatesDirectoryView } from './admin/AdminAffiliatesDirectoryView';
import { AdminAuditLogsView } from './admin/AdminAuditLogsView';
import { AdminCatalogAndMetricsView } from './admin/AdminCatalogAndMetricsView';
import { AdminDispersionSpeiView } from './admin/AdminDispersionSpeiView';
import { AdminEscrowActiveView } from './admin/AdminEscrowActiveView';
import { AdminKinetiClubModerationView } from './admin/AdminKinetiClubModerationView';
import {
  AdminAthletesAndTreasuryView,
  AdminLiquidityView,
} from './AdminTreasuryViews';
import {
  KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
  KineticSwipeCloseHeader,
} from './shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { TacticalAlertModal } from './BovedaPassAndReceiptModals';
import { triggerHaptic } from './SharedUI';

export type AdminModuleType =
  | null
  | 'LIQUIDEZ'
  | 'DIRECTORIO_ATLETAS'
  | 'SOCIOS'
  | 'ESCROW_ACTIVO'
  | 'DISPERSION_B2B'
  | 'SOLICITUDES'
  | 'EVENTOS'
  | 'ATLETAS'
  | 'TESORERIA'
  | 'AUDIT_LOGS'
  | 'KINETI_CLUB'
  | 'KINETICLUB';

const VIRTUALIZED_MODULES = new Set<AdminModuleType>([
  'DISPERSION_B2B',
  'SOCIOS',
  'DIRECTORIO_ATLETAS',
  'AUDIT_LOGS',
]);

export const AdminModuleModal = React.memo(
  ({
    activeModule,
    hasPreviousModule = false,
    walletContextFilter = 'all',
    onStepBack,
    onCloseAll,
    onNavigateModule,
    tokenomicsReales,
    solicitudes = [],
    preRegistrosWeb = [],
    ugcPendingCount = 0,
    experienciasActivas = [],
    sociosReales = [],
    atletasList = [],
    reservasEscrowActivas = [],
    reservasHistoricas = [],
    auditLogs = [],
    distribucionNiveles = [],
    topDisciplinas = [],
    reporteCorteSemanal,
    cambiarEstadoAfiliado,
    crearAfiliadoManual,
    eliminarSocioB2B,
    canAdminDelete = true,
    togglingAffiliateName = null,
    isSavingManualAffiliate = false,
    isSubmitting = false,
    cambiarEstadoServicio,
    gestionarPreRegistroWeb,
    guardarEvento,
    eliminarEvento,
    user,
    abrirEditorOnTheGo,
    inyectarKCAtleta,
    isInjectingKC = false,
    actualizarFiscalSocio,
    isUpdatingFiscal = false,
    exportarReporteMaestroPDF,
    exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF,
    isExportingPDF = false,
    copiarDatoBancario,
    eliminarCuentaAtleta,
    ejecutarBarridoNoShow40,
    isSweepingNoShow = false,
  }: any) => {
    const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
    const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
    const styles = useMemo(
      () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme),
      [resolvedTheme]
    );
    const scrollViewRef = useRef<ScrollView>(null);

    const [isKeyboardVisible, setIsKeyboardVisible] = useState(false);
    const [inlineAlertConfig, setInlineAlertConfig] = useState<any>({ visible: false });
    const [moduleSubOverlay, setModuleSubOverlay] = useState<React.ReactNode>(null);
    const subOverlayBackHandlerRef = useRef<(() => boolean) | null>(null);
    const registerSubOverlayBackHandler = useCallback((fn: (() => boolean) | null) => {
      subOverlayBackHandlerRef.current = fn;
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
      const subs = [
        Keyboard.addListener('keyboardWillShow', () => setIsKeyboardVisible(true)),
        Keyboard.addListener('keyboardDidShow', () => setIsKeyboardVisible(true)),
        Keyboard.addListener('keyboardWillHide', () => setIsKeyboardVisible(false)),
        Keyboard.addListener('keyboardDidHide', () => setIsKeyboardVisible(false)),
      ];
      const onWebFocusIn = (e: any) => {
        const tag = String(e?.target?.tagName || '').toUpperCase();
        if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA') setIsKeyboardVisible(true);
      };
      if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof document !== 'undefined') {
        document.addEventListener('focusin', onWebFocusIn);
      }
      return () => {
        subs.forEach((s) => s.remove());
        if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof document !== 'undefined') {
          document.removeEventListener('focusin', onWebFocusIn);
        }
      };
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (!activeModule) {
        setIsKeyboardVisible(false);
        setInlineAlertConfig({ visible: false });
        setModuleSubOverlay(null);
        subOverlayBackHandlerRef.current = null;
      }
    }, [activeModule]);

    const cachedModuleRef = useRef<AdminModuleType>('LIQUIDEZ');
    const resolvedInputModule: AdminModuleType =
      activeModule === 'TESORERIA' ? 'DIRECTORIO_ATLETAS' : activeModule;
    if (resolvedInputModule) {
      cachedModuleRef.current = resolvedInputModule;
    }
    const displayModule = resolvedInputModule || cachedModuleRef.current;
    const isVirtualizedModule = VIRTUALIZED_MODULES.has(displayModule);
    const [readyModule, setReadyModule] = useState<AdminModuleType>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (!resolvedInputModule) return;
      if (!VIRTUALIZED_MODULES.has(resolvedInputModule)) {
        setReadyModule(resolvedInputModule);
        return;
      }
      let cancelled = false;
      const task = InteractionManager.runAfterInteractions(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setReadyModule(resolvedInputModule);
      });
      return () => {
        cancelled = true;
        task?.cancel?.();
      };
    }, [resolvedInputModule]);

    const canRenderVirtualized = !isVirtualizedModule || readyModule === displayModule;

    useEffect(() => {
      if (activeModule) {
        const timer = setTimeout(() => {
          scrollViewRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: false });
        }, 10);
        return () => clearTimeout(timer);
      }
    }, [activeModule]);

    const closeHandler = onCloseAll || onStepBack;

    const moduleBody = (
      <>
        {displayModule === 'LIQUIDEZ' && (
          <AdminLiquidityView
            tokenomicsReales={tokenomicsReales}
            reporteCorteSemanal={reporteCorteSemanal}
            onNavigateModule={onNavigateModule}
          />
        )}

        {(displayModule === 'TESORERIA' ||
          displayModule === 'DIRECTORIO_ATLETAS') && (
          <AdminAthletesAndTreasuryView
            mode={displayModule}
            atletasList={atletasList}
            walletContextFilter={walletContextFilter}
            inyectarKCAtleta={inyectarKCAtleta}
            isInjectingKC={isInjectingKC}
            onNavigateModule={onNavigateModule}
            eliminarCuentaAtleta={eliminarCuentaAtleta}
            canAdminDelete={canAdminDelete}
          />
        )}

        {displayModule === 'SOCIOS' && (
          <AdminAffiliatesDirectoryView
            sociosReales={sociosReales}
            cambiarEstadoAfiliado={cambiarEstadoAfiliado}
            crearAfiliadoManual={crearAfiliadoManual}
            eliminarSocioB2B={eliminarSocioB2B}
            canAdminDelete={canAdminDelete}
            togglingAffiliateName={togglingAffiliateName}
            isSavingManualAffiliate={isSavingManualAffiliate}
            isModuleActive={activeModule === 'SOCIOS'}
            onMountSubOverlay={setModuleSubOverlay}
            onRegisterSubOverlayBackHandler={registerSubOverlayBackHandler}
            onRequestAlert={setInlineAlertConfig}
          />
        )}

        {displayModule === 'ESCROW_ACTIVO' && (
          <AdminEscrowActiveView
            reservasEscrowActivas={reservasEscrowActivas}
            ejecutarBarridoNoShow40={ejecutarBarridoNoShow40}
            isSweepingNoShow={isSweepingNoShow}
            onRequestAlert={setInlineAlertConfig}
          />
        )}

        {displayModule === 'DISPERSION_B2B' && (
          <AdminDispersionSpeiView
            reporteCorteSemanal={reporteCorteSemanal}
            sociosReales={sociosReales}
            reservasHistoricas={reservasHistoricas}
            actualizarFiscalSocio={actualizarFiscalSocio}
            isUpdatingFiscal={isUpdatingFiscal}
            exportarReporteMaestroPDF={exportarReporteMaestroPDF}
            exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF={exportarEstadoCuentaSocioPDF}
            isExportingPDF={isExportingPDF}
            copiarDatoBancario={copiarDatoBancario}
          />
        )}

        {(displayModule === 'SOLICITUDES' ||
          displayModule === 'EVENTOS' ||
          displayModule === 'ATLETAS') && (
          <AdminCatalogAndMetricsView
            mode={displayModule}
            solicitudes={solicitudes}
            preRegistrosWeb={preRegistrosWeb}
            experienciasActivas={experienciasActivas}
            distribucionNiveles={distribucionNiveles}
            topDisciplinas={topDisciplinas}
            cambiarEstadoServicio={cambiarEstadoServicio}
            gestionarPreRegistroWeb={gestionarPreRegistroWeb}
            copiarDatoBancario={copiarDatoBancario}
            guardarEvento={guardarEvento}
            eliminarEvento={eliminarEvento}
            user={user}
            isSubmitting={isSubmitting}
            abrirEditorOnTheGo={abrirEditorOnTheGo}
            onNavigateModule={onNavigateModule}
          />
        )}

        {displayModule === 'AUDIT_LOGS' && (
          <AdminAuditLogsView auditLogs={auditLogs} />
        )}

        {(displayModule === 'KINETI_CLUB' || displayModule === 'KINETICLUB') && (
          <AdminKinetiClubModerationView ugcPendingCount={ugcPendingCount} onRequestAlert={setInlineAlertConfig} />
        )}
      </>
    );

    return (
      <ReactNativeModal
        isVisible={Boolean(activeModule)}
        onBackdropPress={() => {
          if (inlineAlertConfig?.visible) {
            setInlineAlertConfig((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, visible: false }));
            return;
          }
          if (subOverlayBackHandlerRef.current?.()) {
            return;
          }
          closeHandler?.();
        }}
        onBackButtonPress={() => {
          if (inlineAlertConfig?.visible) {
            setInlineAlertConfig((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, visible: false }));
            return;
          }
          if (subOverlayBackHandlerRef.current?.()) {
            return;
          }
          (onStepBack || onCloseAll)?.();
        }}
        style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
        backdropColor={isLightMode ? '#0F172A' : COLORS.background}
        backdropOpacity={isLightMode ? 0.52 : 0.93}
        backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
        animationOutTiming={180}
        hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
        useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        animationIn="slideInUp"
        animationOut="slideOutDown"
        animationInTiming={200}
        avoidKeyboard={true}
      >
        <KeyboardAvoidingView
          pointerEvents="box-none"
          behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
          style={{ flex: 1, width: '100%', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
        >
          <View
            data-kinetic-sheet-open="true"
            pointerEvents={moduleSubOverlay ? 'none' : 'auto'}
            style={[
              globalStyles.bottomSheet,
              KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
              isLightMode && {
                backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
                borderTopColor: '#0284C7',
              },
              {
                paddingBottom: insets.bottom > 0 ? insets.bottom + 14 : 24,
                ...(moduleSubOverlay ? { elevation: 0 } : null),
              },
            ]}
          >
            <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={closeHandler} />

            {isVirtualizedModule ? (
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                {canRenderVirtualized ? (
                  moduleBody
                ) : (
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', paddingVertical: 48 }}>
                    <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                  </View>
                )}
              </View>
            ) : (
              <ScrollView
                ref={scrollViewRef}
                style={{ flex: 1 }}
                showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
                keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
                nestedScrollEnabled={true}
                bounces={false}
                overScrollMode="never"
                contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 20 }}
              >
                {moduleBody}
              </ScrollView>
            )}

            {!isKeyboardVisible && (
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.backBtn}
                activeOpacity={0.85}
                onPress={onStepBack || onCloseAll}
              >
                <Feather
                  name="arrow-left"
                  size={15}
                  color={themeColors.textPrimary}
                />
                <Text style={[styles.backBtnText, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                  {hasPreviousModule
                    ? tx('VOLVER', 'BACK')
                    : tx('VOLVER AL ADMIN HUB', 'BACK TO ADMIN HUB')}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            )}
          </View>
          {moduleSubOverlay}
          <TacticalAlertModal inlineOverlay alertConfig={inlineAlertConfig} setAlertConfig={setInlineAlertConfig} triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic} />
        </KeyboardAvoidingView>
      </ReactNativeModal>
    );
  }
);

export default AdminModuleModal;

const baseStyles = StyleSheet.create({
  backBtn: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    height: 48,
    borderRadius: 14,

import React, { memo } from 'react';

export const AdminAffiliatesAndEscrowView = memo(
  ({
    mode,
    sociosReales = [],
    reservasEscrowActivas = [],
    cambiarEstadoAfiliado,
    crearAfiliadoManual,
    togglingAffiliateName = null,
    isSavingManualAffiliate = false,
  }: {
    mode: 'SOCIOS' | 'ESCROW_ACTIVO';
    sociosReales: any[];
    reservasEscrowActivas: any[];
    cambiarEstadoAfiliado?: (
      afiliado: Record<string, unknown>,
      nuevoEstado?: 'activo' | 'inactivo' | 'prospecto',
      cb?: () => void
    ) => Promise<unknown>;
    crearAfiliadoManual?: (payload: any, cb?: () => void) => Promise<any>;
    togglingAffiliateName?: string | null;
    isSavingManualAffiliate?: boolean;
  }) => {
    if (mode === 'ESCROW_ACTIVO') {
      return (
        <AdminEscrowActiveView reservasEscrowActivas={reservasEscrowActivas} />
      );
    }
    return (
      <AdminAffiliatesDirectoryView
        sociosReales={sociosReales}
        cambiarEstadoAfiliado={cambiarEstadoAfiliado}
        crearAfiliadoManual={crearAfiliadoManual}
        togglingAffiliateName={togglingAffiliateName}
        isSavingManualAffiliate={isSavingManualAffiliate}
      />
    );
  }
);

export const localizeVipTierLabel = (rawTier: string | undefined, isEn: boolean): string => {
  const found = VIP_GEM_CONFIGS.find(
    (g) => g.dbValue.toLowerCase() === String(rawTier || '').trim().toLowerCase()
  );
  if (found) return isEn ? found.labelEn : found.labelEs;
  return rawTier || (isEn ? 'Base Access' : 'Acceso Base');
};

    // === src/components/AdminTreasuryViews.tsx === //
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { fetchResumenPasesMaestrosRpc } from '../services/admin/adminSecurityAndSpeiHelpers';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { AdminAthletesDirectoryView } from './admin/AdminAthletesDirectoryView';
import { AdminAthleteTreasuryPanel } from './admin/AdminAthleteTreasuryPanel';
import { baseStyles } from './AdminTreasuryViews.styles';

export { AdminAthleteTreasuryPanel };

// ============================================================================
// 1. MÓDULO: RADIOGRAFÍA FINANCIERA (LIQUIDEZ — ARQUEO CONTABLE LIMPIO)
// ============================================================================
export const AdminLiquidityView = memo(
  ({
    tokenomicsReales,
    reporteCorteSemanal,
  }: {
    tokenomicsReales: any;
    reporteCorteSemanal?: any;
    onNavigateModule?: (mod: any, filter?: any) => void;
  }) => {
    const { resolvedTheme, isLightMode, themeColors, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
    const styles = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme), [resolvedTheme]);
    const [rpcMasterPasses, setRpcMasterPasses] = useState<{ dia: number; semana: number; mes: number }>({
      dia: 0,
      semana: 0,
      mes: 0,
    });

    useEffect(() => {
      let cancelled = false;
      fetchResumenPasesMaestrosRpc(null).then((res) => {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setRpcMasterPasses({ dia: res.dia, semana: res.semana, mes: res.mes });
        }
      });
      return () => {
        cancelled = true;
      };
    }, []);

    const pasesDia = Math.max(rpcMasterPasses.dia, Number(reporteCorteSemanal?.total_pases_maestros_dia) || 0);
    const pasesSemana = Math.max(rpcMasterPasses.semana, Number(reporteCorteSemanal?.total_pases_maestros_semana) || 0);
    const pasesMes = Math.max(rpcMasterPasses.mes, Number(reporteCorteSemanal?.total_pases_maestros_mes) || 0);

    const totalRaw = Math.max(0, Number(tokenomicsReales?.masaMonetariaTotal) || 0);
    const totalDivisor = Math.max(1, totalRaw);
    const mem = Math.max(0, Number(tokenomicsReales?.sumaMembresia) || 0);
    const top = Math.max(0, Number(tokenomicsReales?.sumaTopup) || 0);
    const esc = Math.max(0, Number(tokenomicsReales?.escrowTotal) || 0);
    const bookingsCount = tokenomicsReales?.reservasActivasCount || 0;
    const memPct = Math.round((mem / totalDivisor) * 100);
    const topPct = Math.round((top / totalDivisor) * 100);
    const escPct = Math.round((esc / totalDivisor) * 100);

    return (
      <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: 18, paddingTop: 4 }}>
        <Text style={[styles.moduleTitle, { lineHeight: 26, paddingTop: 2 }]}>
          {tx('Radiografía Financiera', 'Financial X-Ray')}
        </Text>
        <Text style={styles.moduleSub}>
          {tx(
            'Arqueo contable del circulante Kineticoins respaldado en billeteras y custodia activa.',
            'Accounting ledger of Kineticoin supply backed across wallets and active escrow.'
          )}
        </Text>

        <View style={styles.heroLiquidityBox}>
          <Text style={[styles.stepLabel, { marginTop: 0 }]}>
            {tx('MASA MONETARIA TOTAL EN CIRCULACIÓN', 'TOTAL MONETARY SUPPLY IN CIRCULATION')}
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.heroBigKc}>
            {totalRaw}{' '}
            <Text style={{ fontSize: 20, color: themeColors.cyanText }}>KC</Text>
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.simSub}>
            {tx(
              'Paridad Operativa Respaldada 1 KC = $1.00 MXN (IVA Incluido)',
              'Backed Operating Parity 1 KC = $1.00 MXN (VAT Included)'
            )}
          </Text>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.cardContainer}>
          <Text style={[styles.stepLabel, { marginTop: 0 }]}>
            {tx('ARQUEO CONTABLE DEL CIRCULANTE', 'CIRCULATING SUPPLY ACCOUNTING')}
          </Text>

          <View style={[styles.distCard3D, { elevation: 0 }]}>
            <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 10 }}>
              <Text style={styles.whiteBold}>{tx('KC en Membresía (Billeteras)', 'Membership KC (Wallets)')}</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.simSub, { marginTop: 3 }]}>
                {tx('Saldo asignado por planes mensuales vigentes', 'Balance allocated from active monthly plans')}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <View style={{ alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>

              <Text style={[styles.simSub, { marginTop: 3 }]}>

              </Text>

                {tx(`${bookingsCount} reservas activas pendientes de check-in QR`, `${bookingsCount} active bookings awaiting QR check-in`)}
