import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase';
import { emitKineticRadarUpdated } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { normalizeAffiliateKey } from '../adminB2BCutEngine';

export {
  deleteKinetiClubPost,
  deleteKinetiClubStory,
  deleteKinetiClubTeam,
  fetchKinetiClubModerationData,
  fetchPendingUgcReportsCount,
  getInitialKinetiClubModerationSnapshot,
  isPrivateOrSystemChannelRow,
  updateKinetiClubUgcReportStatus,
} from './adminUgcModerationHelpers';

export const DELETED_AFFILIATES_STORAGE_KEY = '@kinetic_deleted_affiliates_v1';
const EVENTS_SNAPSHOT_STORAGE_KEY = '@kinetic_events_snapshot_v2';

const emitBovedaUpdated = () => {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.dispatchEvent === 'function') {
    try { window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('kinetic:boveda-updated')); } catch {}
  }
};

async function refundSingleWalletSafe(userId: string, amount: number, wallet: 'membresia' | 'topup'): Promise<void> {
  if (!userId || amount <= 0) return;
  const { error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('inyectar_saldo_boveda', { p_user_id: userId, p_cantidad: amount, p_billetera: wallet });
  if (rpcErr) throw new Error(rpcErr.message);
}

/**
 * Reembolsa el 40% Escrow a todos los atletas con reserva activa/pendiente/confirmada,
 * elimina en cascada TODAS las reservas asociadas (por servicio_id y huérfanas con
 * servicio_id IS NULL cuyo servicio o nombre_servicio coincida con el título del evento)
 * y finalmente elimina el evento de public.servicios.
 */
export async function deleteServiceWithCascadeAndRefund(
  serviceId: string,
  fallbackTitle?: string
): Promise<{ refundedAthletes: number; deletedReservations: number }> {
  const cleanId = String(serviceId || '').trim();
  if (!cleanId) throw new Error('ID de servicio inválido.');

  const { data: servRow } = await supabase
    .from('servicios')
    .select('id, nombre_servicio, negocio_nombre')
    .eq('id', cleanId)
    .maybeSingle();

  const rawTitle = String(servRow?.nombre_servicio || fallbackTitle || '').trim();
  const normTitle = rawTitle.toLowerCase();

  const [byIdRes, orphanNullRes] = await Promise.all([
    supabase
      .from('reservas')
      .select(
        'id, usuario_id, servicio_id, servicio, nombre_servicio, titulo_clase, estado, precio_kc, costo_total, monto_escrow, escrow_membresia, escrow_topup'
      )
      .eq('servicio_id', cleanId),
    normTitle
      ? supabase
          .from('reservas')
          .select(
            'id, usuario_id, servicio_id, servicio, nombre_servicio, titulo_clase, estado, precio_kc, costo_total, monto_escrow, escrow_membresia, escrow_topup'
          )
          .is('servicio_id', null)
          .limit(300)
      : Promise.resolve({ data: [] as any[], error: null }),
  ]);

  const matchedMap = new Map<string, any>();
  (byIdRes.data || []).forEach((r: any) => {
    if (r?.id) matchedMap.set(String(r.id), r);
  });

  if (normTitle && Array.isArray(orphanNullRes.data)) {
    orphanNullRes.data.forEach((r: any) => {
      if (!r?.id) return;
      const s1 = String(r.servicio || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const s2 = String(r.nombre_servicio || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const s3 = String(r.titulo_clase || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (s1 === normTitle || s2 === normTitle || s3 === normTitle) {
        matchedMap.set(String(r.id), r);
      }
    });
  }

  const allMatched = Array.from(matchedMap.values());
  let refundedAthletes = 0;
  const notifyTasks: Promise<any>[] = [];

  for (const r of allMatched) {
    const st = String(r.estado || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const isRefundableStatus = st === 'activa' || st === 'pendiente' || st === 'confirmada';
    if (!isRefundableStatus || !r.usuario_id) continue;

    const escMem = Math.max(0, Number(r.escrow_membresia) || 0);
    const escTop = Math.max(0, Number(r.escrow_topup) || 0);
    const rawMontoEscrow = Number(r.monto_escrow);
    const fallbackTotal =
      !Number.isNaN(rawMontoEscrow) && rawMontoEscrow > 0
        ? Math.round(rawMontoEscrow)
        : 0;

    let refunded = false;
    let totalRefundKc = 0;
    if (escMem > 0 || escTop > 0) {
      if (escMem > 0) await refundSingleWalletSafe(String(r.usuario_id), escMem, 'membresia');
      if (escTop > 0) await refundSingleWalletSafe(String(r.usuario_id), escTop, 'topup');
      refunded = true;
      totalRefundKc = escMem + escTop;
    } else if (fallbackTotal > 0) {
      await refundSingleWalletSafe(String(r.usuario_id), fallbackTotal, 'membresia');
      refunded = true;
      totalRefundKc = fallbackTotal;
    }

    if (refunded) {
      refundedAthletes += 1;
      notifyTasks.push(
        Promise.resolve(
          supabase.from('notificaciones').insert({
            usuario_id: String(r.usuario_id),
            titulo: 'Reembolso 100% Escrow por Cancelación',
            mensaje: `Se ha reembolsado íntegramente ${totalRefundKc} KC a tu Bóveda por la cancelación del evento "${rawTitle || 'Experiencia'}". Tu recibo está disponible en Bóveda.`,
            tipo: 'reembolso',
            leida: false,
            created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          })
        ),
        Promise.resolve(
          supabase.from('admin_audit_logs').insert({
            accion: 'CANCELACION_EVENTO_REEMBOLSO_100_ESCROW',
            usuario_id: String(r.usuario_id),
            servicio_id: cleanId,
            detalles: { evento: rawTitle, reembolso_kc: totalRefundKc, reserva_id: r.id },
            created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          })
        )
      );
    }
  }

  if (notifyTasks.length > 0) {
    await Promise.allSettled(notifyTasks);
  }

  // Borrado total en cascada de public.reservas para erradicar pases huérfanos en Bóveda
  const matchedIds = allMatched.map((r) => String(r.id)).filter(Boolean);
  if (matchedIds.length > 0) {
    await supabase.from('reservas').delete().in('id', matchedIds);
  }
  await supabase.from('reservas').delete().eq('servicio_id', cleanId);

  if (rawTitle) {
    await Promise.allSettled([
      supabase.from('reservas').delete().is('servicio_id', null).ilike('servicio', rawTitle),
      supabase.from('reservas').delete().is('servicio_id', null).ilike('nombre_servicio', rawTitle),
    ]);
  }

  const { error: delServErr } = await supabase.from('servicios').delete().eq('id', cleanId);
  if (delServErr) throw new Error(delServErr.message);

  AsyncStorage.removeItem(EVENTS_SNAPSHOT_STORAGE_KEY).catch(() => {});
  emitKineticRadarUpdated();
  if (refundedAthletes > 0 || matchedIds.length > 0) {
    emitBovedaUpdated();
  }

  return { refundedAthletes, deletedReservations: matchedIds.length };
}

// === PERSISTENCIA DE AFILIADOS ELIMINADOS (EVITA RESURRECCIÓN DE SEMILLA LOCAL) === //
export async function readDeletedAffiliatesSet(): Promise<Set<string>> {
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(DELETED_AFFILIATES_STORAGE_KEY);
    const arr = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
    return new Set(Array.isArray(arr) ? arr.map((x) => String(x || '').trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean) : []);
  } catch {
    return new Set();
  }
}

export async function markAffiliateDeleted(id?: string | null, nombre?: string | null): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const set = await readDeletedAffiliatesSet();
    const cleanId = String(id || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const normKey = normalizeAffiliateKey(String(nombre || ''));
    const rawLower = String(nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (cleanId) set.add(`id:${cleanId}`);
    if (normKey) set.add(`key:${normKey}`);
    if (rawLower) set.add(`name:${rawLower}`);
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(DELETED_AFFILIATES_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(Array.from(set)));
  } catch {}
}

export async function unmarkAffiliateDeleted(id?: string | null, nombre?: string | null): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const set = await readDeletedAffiliatesSet();
    const cleanId = String(id || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const normKey = normalizeAffiliateKey(String(nombre || ''));
    const rawLower = String(nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    set.delete(`id:${cleanId}`);
    set.delete(`key:${normKey}`);
    set.delete(`name:${rawLower}`);
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(DELETED_AFFILIATES_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(Array.from(set)));
  } catch {}
}

export function isAffiliateMarkedDeleted(deletedSet: Set<string>, id?: string | null, nombre?: string | null): boolean {
  if (!deletedSet || deletedSet.size === 0) return false;
  const cleanId = String(id || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const normKey = normalizeAffiliateKey(String(nombre || ''));
  const rawLower = String(nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  return (
    (Boolean(cleanId) && deletedSet.has(`id:${cleanId}`)) ||
    (Boolean(normKey) && deletedSet.has(`key:${normKey}`)) ||
    (Boolean(rawLower) && deletedSet.has(`name:${rawLower}`))
  );
}

    FILE: src/services/admin/adminPdfAthleteReceipt.ts

    // === src/services/admin/adminPdfAthleteReceipt.ts === //
import { getActiveKineticLanguage } from '../../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';
import { isMasterPass0Kc } from '../adminB2BCutEngine';
import {
  escapeHtmlSafe,
  formatMxn,
  safeOpenOrShareInvoicePdf,
} from '../kineticPdfEngine';

export const exportarPDFReciboReservaAtleta = async (
  reserva: any,
  athleteName: string
) => {
  const isEn = getActiveKineticLanguage() === 'en';
  if (!reserva) {
    throw new Error(
      isEn
        ? 'Booking record was not found to generate PDF receipt.'
        : 'No se encontró la reserva para generar el PDF.'
    );
  }
  const st = String(reserva.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const isCancel = st === 'cancelada' || st === 'rechazada' || st === 'eliminada';
  const isMasterPass = isMasterPass0Kc(reserva);
  const totalKc = isMasterPass
    ? 0
    : Math.max(
        Number(reserva.costo_total) || 0,
        Number(reserva.precio_kc) || 0,
        (Number(reserva.monto_escrow) || 0) + (Number(reserva.monto_puerta) || 0)
      );
  const escrowKc = isMasterPass
    ? 0
    : Number(reserva.monto_escrow) || Math.round(totalKc * 0.4);
  const puertaKc = isMasterPass
    ? 0
    : Number(reserva.monto_puerta) || Math.max(0, totalKc - escrowKc);
  const folio = `KNT-${String(reserva.id || '00000000')
    .slice(0, 8)
    .toUpperCase()}`;
  const opDateStr = new Date(
    reserva.updated_at || reserva.created_at || Date.now()
  ).toLocaleDateString(isEn ? 'en-US' : 'es-MX', {
    year: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    day: 'numeric',
  });
  const venueFallback = isEn ? 'Official Kinetic Venue' : 'Sede Oficial Kinetic';

  const docTitle = isMasterPass
    ? isCancel
      ? isEn
        ? 'KINETICAPP S.A.S. - CANCELLED MASTER PASS (0 KC)'
        : 'KINETICAPP S.A.S. - PASE MAESTRO CANCELADO (0 KC)'
      : isEn
      ? 'KINETICAPP S.A.S. - OFFICIAL MASTER PASS RECEIPT (0 KC)'
      : 'KINETICAPP S.A.S. - COMPROBANTE PASE MAESTRO OFICIAL (0 KC)'
    : isCancel
    ? isEn
      ? 'KINETICAPP S.A.S. - OFFICIAL ESCROW REFUND RECEIPT'
      : 'KINETICAPP S.A.S. - COMPROBANTE OFICIAL DE REEMBOLSO DE APARTADO'
    : isEn
    ? 'KINETICAPP S.A.S. - OFFICIAL BOOKING & QR SETTLEMENT RECEIPT'
    : 'KINETICAPP S.A.S. - RECIBO OFICIAL DE RESERVA Y LIQUIDACIÓN QR';

  const statusLabel = isMasterPass
    ? isCancel
      ? isEn
        ? 'CANCELLED MASTER PASS (0 KC)'
        : 'PASE MAESTRO CANCELADO (0 KC)'
      : isEn
      ? 'MASTER PASS 0 KC (EXEMPT FROM SPEI PAYOUT $0.00)'
      : 'PASE MAESTRO 0 KC (EXENTO DE DISPERSIÓN SPEI $0.00)'
    : isCancel
    ? isEn
      ? 'REFUNDED TO ATHLETE (40% RETURNED)'
      : 'REEMBOLSADA AL ATLETA (40% DEVUELTO)'
    : isEn
    ? 'COMPLETED & 100% SETTLED'
    : 'COMPLETADA Y LIQUIDADA AL 100%';

  return safeOpenOrShareInvoicePdf(
    `${isEn ? 'Kinetic_Receipt' : 'Recibo_Kinetic'}_${folio}`,
    {
      docTitle,
      docSubtitle: isMasterPass
        ? isEn
          ? 'Master Pass 0 KC Audit Receipt • Exempt from B2B SPEI Settlement ($0.00)'
          : 'Comprobante Auditado de Pase Maestro 0 KC • Exento de Dispersión SPEI ($0.00)'
        : isEn
        ? 'Audited Digital Kineticoins Vault Transaction Receipt (Parity 1 KC = $1.00 MXN)'
        : 'Comprobante Digital Auditado de Operación en Bóveda Kineticoins (Paridad 1 KC = $1.00 MXN)',
      folio,
      periodoLabel: isEn
        ? `TRANSACTION DATE: ${opDateStr.toUpperCase()}`
        : `FECHA DE OPERACIÓN: ${opDateStr.toUpperCase()}`,
      emisorInfo: isEn
        ? `Vault Holder: ${escapeHtmlSafe(athleteName)} | Venue Operator: ${escapeHtmlSafe(reserva.negocio_nombre || venueFallback)}`
        : `Titular de la Bóveda: ${escapeHtmlSafe(athleteName)} | Operador Sede: ${escapeHtmlSafe(reserva.negocio_nombre || venueFallback)}`,
      kpis: [
        {
          label: isEn ? 'SESSION VALUE' : 'VALOR DE LA SESIÓN',
          value: `${totalKc} KC`,
          sub: isMasterPass
            ? isEn ? 'Exempt SPEI $0.00' : 'Exento SPEI $0.00'
            : formatMxn(totalKc),
          accentRgb: [0.06, 0.45, 0.68],
        },
        {
          label: isEn ? 'PROTECTED ESCROW (40%)' : 'APARTADO PROTEGIDO (40%)',
          value: `${escrowKc} KC`,
          sub: isMasterPass
            ? isEn ? 'Master Pass 0 KC' : 'Pase Maestro 0 KC'
            : isCancel
            ? isEn ? 'Refunded to Vault' : 'Reintegrado a Bóveda'
            : isEn ? 'Initial custody' : 'Custodia inicial',
          accentRgb: [0.55, 0.22, 0.82],
        },
        {
          label: isEn ? 'FRONT DESK QR (60%)' : 'LIQUIDACIÓN QR (60%)',
          value: isCancel || isMasterPass ? '0 KC' : `${puertaKc} KC`,
          sub: isMasterPass
            ? isEn ? 'Exento SPEI $0.00' : 'Exento SPEI $0.00'
            : isCancel
            ? isEn ? 'No front-desk charge' : 'Sin cargo en puerta'
            : isEn ? 'Settled at reception' : 'Liquidado en recepción',
          accentRgb: [0.0, 0.62, 0.28],
        },
      ],
      detailBlocks: [
        {
          sectionTitle: isEn
            ? 'EXPERIENCE & VAULT TRANSACTION DETAILS'
            : 'DETALLE DE LA EXPERIENCIA Y TRANSACCIÓN EN BÓVEDA',
          lines: [
            { label: isEn ? 'Audit Folio:' : 'Folio de Auditoría:', value: `#${folio}` },
            { label: isEn ? 'Account Athlete:' : 'Atleta Titular:', value: escapeHtmlSafe(athleteName) },
            {
              label: isEn ? 'Affiliated Club / Specialist:' : 'Club / Especialista Afiliado:',
              value: escapeHtmlSafe(reserva.negocio_nombre || venueFallback),
            },
            {
              label: isEn ? 'Sports Experience:' : 'Experiencia Deportiva:',
              value:
                escapeHtmlSafe(reserva.servicio || reserva.nombre_servicio || reserva.titulo_clase) ||
                (isEn ? 'Kinetic Session' : 'Sesión Kinetic'),
            },
            {
              label: isEn ? 'Transaction Status:' : 'Estatus de la Operación:',
              value: statusLabel,
              highlight: true,
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
      footerLegal: isEn
        ? [
            'DIGITAL RECEIPT ISSUED BY KINETICAPP S.A.S. • VALIDATED VIA DYNAMIC TOTP QR PROTOCOL.',
            'For any inquiry regarding this receipt, contact our 24/7 Concierge referencing your Audit Folio.',
          ]
        : [
            'COMPROBANTE DIGITAL EMITIDO POR KINETICAPP S.A.S. • VALIDADO CON PROTOCOLO TOTP QR.',
            'Para cualquier aclaración sobre este ticket, contacta al Concierge 24/7 indicando tu Folio de Auditoría.',
          ],
    }
  );
};

import { SORTED_CATEGORIES } from '../../constants/exploreData';
import { IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO } from '../../types';

export type AdminTxFn = (es: string, en: string) => string;

export const buildTodayIsoDate = (): string => getMazatlanNowParts().isoDate;

export const resolveLocalizedOfficialCoachName = (
  rawName: string | undefined | null,
  tx: AdminTxFn
): string => {
  const trimmed = String(rawName || '').trim();
  if (
    !trimmed ||
    trimmed.toLowerCase() === 'coach oficial kinetic' ||
    trimmed.toLowerCase() === 'official kinetic coach'
  ) {
    return tx('Coach Oficial Kinetic', 'Official Kinetic Coach');
  }
  return trimmed;
};

export const buildDefaultAdminEvent = (tx: AdminTxFn) => ({
  id: null,
  titulo: '',
  nombre_servicio: '',
  categoria: 'Fuerza',
  duracion_minutos: 60,
  frecuencia: 'Única Vez',
  nivel_acceso: 'Bronze',
  fecha: buildTodayIsoDate(),
  hora: '08:00',
  hora_inicio: '08:00',
  cupo_maximo: 12,
  precio_kc: 200,
  instructor_nombre: tx('Coach Oficial Kinetic', 'Official Kinetic Coach'),
  edad_minima: '18+ Adultos',
  requisitos_traer: 'Agua / Hidratación personal, Toalla de entrenamiento',
  ubicacion: '',
  descripcion: '',
  negocio_nombre: 'Kinetic Oficial',
  openInEditMode: true,
});

export const buildEditableAdminEvent = (item: any, tx: AdminTxFn) => {
  const rawDesc = String(item?.descripcion || '');
  const freqMatch = rawDesc.match(/\[Frecuencia Programada:\s*(.*?)\]/i);
  const extractedFreq = item?.frecuencia || freqMatch?.[1]?.trim() || 'Única Vez';
  const cleanDesc = rawDesc.replace(/\[Frecuencia Programada:.*?\]/gi, '').trim();
  const resolvedHora = String(item?.hora_inicio || item?.hora || '08:00').substring(0, 5);
  const resolvedFecha = item?.fecha
    ? String(item.fecha).slice(0, 10)
    : item?.fecha_evento
    ? String(item.fecha_evento).slice(0, 10)
    : buildTodayIsoDate();

  return {
    ...item,
    id: item?.id,
    titulo: item?.nombre_servicio || item?.titulo || '',
    nombre_servicio: item?.nombre_servicio || item?.titulo || '',
    categoria: item?.categoria || item?.disciplina || 'Fuerza',
    duracion_minutos: Number(item?.duracion_minutos) || 60,
    frecuencia: extractedFreq,
    nivel_acceso: item?.nivel_acceso || item?.nivel_requerido || 'Bronze',
    fecha: resolvedFecha,
    hora: resolvedHora,
    hora_inicio: resolvedHora,
    cupo_maximo: Number(item?.cupo_maximo || item?.cupo || 10),
    precio_kc: Number(item?.precio_kc ?? item?.precio ?? 150),
    instructor_nombre: resolveLocalizedOfficialCoachName(item?.instructor_nombre, tx),
    edad_minima: item?.edad_minima || '18+ Adultos',
    requisitos_traer:
      item?.requisitos_traer || 'Agua / Hidratación personal, Toalla de entrenamiento',
    ubicacion: item?.ubicacion || '',
    descripcion: cleanDesc,
    negocio_nombre: item?.negocio_nombre || 'Kinetic Oficial',
    openInEditMode: true,
  };
};

export const normalizeAdminTopDisciplinas = (
  topDisciplinas: any[],
  experienciasActivas: any[],
  solicitudes: any[],
  sociosReales: any[]
) => {
  const serviceCatByName = new Map<string, string>();
  [...(experienciasActivas || []), ...(solicitudes || [])].forEach((s: any) => {
    const k = String(s?.nombre_servicio || s?.titulo || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (k && s?.categoria) serviceCatByName.set(k, String(s.categoria).trim());
  });
  const clubCatByName = new Map<string, string>();
  (sociosReales || []).forEach((af: any) => {
    const k = String(af?.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (k && (af?.disciplina || af?.categoria)) {
      clubCatByName.set(k, String(af.disciplina || af.categoria).trim());
    }
  });
  const validCatMap = new Map<string, string>();
  (SORTED_CATEGORIES || []).forEach((c) => validCatMap.set(c.toLowerCase(), c));

  const grouped = new Map<
    string,
    { count: number; volumenKc: number; completadas: number; clubesSet: Set<string> }
  >();

  (topDisciplinas || []).forEach((d: any) => {
    const raw = String(d?.disciplina || d?.cat || '').trim();
    const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
    let canonical =
      validCatMap.get(lower) ||
      serviceCatByName.get(lower) ||
      (Array.isArray(d?.clubes) &&
        d.clubes[0] &&
        clubCatByName.get(String(d.clubes[0]).toLowerCase())) ||
      '';
    if (!canonical) {
      if (lower.includes('padel') || lower.includes('pádel')) canonical = 'Pádel';
      else if (lower.includes('pilates')) canonical = 'Pilates';
      else if (lower.includes('crossfit') || lower.includes('hyrox')) canonical = 'CrossFit';
      else if (lower.includes('barre')) canonical = 'Barre';
      else if (lower.includes('box')) canonical = 'Boxeo';
      else if (lower.includes('yoga')) canonical = 'Yoga';
      else if (lower.includes('surf')) canonical = 'Surf';
      else canonical = raw || 'Fuerza';
    }
    const prev = grouped.get(canonical) || {
      count: 0,
      volumenKc: 0,
      completadas: 0,
      clubesSet: new Set<string>(),
    };
    const itemCount = Number(d?.reservasCount ?? d?.count) || 0;
    const itemComp = typeof d?.completadas === 'number' ? d.completadas : itemCount;
    prev.count += itemCount;
    prev.volumenKc += Number(d?.volumenKc) || 0;
    prev.completadas += itemComp;
    if (Array.isArray(d?.clubes)) {
      d.clubes.forEach((c: string) => prev.clubesSet.add(c));
    }
    grouped.set(canonical, prev);
  });

  const totalCount = Array.from(grouped.values()).reduce((a, b) => a + b.count, 0);
  return Array.from(grouped.entries())
    .map(([disciplina, info]) => {
      const pct = totalCount > 0 ? Math.round((info.count / totalCount) * 100) : 0;
      const tasaAsistenciaPct =
        info.count > 0 ? Math.round((info.completadas / info.count) * 100) : 100;
      return {
        disciplina,
        cat: disciplina,
        count: info.count,
        reservasCount: info.count,
        volumenKc: info.volumenKc,
        completadas: info.completadas,
        percentage: pct,
        pct,
        tasaAsistenciaPct,
        clubes: Array.from(info.clubesSet),
      };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count || b.volumenKc - a.volumenKc);
};

export const adminPick = (isEn: boolean, es: string, en: string) => (isEn ? en : es);

export const localizeAdminRankName = (rank: string, isEn: boolean): string => {
  if (!isEn) return rank;
  const low = String(rank || '').toLowerCase();
  if (low === 'acceso base') return 'Base Access';
  if (low === 'leyenda vip') return 'VIP Legend';
  return rank;
};

export const patchAffiliateStatusInList = (
  prev: any[],
  afiliado: any,
  nombreAf: string,
  isActivating: boolean,
  targetEstado: 'activo' | 'inactivo'
): any[] =>
  prev.map((item) =>
    (afiliado?.id && String(item?.id) === String(afiliado.id)) ||
    String(item?.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase() === nombreAf.toLowerCase()
      ? {
          ...item,
          ...(afiliado?.whatsapp ? { whatsapp: afiliado.whatsapp } : {}),
          ...(afiliado?.instagram ? { instagram: afiliado.instagram } : {}),
          esClubActivo: isActivating,
          verificado: isActivating,
          estado: targetEstado,
        }
      : item
  );

export const removeAffiliateFromList = (prev: any[], idStr: string, nombreStr: string): any[] =>
  prev.filter(
    (item) =>
      !(idStr && String(item?.id) === idStr) &&
      !(nombreStr && String(item?.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase() === nombreStr.toLowerCase())
  );

export const computeTreasuryPatchedAthletesAndKpis = (
  prevAthletes: any[],
  prevKpis: any,
  atletaId: string,
  res: any
): { nextAthletes: any[]; nextKpis: any } => {
  const nextAthletes = (prevAthletes || []).map((a) => {
    if (String(a?.id) !== String(atletaId)) return a;
    const kcMem = Math.max(0, Number(res?.kc_membresia) || 0);
    const kcTop = Math.max(0, Number(res?.kc_topup) || 0);
    return { ...a, kc_membresia: kcMem, kc_topup: kcTop, saldo_total_kc: kcMem + kcTop, nivel: res?.nivel || a?.nivel };
  });
  if (!prevKpis) return { nextAthletes, nextKpis: prevKpis };
  const sMem = nextAthletes.reduce((acc, item) => acc + (Number(item?.kc_membresia) || 0), 0);
  const sTop = nextAthletes.reduce((acc, item) => acc + (Number(item?.kc_topup) || 0), 0);
  return {
    nextAthletes,
    nextKpis: {
      ...prevKpis,
      sumaMembresia: sMem,
      sumaTopup: sTop,
      saldoDisponibleUsuarios: sMem + sTop,
      masaMonetariaTotal: sMem + sTop + (Number(prevKpis.escrowTotal) || 0),
    },
  };
};

export const patchPreRegistroDecisionInList = (
  prev: any[],
  registroId: string,
  accion: 'aceptar' | 'descartar'
): any[] =>
  accion === 'descartar'
    ? prev.filter((item) => String(item?.id) !== String(registroId))
    : prev.map((item) =>
        String(item?.id) === String(registroId) ? { ...item, estado: 'aceptado', estadoRevision: 'aceptado' } : item
      );

export const resolveTargetB2BCorteReport = (
  customReporte: any,
  reporteCorteSemanal: IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO | null,
  sociosReales?: any[],
  reservasHistoricas?: any[]
): any =>
  customReporte && Array.isArray(customReporte.cortes_por_afiliado)
    ? customReporte
    : reporteCorteSemanal || calcularCorteSemanalB2B(sociosReales || [], reservasHistoricas || []);

export const buildTreasuryMutationAlert = (p: {
  nuevoNivel?: string | null;
  modoOperacion: 'inyectar' | 'retirar' | 'reset';
  billetera: 'membresia' | 'topup';
  signedDelta: number;
  isEn: boolean;
}) => {
  const dispRank = p.nuevoNivel ? localizeAdminRankName(p.nuevoNivel, p.isEn) : '';
  const walletLabel = p.billetera === 'membresia' ? adminPick(p.isEn, 'MEMBRESÍA', 'MEMBERSHIP') : 'TOP-UP';
  const deltaStr = p.signedDelta >= 0 ? `+${p.signedDelta}` : `${p.signedDelta}`;
  return {
    title: p.nuevoNivel
      ? adminPick(p.isEn, `RANGO ACTUALIZADO A ${dispRank.toUpperCase()}`, `VIP TIER UPDATED TO ${dispRank.toUpperCase()}`)
      : adminPick(p.isEn, 'TESORERÍA SINCRONIZADA', 'TREASURY SYNCED'),
    message: p.nuevoNivel
      ? adminPick(p.isEn, `El rango VIP del atleta fue actualizado a ${dispRank} sin alterar sus Kineticoins.`, `The athlete’s VIP tier was updated to ${dispRank} without altering Kineticoins.`)
      : p.modoOperacion === 'reset'
      ? adminPick(p.isEn, `La billetera ${walletLabel} del atleta fue vaciada a 0 KC.`, `The athlete’s ${walletLabel} wallet was reset to 0 KC.`)
      : adminPick(p.isEn, `Se aplicaron ${deltaStr} KC en la billetera ${walletLabel} y se recalcularon las métricas globales.`, `Applied ${deltaStr} KC to the ${walletLabel} wallet and recalculated global metrics.`),
  };
};

export const buildPreRegistroAlert = (registro: any, accion: 'aceptar' | 'descartar', isEn: boolean) => ({

      : adminPick(isEn, `El registro de "${registro.nombre}" fue retirado.`, `The lead "${registro.nombre}" was removed.`),
});

}

const ALLOWED_B2B_SHOWCASE_KEYS = new Set([
  'nombre', 'ubicacion', 'whatsapp', 'horarios', 'disciplina', 'portada_url', 'avatar_url',
]);
const URL_SHOWCASE_KEYS = new Set(['portada_url', 'avatar_url']);
const POISON_KEYS = new Set(['__proto__', 'constructor', 'prototype']);
const RESERVED_OFFICIAL_NAMES = new Set([
  'kinetic oficial', 'kineticapp s.a.s.', 'kineticapp sas', 'kinetic admin',
]);
const MAX_TREASURY_SINGLE_OP_KC = 100_000;
const CLABE_18_RE = /^\d{18}$/;

function isSafeHttpsUrl(candidate: string): boolean {
  if (!candidate || candidate.length > 600 || /\s/.test(candidate)) return false;
  if (/^(javascript|data|vbscript|file|blob):/i.test(candidate)) return false;
  try {
    const parsed = new URL(candidate);
    return parsed.protocol === 'https:' && Boolean(parsed.hostname) && !parsed.username && !parsed.password;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export function sanitizePendingB2BChanges(raw: unknown): Record<string, string> {
  if (raw == null) return {};
  let sourceObj: Record<string, unknown> | null = null;
  if (typeof raw === 'string') {
    const trimmedJson = raw.trim();
    if (!trimmedJson || trimmedJson.length > 16_384 || !trimmedJson.startsWith('{')) return {};
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(trimmedJson);
      if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && !Array.isArray(parsed)) sourceObj = parsed as Record<string, unknown>;
    } catch {
      return {};
    }
  } else if (typeof raw === 'object' && !Array.isArray(raw)) {
    sourceObj = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
  }
  if (!sourceObj) return {};

  const safeResult: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const key of Object.keys(sourceObj)) {
    if (POISON_KEYS.has(key) || !ALLOWED_B2B_SHOWCASE_KEYS.has(key)) continue;
    const rawVal = sourceObj[key];
    if (typeof rawVal !== 'string') continue;

    const cleaned = rawVal.replace(/[\u0000-\u001F\u007F<>]/g, '').trim();
    if (!cleaned || POISON_KEYS.has(cleaned.toLowerCase())) continue;

    if (URL_SHOWCASE_KEYS.has(key)) {
      if (isSafeHttpsUrl(cleaned)) safeResult[key] = cleaned;
      continue;
    }
    if (/^(javascript|data|vbscript):/i.test(cleaned)) continue;
    const bounded = cleaned.slice(0, 240).trim();
    if (!bounded) continue;
    if (key === 'nombre' && RESERVED_OFFICIAL_NAMES.has(bounded.toLowerCase())) continue;
    safeResult[key] = bounded;
  }
  return safeResult;
}

export function computeSafeAdminTreasuryMutation(
  params: ISafeAdminTreasuryMutationParams
): ISafeAdminTreasuryMutationResult {
  const numMem = Number(params?.currentMem);
  const numTop = Number(params?.currentTop);
  const safeMem = Number.isFinite(numMem) ? Math.max(0, Math.round(numMem)) : 0;
  const safeTop = Number.isFinite(numTop) ? Math.max(0, Math.round(numTop)) : 0;

  const wallet = params?.wallet;
  const mode = params?.mode;
  const cleanNivel = typeof params?.nuevoNivel === 'string'
    ? params.nuevoNivel.trim().replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_ -]/g, '').slice(0, 32)
    : '';

  const validWallet = wallet === 'membresia' || wallet === 'topup';
  const validMode = mode === 'inyectar' || mode === 'retirar' || mode === 'reset';
  const auditAction = validWallet && validMode
    ? cleanNivel
      ? `CAMBIO_RANGO_${cleanNivel.toUpperCase().replace(/\s+/g, '_')}`
      : `${mode.toUpperCase()}_${wallet.toUpperCase()}`
    : 'INVALID_TREASURY_OP';

  const failClosed = (): ISafeAdminTreasuryMutationResult => ({
    ok: false, effectiveDelta: 0, projectedMem: safeMem, projectedTop: safeTop,
    projectedTotal: safeMem + safeTop, auditAction,
  });
  if (!validWallet || !validMode) return failClosed();

  const targetBalance = wallet === 'membresia' ? safeMem : safeTop;
  let cleanAmount = 0;

  if (mode === 'reset') {
    if (params.rawDelta !== undefined && params.rawDelta !== null && params.rawDelta !== '') {
      if (typeof params.rawDelta !== 'number' && typeof params.rawDelta !== 'string') return failClosed();
      const parsedReset = Number(params.rawDelta);
      if (!Number.isFinite(parsedReset) || parsedReset < 0 || Math.round(parsedReset) > MAX_TREASURY_SINGLE_OP_KC) {
        return failClosed();
      }
    }
  } else {
    if (
      params.rawDelta === null || params.rawDelta === undefined || typeof params.rawDelta === 'boolean' ||
      (typeof params.rawDelta === 'string' && params.rawDelta.trim() === '')
    ) {
      return failClosed();
    }
    const parsedDelta = Number(params.rawDelta);
    if (!Number.isFinite(parsedDelta) || parsedDelta < 0) return failClosed();
    cleanAmount = Math.round(parsedDelta);
    if (cleanAmount < 0 || cleanAmount > MAX_TREASURY_SINGLE_OP_KC) return failClosed();
  }

  const rawEffective = mode === 'inyectar'
    ? cleanAmount
    : mode === 'retirar'
      ? -Math.min(targetBalance, cleanAmount)
      : -targetBalance;
  const effectiveDelta = rawEffective === 0 ? 0 : rawEffective;

  const projectedMem = wallet === 'membresia' ? Math.max(0, safeMem + effectiveDelta) : safeMem;
  const projectedTop = wallet === 'topup' ? Math.max(0, safeTop + effectiveDelta) : safeTop;
  return {
    ok: true, effectiveDelta, projectedMem, projectedTop,
    projectedTotal: projectedMem + projectedTop, auditAction,
  };
}

export function buildSpeiTransferClipboardPayload(corte: any, isEn = false): ISpeiTransferClipboardPayload {
  const rawClabe = typeof corte?.clabe_bancaria === 'string' ? corte.clabe_bancaria.trim() : '';
  if (!CLABE_18_RE.test(rawClabe)) {
    return { ok: false, clabeClean: '', netAmountFixed: '0.00', clipboardText: '' };
  }
  const rawNet = Number(corte?.neto_dispersar_spei_mxn);
  const netAmountFixed = (Number.isFinite(rawNet) ? Math.max(0, rawNet) : 0).toFixed(2);
  const fallbackPartner = isEn ? 'Kinetic Partner' : 'Socio Kinetic';
  const beneficiario = String(corte?.afiliado_nombre || fallbackPartner).replace(/[\r\n|<>]/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 72) || fallbackPartner;
  const refDate = String(corte?.periodo_fin || corte?.periodo_inicio || '').replace(/[^0-9-]/g, '').slice(0, 10);
  const folioRef = refDate ? `KNT-B2B-${refDate}` : 'KNT-B2B-SPEI';
  const clipboardText = isEn
    ? `CLABE: ${rawClabe} | Beneficiary: ${beneficiario} | Net MXN: $${netAmountFixed} | Concept: ${folioRef}`
    : `CLABE: ${rawClabe} | Beneficiario: ${beneficiario} | Neto MXN: $${netAmountFixed} | Concepto: ${folioRef}`;
  return { ok: true, clabeClean: rawClabe, netAmountFixed, clipboardText };
}

export function buildPartnerSpeiWhatsappUrl(params: IPartnerSpeiWhatsappParams): string | null {
  const rawPhone = typeof params?.whatsappRaw === 'string' || typeof params?.whatsappRaw === 'number'
    ? String(params.whatsappRaw).replace(/\D/g, '')
    : '';
  if (rawPhone.length < 10 || rawPhone.length > 15 || /^0+$/.test(rawPhone)) return null;
  const phoneDigits = rawPhone.length === 10 ? `52${rawPhone}` : rawPhone;
  if (!/^\d{11,15}$/.test(phoneDigits)) return null;

  const isEn = Boolean(params?.isEn);
  const safeMoney = (v?: number) => (Number.isFinite(Number(v)) ? Math.max(0, Number(v)).toFixed(2) : '0.00');
  const partner = String(params?.partnerName || (isEn ? 'Affiliate Partner' : 'Socio Afiliado'))
    .replace(/[\r\n<>]/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 72) || (isEn ? 'Affiliate Partner' : 'Socio Afiliado');
  const pIni = String(params?.periodoInicio || '').replace(/[^0-9A-Za-z ./-]/g, '').trim().slice(0, 20);
  const pFin = String(params?.periodoFin || '').replace(/[^0-9A-Za-z ./-]/g, '').trim().slice(0, 20);
  const periodLabel = pIni && pFin ? `${pIni} - ${pFin}` : pFin || pIni || (isEn ? 'Weekly Cut' : 'Corte Semanal');
  const bruto = safeMoney(params?.brutoMxn);
  const retSat = safeMoney(params?.retencionSatMxn);
  const netoSpei = safeMoney(params?.netoSpeiMxn);
  const msg = isEn
    ? `Hello ${partner}, KineticApp S.A.S. notifies your B2B SPEI settlement (${periodLabel}): Gross 100% $${bruto} MXN | SAT Withholding $${retSat} MXN | Net SPEI Transferred $${netoSpei} MXN.`
    : `Hola ${partner}, KineticApp S.A.S. notifica tu liquidación SPEI B2B (${periodLabel}): Bruto 100% $${bruto} MXN | Retención SAT $${retSat} MXN | Neto SPEI Transferido $${netoSpei} MXN.`;
  return `https://wa.me/${phoneDigits}?text=${encodeURIComponent(msg)}`;
}

export async function fetchResumenPasesMaestrosRpc(
  p_negocio_nombre?: string | null
): Promise<IResumenPasesMaestros> {
  const cleanNombre =
    typeof p_negocio_nombre === 'string' && p_negocio_nombre.trim()
      ? p_negocio_nombre.trim()
      : null;
  try {
    const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('obtener_resumen_pases_maestros', {
      p_negocio_nombre: cleanNombre,
    });
    if (error || !data) {
      return { dia: 0, semana: 0, mes: 0, source: 'fallback' };
    }
    const row = Array.isArray(data) ? data[0] : data;
    if (!row || typeof row !== 'object') {
      return { dia: 0, semana: 0, mes: 0, source: 'fallback' };
    }
    return {
      dia: Math.max(0, Number((row as any).dia ?? (row as any).pases_dia ?? (row as any).hoy ?? 0) || 0),
      semana: Math.max(0, Number((row as any).semana ?? (row as any).pases_semana ?? 0) || 0),
      mes: Math.max(0, Number((row as any).mes ?? (row as any).pases_mes ?? 0) || 0),
      source: 'rpc',
    };
  } catch {
    return { dia: 0, semana: 0, mes: 0, source: 'fallback' };
  }
}

import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase';
import { emitKineticRadarUpdated } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';

  WALL_COLS_FULL,
  WALL_COLS_LEGACY,
  getMemoryWallSnapshot,

  purgePostFromMemoryWallSnapshot,

  publishStoryEventToCloud,

  syncStoriesFromCloud,

const WALL_POSTS_LOCAL_KEY = '@kinetic_real_wall_posts_v26';
const LEGACY_WALL_POSTS_KEYS = ['@kinetic_social_feed_posts_v25', '@kinetic_community_feed_cache'];
const UGC_REPORT_OVERRIDES_KEY = '@kinetic_ugc_report_status_overrides_v1';

export interface IKinetiClubModerationSnapshot {
  posts: any[];
  stories: any[];
  teams: any[];
  reports: any[];
}

let _moderationSwrCache: IKinetiClubModerationSnapshot | null = null;

const cleanTeamLabel = (raw?: string | null, fallback = 'Equipo Kinetic'): string => {
  const cleaned = String(raw || '')
    .replace(/\bescuadr[oó]n\b/gi, 'Equipo')
    .replace(/\s+(?:en|in)\s+los\s+cabos\.?/gi, '')
    .replace(/\s*[•|-]\s*los\s+cabos\.?/gi, '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();
  return cleaned || fallback;
};

const resolvePostMediaThumb = (p: any): string | null => {
  const direct = String(p?.imagen_url || p?.image_url || '').trim();
  if (/^(?:https?:\/\/|data:image\/)/i.test(direct)) return direct;
  const arr = Array.isArray(p?.galeria) ? p.galeria : Array.isArray(p?.mediaUris) ? p.mediaUris : [];
  const first = arr.find((u: any) => typeof u === 'string' && /^(?:https?:\/\/|data:image\/)/i.test(u.trim()));
  return first ? String(first).trim() : null;
};

export function getInitialKinetiClubModerationSnapshot(): IKinetiClubModerationSnapshot {
  if (_moderationSwrCache) return _moderationSwrCache;
  const memPosts = (getMemoryWallSnapshot().posts || [])
    .filter((p: any) => p?.id && !isPrivateOrSystemChannelRow(p) && !isOffTopicGamingPost(p))
    .map((p: any) => ({
      ...p,
      id: String(p.id),
      autor_nombre: p.authorName || p.autor_nombre || 'Atleta Kinetic',
      contenido: String(p.bodyEs || p.bodyEn || p.contenido || p.caption || '').trim() || 'Publicación multimedia en KinetiClub.',
      disciplina: p.discipline || p.disciplina || 'Comunidad',
      imagen_url: resolvePostMediaThumb(p),
      created_at: p.created_at || p.createdAt || '',
    }));
  return { posts: memPosts, stories: [], teams: [], reports: [] };
}

async function readUgcOverrides(): Promise<Record<string, string>> {
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(UGC_REPORT_OVERRIDES_KEY);
    const parsed = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
    return parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' ? parsed : {};
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

async function writeUgcOverride(reportId: string, estado: string): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const current = await readUgcOverrides();
    current[reportId] = estado;
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(UGC_REPORT_OVERRIDES_KEY, JSON.stringify(current));
  } catch {}
}

export function isPrivateOrSystemChannelRow(row: any): boolean {
  if (!row) return true;
  const codeLower = String(row.codigo_invitacion || row.canal || row.channel || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const name = String(row.nombre || row.name || '').trim();
  const nameLower = name.toLowerCase();
  const disc = String(row.disciplina || row.tipo || '').trim().toLowerCase();

  return (
    codeLower.startsWith('dm-') ||
    codeLower.startsWith('dm__') ||
    codeLower === 'stories-v25' ||
    nameLower.startsWith('dm__') ||
    nameLower.startsWith('dm-') ||
    name.startsWith('{') ||
    disc === 'stories_24h' ||
    disc === 'dm' ||
    disc === 'privado_1a1' ||
    Boolean(row.es_mensaje_directo)
  );
}

const SQUAD_ADMIN_MOD_COLS =
  'id, nombre, creador_id, creador_nombre, disciplina, cupo_maximo, codigo_invitacion, created_at';

async function fetchSafeModerationPosts(limit = 80) {
  try {
    const channelFilter = 'disciplina.is.null,and(disciplina.neq.stories_24h,disciplina.neq.dm,disciplina.neq.privado_1a1)';
    const r1 = await supabase
      .from('comunidad_posts')
      .select(WALL_COLS_FULL)
      .or(channelFilter)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(limit);
    if (!r1.error && Array.isArray(r1.data)) return r1.data;
    const r2 = await supabase
      .from('comunidad_posts')
      .select(WALL_COLS_LEGACY)
      .or(channelFilter)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(limit);
    return !r2.error && Array.isArray(r2.data) ? r2.data : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

async function fetchSafeModerationTeams(limit = 100) {
  try {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('equipos_kinetic')
      .select(SQUAD_ADMIN_MOD_COLS)
      .or('codigo_invitacion.is.null,and(codigo_invitacion.neq.STORIES-V25,codigo_invitacion.not.like.dm-%,codigo_invitacion.not.like.dm__%)')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(limit);
    return !error && Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export async function fetchKinetiClubModerationData(): Promise<{
  posts: any[];
  stories: any[];
  teams: any[];
  reports: any[];
}> {
  const safeUgcReports = async (): Promise<any[]> => {
    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('comunidad_reportes_ugc')
        .select('id, reporter_id, target_user_id, target_post_id, tipo_contenido, categoria_motivo, detalle, estado, created_at')
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
        .limit(80);
      return !error && Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  };

  const [
    comunidadPosts, storiesCloudMap, equiposKineticTbl, equipoMiembrosTbl,
    localWallRaw, ugcReportsRows, ugcOverrides,
  ] = await Promise.all([
    fetchSafeModerationPosts(80),
    syncStoriesFromCloud(true).catch(() => ({} as Record<string, any[]>)),
    fetchSafeModerationTeams(100),
    supabase.from('equipo_miembros').select('equipo_id, estado').limit(400).then((r) => (!r.error && Array.isArray(r.data) ? r.data : []), () => []),
    AsyncStorage.getItem(WALL_POSTS_LOCAL_KEY).catch(() => null),
    safeUgcReports(),
    readUgcOverrides(),
  ]);

  let localWallPosts: any[] = [];
  if (localWallRaw) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(localWallRaw);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
        const cleanLocal = parsed.filter((p: any) => p && !isOffTopicGamingPost(p));
        if (cleanLocal.length !== parsed.length) {
          AsyncStorage.setItem(WALL_POSTS_LOCAL_KEY, JSON.stringify(cleanLocal)).catch(() => {});
        }
        localWallPosts = cleanLocal;
      }
    } catch {}
  }

  const postsMap = new Map<string, any>();
  [...comunidadPosts, ...localWallPosts].forEach((p: any) => {
    if (!p?.id || isPrivateOrSystemChannelRow(p) || isOffTopicGamingPost(p)) return;
    const text = String(p.contenido || p.texto || p.caption || p.bodyEs || p.bodyEn || p.mensaje || '').trim();
    if (text.startsWith('__KINETIC_') || isOffTopicGamingPost(text)) return;
    postsMap.set(String(p.id), {
      ...p,
      id: String(p.id),
      autor_nombre: p.autor_nombre || p.usuario_nombre || p.authorName || p.autor || 'Atleta Kinetic',
      contenido: text || 'Publicación multimedia en el Muro KinetiClub.',
      disciplina: p.disciplina || p.tag || p.categoria || 'Comunidad',
      imagen_url: resolvePostMediaThumb(p),
      created_at: p.created_at || p.createdAt || p.fecha || '',
    });
  });

  const storiesMap = new Map<string, any>();
  Object.values(storiesCloudMap || {}).forEach((arr: any) => {
    if (!Array.isArray(arr)) return;
    arr.forEach((s: any) => {
      if (!s?.id) return;
      storiesMap.set(String(s.id), {
        id: String(s.id),
        ownerKey: s.ownerKey || '',
        autor_nombre: s.ownerName || 'Atleta Kinetic',
        contenido: s.caption || s.tag || 'Historia efímera activa (24h)',
        disciplina: s.tag || 'STORY 24H',
        imageUrl: s.imageUrl || '',
        created_at: s.createdAtMs ? new Date(Number(s.createdAtMs)).toISOString() : '',
      });
    });
  });

  const membersCountByTeam = new Map<string, number>();
  (equipoMiembrosTbl || []).forEach((m: any) => {
    const eid = String(m?.equipo_id || '').trim();
    const mst = String(m?.estado || 'aceptado').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (!eid || mst === 'rechazado' || mst === 'eliminado') return;
    membersCountByTeam.set(eid, (membersCountByTeam.get(eid) || 0) + 1);
  });

  const teamsMap = new Map<string, any>();
  (equiposKineticTbl || []).forEach((t: any) => {
    if (!t?.id || isPrivateOrSystemChannelRow(t)) return;
    const tid = String(t.id);
    const cleanedName = cleanTeamLabel(t.nombre, 'Equipo Kinetic');
    const cleanedDesc = cleanTeamLabel(
      t.descripcion,
      t.codigo_invitacion ? `Código de acceso: ${t.codigo_invitacion}` : 'Equipo deportivo activo en KinetiClub.'
    );
    teamsMap.set(tid, {
      ...t,
      id: tid,
      nombre: cleanedName,
      disciplina: t.disciplina || t.categoria || 'Fuerza',
      creador_nombre: t.creador_nombre || t.lider_nombre || 'Capitán Kinetic',
      miembros_count: Math.max(1, membersCountByTeam.get(tid) || Number(t.miembros_count) || 1),
      max_miembros: Math.max(2, Number(t.cupo_maximo || t.max_miembros) || 20),
      avatar_url: t.avatar_url || t.icono_url || null,
      descripcion: cleanedDesc,
    });
  });

  const rawReports = Array.isArray(ugcReportsRows) ? ugcReportsRows : [];
  const profileIds = Array.from(
    new Set(
      rawReports
        .flatMap((r: any) => [r?.reporter_id, r?.target_user_id])
        .map((id) => String(id || '').trim())
        .filter((id) => id.length >= 16)
    )
  );
  const namesMap: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (profileIds.length > 0) {
    try {
      const { data: pubRows } = await supabase
        .from('perfiles_publicos')
        .select('id, nombre')
        .in('id', profileIds);
      (pubRows || []).forEach((p: any) => {
        if (p?.id && p?.nombre) {
          namesMap[String(p.id)] = String(p.nombre).trim();
        }
      });
    } catch {}
    const missingIds = profileIds.filter((id) => !namesMap[id]);
    if (missingIds.length > 0) {
      try {
        const { data: perfRows } = await supabase
          .from('perfiles')
          .select('id, nombre')
          .in('id', missingIds);
        (perfRows || []).forEach((p: any) => {
          if (p?.id && p?.nombre) {
            namesMap[String(p.id)] = String(p.nombre).trim();
          }
        });
      } catch {}
    }
  }

  const reports = rawReports.map((r) => {
    const rid = String(r?.id || '').trim();
    const dbEstado = String(r?.estado || 'pendiente').toLowerCase().trim();
    const override = rid && dbEstado === 'pendiente' ? ugcOverrides[rid] : undefined;
    return {
      ...r,
      estado: override || dbEstado || 'pendiente',
      reporter_nombre: namesMap[String(r?.reporter_id || '')] || r?.reporter_nombre || 'Atleta Kinetic',
      target_nombre: namesMap[String(r?.target_user_id || '')] || r?.target_nombre || 'Atleta Kinetic',
    };
  });

  const snapshot: IKinetiClubModerationSnapshot = {
    posts: Array.from(postsMap.values()),
    stories: Array.from(storiesMap.values()),
    teams: Array.from(teamsMap.values()),
    reports,
  };
  _moderationSwrCache = snapshot;
  return snapshot;
}

export async function fetchPendingUgcReportsCount(): Promise<number> {
  try {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('comunidad_reportes_ugc')
      .select('id, estado')
      .limit(120);
    if (error || !Array.isArray(data)) return 0;
    return data.filter(
      (row) => String(row?.estado || 'pendiente').toLowerCase().trim() === 'pendiente'
    ).length;
  } catch {
    return 0;
  }
}

export async function updateKinetiClubUgcReportStatus(
  reportId: string,
  estado: 'revisado' | 'accion_tomada' | 'descartado'
): Promise<boolean> {
  const cleanId = String(reportId || '').trim();
  if (!cleanId) return false;
  let persisted = false;
  try {
    const { data: sess } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
    if (!sess?.session?.user?.id) return false;
    const { error: adminRpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('admin_resolver_reporte_ugc', {
      p_reporte_id: cleanId,
      p_nuevo_estado: estado,
    });
    if (!adminRpcErr) {
      persisted = true;
    } else {
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('comunidad_reportes_ugc')
        .update({ estado })
        .eq('id', cleanId);
      if (!error) {
        persisted = true;
      } else {
        const modRes = await supabase.rpc('moderar_reporte_ugc', { p_reporte_id: cleanId, p_estado: estado });
        if (!modRes.error) {
          persisted = true;
        } else {
          const resRpc = await supabase.rpc('resolver_reporte_ugc', { p_report_id: cleanId, p_estado: estado });
          if (!resRpc.error) persisted = true;
        }
      }
    }
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
  if (!persisted) return false;
  await writeUgcOverride(cleanId, estado);
  if (_moderationSwrCache) {
    _moderationSwrCache = {
      ..._moderationSwrCache,
      reports: _moderationSwrCache.reports.map((r) => (String(r?.id) === cleanId ? { ...r, estado } : r)),
    };
  }
  emitKineticRadarUpdated();
  return true;
}

export async function deleteKinetiClubPost(postId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const cleanId = String(postId || '').trim();
  if (!cleanId) return false;
  let deleted = false;
  try {
    const { error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('eliminar_post_comunidad_admin', { p_post_id: cleanId });
    if (!rpcErr) deleted = true;
  } catch {}
  if (!deleted) {
    await supabase.from('comunidad_posts').delete().eq('id', cleanId).then(() => {}, () => {});
  }
  purgePostFromMemoryWallSnapshot(cleanId);
  if (_moderationSwrCache) {
    _moderationSwrCache = {
      ..._moderationSwrCache,
      posts: _moderationSwrCache.posts.filter((p) => String(p?.id) !== cleanId && !isOffTopicGamingPost(p)),
    };
  }
  for (const key of [WALL_POSTS_LOCAL_KEY, ...LEGACY_WALL_POSTS_KEYS]) {
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(key);
      if (!raw) continue;
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
        await AsyncStorage.setItem(
          key,
          JSON.stringify(parsed.filter((p: any) => String(p?.id) !== cleanId && !isOffTopicGamingPost(p)))
        );
      }
    } catch {}
  }
  emitKineticRadarUpdated();
  return true;
}

export async function deleteKinetiClubStory(
  storyId: string,
  ownerKey?: string,
  ownerName?: string
): Promise<boolean> {
  const cleanId = String(storyId || '').trim();
  if (!cleanId) return false;
  try {
    const map = await readLocalStoriesMap();
    const nextMap: Record<string, any[]> = {};
    Object.keys(map || {}).forEach((k) => {
      const remaining = (map[k] || []).filter((s) => String(s?.id) !== cleanId);
      if (remaining.length > 0) nextMap[k] = remaining;
    });
    await writeLocalStoriesMap(nextMap);
  } catch {}
  await Promise.allSettled([
    publishStoryEventToCloud({
      type: 'DELETE_STORY',
      storyId: cleanId,
      ownerKey: ownerKey || ownerName || 'kinetic',
      ownerName: ownerName || 'Kinetic Oficial',
    }),
    supabase.from('kinetic_stories').delete().eq('id', cleanId),
    supabase.from('historias_club').delete().eq('id', cleanId),
    supabase.from('historias_24h').delete().eq('id', cleanId),
  ]);
  return true;
}

export async function deleteKinetiClubTeam(teamId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const cleanId = String(teamId || '').trim();
  if (!cleanId) return false;
  await Promise.allSettled([
    supabase.from('equipo_mensajes').delete().eq('equipo_id', cleanId),
    supabase.from('equipo_miembros').delete().eq('equipo_id', cleanId),
    supabase.from('equipos_kinetic').delete().eq('id', cleanId),
    supabase.from('equipos').delete().eq('id', cleanId),
    supabase.from('club_squads').delete().eq('id', cleanId),
  ]);
  return true;
}

    FILE: src/services/admin/adminWaitlistService.ts

    // === src/services/admin/adminWaitlistService.ts === //
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase';

const WAITLIST_STATUS_STORAGE_KEY = '@kinetic_waitlist_status_map_v1';

export type WaitlistReviewState = 'pendiente' | 'aceptado' | 'descartado';

export interface IWaitlistPreRegistro {
  id: string;
  nombre: string;
  email: string;
  telefono: string;
  tipo_perfil: string;
  esSocioB2B: boolean;
  esEspecialistaB2B: boolean;
  ciudad_zona: string;
  disciplina_interes: string;
  origen: string;
  estado: 'pendiente' | 'aceptado' | 'descartado';
  estadoRevision: WaitlistReviewState;
  created_at: string | null;
  afiliado_id?: string | null;
  cambios_pendientes_b2b?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
}

function parsePendingB2BPayload(raw: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  if (!raw) return null;
  if (typeof raw === 'object' && !Array.isArray(raw)) return raw as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (typeof raw === 'string' && raw.trim().startsWith('{')) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      return parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && !Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

const B2B_RE = /\b(socio|b2b|club|estudio|especialista|consultorio|partner|clinic|studio|clinica|clínica)\b/i;
const SPEC_RE = /\b(especialista|consultorio|fisioterap|nutri|medico|médico|specialist|clinic|clínica)\b/i;

async function readLocalStatusMap(): Promise<Record<string, WaitlistReviewState>> {
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(WAITLIST_STATUS_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return {};
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' ? parsed : {};
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

async function writeLocalStatus(id: string, status: WaitlistReviewState): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const map = await readLocalStatusMap();
    map[String(id)] = status;
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(WAITLIST_STATUS_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(map));
  } catch {
    // Silencioso en caso de cuota de almacenamiento llena
  }
}

function resolveRowStatus(row: any, localMap: Record<string, WaitlistReviewState>): WaitlistReviewState {
  const rawEstado = String(row?.estado || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (rawEstado === 'aceptado' || rawEstado === 'aprobado') return 'aceptado';
  if (rawEstado === 'descartado' || rawEstado === 'rechazado') return 'descartado';
  if (rawEstado === 'pendiente') {
    const idStr = String(row?.id || '');
    if (idStr && localMap[idStr] === 'aceptado') return 'aceptado';
    if (idStr && localMap[idStr] === 'descartado') return 'descartado';
    return 'pendiente';
  }
  const idStr = String(row?.id || '');
  if (idStr && localMap[idStr]) return localMap[idStr];
  const rawOrigen = String(row?.origen || '').toLowerCase();
  if (rawOrigen.includes('descartado')) return 'descartado';
  if (rawOrigen.includes('aceptado')) return 'aceptado';
  return 'pendiente';
}

export async function fetchWaitlistPreRegistros(): Promise<IWaitlistPreRegistro[]> {
  try {
    const [res, localMap] = await Promise.all([
      supabase
        .from('fundadores_waitlist')
        .select('*')
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
        .limit(80),
      readLocalStatusMap(),
    ]);

    const rows = Array.isArray(res?.data) ? res.data : [];
    return rows
      .map((row: any, idx: number): IWaitlistPreRegistro => {
        const id = String(row?.id || `waitlist_${idx}_${row?.email || ''}`);
        const tipoPerfil = String(
          row?.tipo_perfil || row?.perfil_interes || 'Atleta Acceso Prioritario'
        ).trim();
        const rawOrigen = String(row?.origen || 'kineticapp.com.mx')
          .replace(/\s*•\s*(aceptado|descartado)/gi, '')
          .trim();
        const resolvedStatus = resolveRowStatus(row, localMap);
        return {
          id,
          nombre: String(row?.nombre || 'Registro Web').trim(),
          email: String(row?.email || '').trim(),
          telefono: String(row?.telefono || row?.whatsapp || '').trim(),
          tipo_perfil: tipoPerfil,
          esSocioB2B: B2B_RE.test(tipoPerfil),
          esEspecialistaB2B: SPEC_RE.test(tipoPerfil),
          ciudad_zona: String(row?.ciudad_zona || row?.zona || '').trim(),
          disciplina_interes: String(
            row?.disciplina_interes || row?.disciplina_favorita || ''
          ).trim(),
          origen: rawOrigen || 'kineticapp.com.mx',
          estado: resolvedStatus,
          estadoRevision: resolvedStatus,
          created_at: row?.created_at ? String(row.created_at) : null,
          afiliado_id: row?.afiliado_id ? String(row.afiliado_id).trim() : null,
          cambios_pendientes_b2b: parsePendingB2BPayload(row?.cambios_pendientes_b2b),
        };
      })
      .filter((item) => item.estadoRevision !== 'descartado');
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export async function resolverEstadoPreRegistro(
  registro: IWaitlistPreRegistro,
  accion: 'aceptar' | 'descartar',
  onRegisterProspectB2B?: (payload: any) => Promise<any>
): Promise<boolean> {
  const id = String(registro?.id || '').trim();
  if (!id) return false;
  const nextStatus: WaitlistReviewState = accion === 'aceptar' ? 'aceptado' : 'descartado';

  const cleanBaseOrigen = String(registro.origen || 'kineticapp.com.mx')
    .replace(/\s*•\s*(aceptado|descartado)/gi, '')
    .trim();

  if (accion === 'descartar') {
    const { error: delErr } = await supabase
      .from('fundadores_waitlist')
      .delete()
      .eq('id', id);
    if (delErr) {
      await supabase
        .from('fundadores_waitlist')
        .update({ estado: 'descartado' })
        .eq('id', id);
    }
    await writeLocalStatus(id, 'descartado');
  } else {
    const { data: updData, error: updErr } = await supabase
      .from('fundadores_waitlist')
      .update({ estado: 'aceptado' })
      .eq('id', id)
      .select('id');
    if (updErr || !updData || updData.length === 0) {
      await supabase
        .from('fundadores_waitlist')
        .update({ origen: `${cleanBaseOrigen} • aceptado` })
        .eq('id', id);
    }
    await writeLocalStatus(id, 'aceptado');

    if (registro.esSocioB2B && typeof onRegisterProspectB2B === 'function') {
      try {
        await onRegisterProspectB2B({
          nombre: registro.nombre,
          disciplina: registro.disciplina_interes || 'Fuerza',
          es_especialista: registro.esEspecialistaB2B,
          ubicacion: registro.ciudad_zona || 'Ubicación por confirmar',
          whatsapp: registro.telefono,
          activo: false,
        });
      } catch {}
    }
  }

  return true;
}

import {
  ICorteSemanalAfiliado,
  IDesgloseReservaCorte,
  IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO,
  RegimenFiscalSAT,
} from '../types';
import { getMazatlanWeekAndDayBounds, parseMazatlanTimestampMs } from './kineticVenueCanonicalGeoAndHours';

const ADMIN_OR_B2B_ROLES = new Set([
  'admin', 'fundador', 'ceo', 'socio', 'especialista', 'club', 'afiliado',
  'partner b2b', 'partner_b2b', 'negocio', 'estadio', 'operador', 'b2b',
]);

const OFFICIAL_ACCOUNT_NAMES = new Set([
  'kinetic oficial', 'kinetic sports club', 'kinetic especialista',
  'kinetic admin', 'kineticapp s.a.s.', 'kineticapp sas',
]);

export const isAdminOrB2BAccount = (p: any): boolean => {
  if (!p) return false;
  const email = String(p.email || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const rol = String(p.rol || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const tipoCuenta = String(p.tipo_cuenta || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const nombre = String(p.nombre || '').toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();

  if (
    email === 'administrador@kinetic.com.mx' || email === 'administrador@kinetic.com' ||
    email === 'kinetic@kinetic.com' || email === 'admin@kinetic.com' ||
    email.startsWith('administrador@') || email.startsWith('club@') || email.startsWith('especialista@')
  ) {
    return true;
  }
  return ADMIN_OR_B2B_ROLES.has(rol) || ADMIN_OR_B2B_ROLES.has(tipoCuenta) || OFFICIAL_ACCOUNT_NAMES.has(nombre);
};

export const isMasterPass0Kc = (r: any): boolean => {
  if (!r) return false;
  if (r.es_pase_maestro === true || r.is_master_pass === true || r.pase_maestro === true) return true;
  const tipo = String(r.tipo_pase || r.tipo_reserva || r.origen || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (tipo.includes('pase_maestro') || tipo.includes('master_pass')) return true;
  const precioTotal = Math.max(
    Number(r.costo_total) || 0, Number(r.precio_kc) || 0, Number(r.precio_total_kc) || 0,
    (Number(r.monto_escrow) || 0) + (Number(r.monto_puerta) || 0)
  );
  return precioTotal <= 0;
};

const SANDBOX_REVIEWER_EMAILS = new Set([
  'apple.tester@kineticapp.com.mx',
  'google.tester@kineticapp.com.mx',
  'reviewer@kineticapp.com.mx',
  'sandbox@kineticapp.com.mx',
]);

export const isSandboxAccountOrReservation = (r: any, perfilOverride?: any): boolean => {
  if (!r && !perfilOverride) return false;
  const perfil = perfilOverride || r?.perfiles || r?.perfil || {};
  if (
    r?.es_cuenta_sandbox === true || r?.is_sandbox === true || r?.sandbox === true ||
    r?.es_demo === true || r?.is_demo === true || r?.is_test === true ||
    perfil?.es_cuenta_sandbox === true || perfil?.is_sandbox === true || perfil?.sandbox === true ||
    perfil?.es_demo === true || perfil?.is_demo === true || perfil?.is_test === true
  ) {
    return true;
  }
  const roleStr = String(r?.atleta_rol || perfil?.rol || perfil?.tipo_cuenta || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (roleStr === 'sandbox' || roleStr === 'demo' || roleStr === 'test' || roleStr === 'qa' || roleStr === 'tester') {
    return true;
  }
  const emailCandidates = [r?.email, r?.atleta_email, r?.usuario_email, perfil?.email]
    .map((v) => String(v || '').toLowerCase().trim())
    .filter(Boolean);
  if (
    emailCandidates.some(
      (em) =>
        SANDBOX_REVIEWER_EMAILS.has(em) ||
        em.endsWith('@sandbox.kineticapp.com.mx') ||
        em.startsWith('apple.tester@') ||
        em.startsWith('google.tester@') ||
        em.startsWith('sandbox.') ||
        /^(sandbox|demo|test|qa|tester)([._+@]|-|$)/.test(em) ||
        /[.+_-](sandbox|demo|qa|test|tester)@/.test(em) ||
        /\+(sandbox|test|demo|qa)@/.test(em)
    )
  ) {
    return true;
  }
  const originStr = String(r?.tipo_pase || r?.tipo_reserva || r?.origen || r?.canal_origen || '')
    .toLowerCase()
    .trim();
  if (originStr.includes('sandbox') || originStr.includes('app_review') || originStr.includes('appreview')) {
    return true;
  }
  const paymentRef = String(r?.stripe_payment_intent_id || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  return paymentRef.startsWith('sandbox_') || paymentRef.startsWith('test_') || paymentRef.startsWith('demo_');
};

const MASTER_PASS_COUNT_STATUSES = new Set(['completada', 'activa', 'confirmada', 'pendiente']);

export const isOrphanEscrowReservation = (
  r: any,
  validServiceIds?: Set<string>,
  validServiceNames?: Set<string>
): boolean => {
  if (!r) return true;
  const st = String(r.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (st === 'cancelada' || st === 'rechazada' || st === 'eliminada') {
    return true;
  }
  // Solo validamos orfandad contra catálogo vigente en reservas activas/pendientes/confirmadas
  // (las reservas completadas/no_show históricas conservan su trazabilidad contable SPEI)
  const isActiveCustody = st === 'activa' || st === 'pendiente' || st === 'confirmada';
  if (!isActiveCustody) return false;
  if (!validServiceIds || validServiceIds.size === 0) return false;

  const sid = String(r.servicio_id || '').trim();
  if (sid) {
    return !validServiceIds.has(sid);
  }
  if (validServiceNames && validServiceNames.size > 0) {
    const candidateNames = [r.servicio, r.nombre_servicio, r.titulo_clase]
      .map((x) => String(x || '').trim().toLowerCase())
      .filter(Boolean);
    if (candidateNames.length === 0) return true;
    return !candidateNames.some((n) => validServiceNames.has(n));
  }
  return false;
};

  if (clean.includes('baja fitness') && clean.includes('medano')) return 'baja fitness medano';
  if (clean === 'palestra club' || clean.includes('palesttra')) return 'palesttra club';

  if (clean.includes('casa muv')) return 'casa muv studio';
  if (clean.includes('cabo yoga')) return 'cabo yoga & fitness center';

  if (clean.includes('sukha yoga')) return 'sukha yoga studio';

  if (clean.includes('seal cross')) return 'seal crosstraining';
  if (clean.includes('el tule fitness')) return 'el tule fitness & crossfit';
  if (clean.includes('f45')) return 'f45 training san jose del cabo';
  if (clean.includes('40-0') || clean.includes('40 0')) return '40-0 padel club';
  if (clean.includes('cardon padel')) return 'cardon padel club';

  if (clean.includes('cabo sports massage')) return 'cabo sports massage & recovery studio';
  return clean;
};

export interface IB2BPeriodOverride {
  startMs: number;
  endMs: number;
  inicioIso: string;
  finIso: string;
  strictWindow?: boolean;
}

const parseSafeTimestampMs = (rawDate: unknown): number => parseMazatlanTimestampMs(rawDate);

export interface ICorteSemanalAfiliadoConPases extends ICorteSemanalAfiliado {
  pases_maestros_dia: number;
  pases_maestros_semana: number;
  pases_maestros_mes: number;
}

export interface IReporteMaestroConPases extends IReporteMaestroSemanalCEO {
  periodoInicio: string;
  periodoFin: string;
  total_pases_maestros_dia: number;
  total_pases_maestros_semana: number;
  total_pases_maestros_mes: number;
  cortes_por_afiliado: ICorteSemanalAfiliadoConPases[];
}

export const calcularCorteSemanalB2B = (
  afiliados: any[],
  reservas: any[],
  periodOverride?: IB2BPeriodOverride,
  validServiceIds?: Set<string>,
  validServiceNames?: Set<string>
): IReporteMaestroConPases => {
  const nonOrphanReservas = (reservas || []).filter(
    (r: any) => !isOrphanEscrowReservation(r, validServiceIds, validServiceNames)
  );
  const cleanReservas = nonOrphanReservas.filter(
    (r: any) =>
      !isMasterPass0Kc(r) &&
      !isSandboxAccountOrReservation(r) &&
      !isAdminOrB2BAccount(
        r?.perfiles || { nombre: r?.atleta_nombre, email: r?.atleta_email || r?.email, rol: r?.atleta_rol }
      )
  );
  const masterPassReservas = nonOrphanReservas.filter((r: any) => {
    if (!isMasterPass0Kc(r) || isSandboxAccountOrReservation(r)) return false;
    const st = String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    return !st || MASTER_PASS_COUNT_STATUSES.has(st);
  });
  const now = new Date();
  const mz = getMazatlanWeekAndDayBounds(now);
  const dayStartMs = mz.startOfTodayMs;
  const dayEndMs = mz.endOfTodayMs;
  const monthStartMs = mz.monthStartMs;
  const monthEndMs = mz.monthEndMs;
  const startMs = periodOverride ? periodOverride.startMs : mz.startMs;
  const endMs = periodOverride ? periodOverride.endMs : mz.endMs;
  const periodoInicio = periodOverride ? periodOverride.inicioIso : mz.periodoInicio;
  const periodoFin = periodOverride ? periodOverride.finIso : mz.periodoFin;
  const strictWindow = Boolean(periodOverride?.strictWindow);

  const hasWindowTransactions = strictWindow
    ? true
    : cleanReservas.some((r: any) => {
        const st = String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
        if (!['completada', 'no_show', 'penalizada'].includes(st)) return false;
        const ts = parseSafeTimestampMs(
          r?.updated_at || r?.fecha_clase || r?.created_at
        );
        return !Number.isNaN(ts) && ts >= startMs && ts <= endMs;
      });

  const countMasterPassWindows = (list: any[]) => {
    let dia = 0;
    let semana = 0;
    let mes = 0;
    list.forEach((r: any) => {
      const ts = parseSafeTimestampMs(r.updated_at || r.fecha_clase || r.created_at);
      const effectiveTs = Number.isNaN(ts) ? now.getTime() : ts;
      if (effectiveTs >= dayStartMs && effectiveTs <= dayEndMs) dia += 1;
      if (effectiveTs >= startMs && effectiveTs <= endMs) semana += 1;
      if (effectiveTs >= monthStartMs && effectiveTs <= monthEndMs) mes += 1;
    });
    return { dia, semana, mes };
  };

  const effectiveAfiliados = [...(afiliados || [])];
  const seenKeys = new Set(
    effectiveAfiliados.map((af) => normalizeAffiliateKey(af?.nombre)).filter(Boolean)
  );
  cleanReservas.forEach((r) => {
    const rawNeg = String(r?.negocio_nombre || '').trim();
    const k = normalizeAffiliateKey(rawNeg);
    if (!k || seenKeys.has(k)) return;
    seenKeys.add(k);
    effectiveAfiliados.push({
      id: k === 'kinetic oficial' ? 'kinetic_oficial' : `synth_${k.replace(/\s+/g, '_')}`,
      nombre: k === 'kinetic oficial' ? 'Kinetic Oficial' : rawNeg,
      disciplina: 'Fitness',
      categoria: 'Club',
      estado: 'activo',
      regimen_fiscal: k === 'kinetic oficial' ? 'persona_moral' : 'sin_rfc',
    });
  });

  const cortesPorAfiliado: ICorteSemanalAfiliadoConPases[] = effectiveAfiliados.map(
    (af: any) => {
      const nombreAf = String(af.nombre || '').trim();
      const normKey = normalizeAffiliateKey(nombreAf);
      const reservasAf = cleanReservas.filter(
        (r: any) => normalizeAffiliateKey(r.negocio_nombre) === normKey
      );
      const masterAf = masterPassReservas.filter(
        (r: any) => normalizeAffiliateKey(r.negocio_nombre) === normKey
      );
      const masterCounts = countMasterPassWindows(masterAf);
      let volumenBruto = 0;
      let brutoCheckinsKc = 0;
      let brutoNoshowKc = 0;
      let custodiaActiva = 0;
      let countReservasActivas = 0;
      let sesionesCompletadas = 0;
      let compensacionesNoshow = 0;
      const desgloseReservas: IDesgloseReservaCorte[] = [];

      reservasAf.forEach((r: any) => {
        const estado = String(r.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
        const precioTotal = Math.max(
          Number(r.costo_total) || 0,
          Number(r.precio_kc) || 0,
          (Number(r.monto_escrow) || 0) + (Number(r.monto_puerta) || 0)
        );
        const escrow = Number(r.monto_escrow) || Math.round(precioTotal * 0.4);
        const puerta = Number(r.monto_puerta) || Math.max(0, precioTotal - escrow);
        const rawDate = r.updated_at || r.fecha_clase || r.created_at;
        const ts = parseSafeTimestampMs(rawDate);
        const isInCurrentWeek = strictWindow
          ? !Number.isNaN(ts) && ts >= startMs && ts <= endMs
          : !hasWindowTransactions ||
            Number.isNaN(ts) ||
            (ts >= startMs && ts <= endMs);

        if (estado === 'completada' && isInCurrentWeek) {
          volumenBruto += precioTotal;
          brutoCheckinsKc += precioTotal;
          sesionesCompletadas += 1;
          desgloseReservas.push({
            id: String(r.id),
            folio: `#KNT-${String(r.id || '').slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`,
            servicio:
              r.servicio || r.nombre_servicio || r.titulo_clase || 'Sesión Kinetic',
            fecha: rawDate || periodoFin,
            estado: 'completada',
            precio_total_kc: precioTotal,
            monto_escrow_kc: escrow,
            monto_puerta_kc: puerta,
            ingreso_computable_mxn: precioTotal,
          });
        } else if (
          (estado === 'no_show' || estado === 'penalizada') &&
          isInCurrentWeek
        ) {
          volumenBruto += escrow;
          brutoNoshowKc += escrow;
          compensacionesNoshow += 1;
          desgloseReservas.push({
            id: String(r.id),
            folio: `#KNT-${String(r.id || '').slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`,
            servicio:
              r.servicio ||
              r.nombre_servicio ||
              r.titulo_clase ||
              'Sesión (Garantía 40% No-Show)',
            fecha: rawDate || periodoFin,
            estado: 'no_show',
            precio_total_kc: precioTotal,
            monto_escrow_kc: escrow,
            monto_puerta_kc: 0,
            ingreso_computable_mxn: escrow,
          });
        } else if (['pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada'].includes(estado)) {
          custodiaActiva += escrow;
          countReservasActivas += 1;
        }
      });

      volumenBruto = Number(volumenBruto.toFixed(2));
      brutoCheckinsKc = Number(brutoCheckinsKc.toFixed(2));
      brutoNoshowKc = Number(brutoNoshowKc.toFixed(2));
      const comisionTotal20 = Number((volumenBruto * 0.2).toFixed(2));
      const comisionBase = Number((comisionTotal20 / 1.16).toFixed(2));
      const comisionIva = Number((comisionTotal20 - comisionBase).toFixed(2));
      const subtotalSocio80 = Number((volumenBruto * 0.8).toFixed(2));

      const rawRegimen = String(af.regimen_fiscal || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      const cleanRfc = String(af.rfc || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      const hasRfc =
        cleanRfc.length >= 12 &&
        cleanRfc !== 'XAXX010101000' &&
        cleanRfc !== 'XEXX010101000';

      let regimen: RegimenFiscalSAT = 'sin_rfc';
      let tasaIsr = 20;
      let tasaIva = 16;
      if (rawRegimen === 'persona_moral' && (hasRfc || !rawRegimen.includes('sin_rfc'))) {
        regimen = 'persona_moral';
        tasaIsr = 0;
        tasaIva = 0;
      } else if (
        (rawRegimen === 'persona_fisica_con_rfc' || hasRfc) &&
        !rawRegimen.includes('sin_rfc')
      ) {
        regimen = 'persona_fisica_con_rfc';
        tasaIsr = 2.1;
        tasaIva = 8;
      }

      const retencionIsr = Number((subtotalSocio80 * (tasaIsr / 100)).toFixed(2));
      const retencionIva = Number((subtotalSocio80 * (tasaIva / 100)).toFixed(2));
      const totalRetenciones = Number((retencionIsr + retencionIva).toFixed(2));
      const netoSpei = Number(Math.max(0, subtotalSocio80 - totalRetenciones).toFixed(2));

      return {
        afiliado_id: String(af.id),
        afiliado_nombre: nombreAf,
        categoria_operador: af.es_especialista ? 'Especialista' : 'Club',
        disciplina: af.disciplina || af.categoria || 'Fitness',
        clabe_bancaria: af.clabe_bancaria || '',
        rfc: cleanRfc,
        regimen_fiscal: regimen,
        csf_validada: Boolean(af.csf_validada || hasRfc),
        periodo_inicio: periodoInicio,
        periodo_fin: periodoFin,
        sesiones_completadas: sesionesCompletadas,
        compensaciones_noshow: compensacionesNoshow,
        checkins_completados_count: sesionesCompletadas,
        noshow_penalizados_count: compensacionesNoshow,
        bruto_checkins_kc: brutoCheckinsKc,
        bruto_noshow_kc: brutoNoshowKc,
        ingreso_bruto_devengado_kc: volumenBruto,
        volumen_bruto_mxn: volumenBruto,
        comision_kinetic_total_mxn: comisionTotal20,
        comision_kinetic_base_mxn: comisionBase,
        comision_kinetic_iva_mxn: comisionIva,
        subtotal_socio_80_mxn: subtotalSocio80,
        tasa_retencion_isr_pct: tasaIsr,
        tasa_retencion_iva_pct: tasaIva,
        retencion_isr_mxn: retencionIsr,
        retencion_iva_mxn: retencionIva,
        total_retenciones_sat_mxn: totalRetenciones,
        neto_dispersar_spei_mxn: netoSpei,
        en_custodia_escrow_kc: custodiaActiva,
        reservas_activas_escrow_count: countReservasActivas,
        pases_maestros_dia: masterCounts.dia,
        pases_maestros_semana: masterCounts.semana,
        pases_maestros_mes: masterCounts.mes,
        desglose_reservas: desgloseReservas,
      };
    }
  );

  cortesPorAfiliado.sort(
    (a, b) =>
      b.neto_dispersar_spei_mxn - a.neto_dispersar_spei_mxn ||
      a.afiliado_nombre.localeCompare(b.afiliado_nombre, 'es')
  );

  const sumField = (fn: (c: ICorteSemanalAfiliado) => number) =>
    Number(cortesPorAfiliado.reduce((acc, c) => acc + fn(c), 0).toFixed(2));
  const totalMasterCounts = countMasterPassWindows(masterPassReservas);

  return {
    periodo_inicio: periodoInicio,
    periodo_fin: periodoFin,
    periodoInicio: periodoInicio,
    periodoFin: periodoFin,
    zona_horaria: 'America/Mazatlan',
    generado_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    total_volumen_bruto_mxn: sumField((c) => c.volumen_bruto_mxn),
    total_comision_kinetic_20_mxn: sumField((c) => c.comision_kinetic_total_mxn),
    total_comision_kinetic_base_mxn: sumField((c) => c.comision_kinetic_base_mxn),
    total_comision_kinetic_iva_mxn: sumField((c) => c.comision_kinetic_iva_mxn),
    total_subtotal_socios_80_mxn: sumField((c) => c.subtotal_socio_80_mxn),
    total_retencion_isr_sat_mxn: sumField((c) => c.retencion_isr_mxn),
    total_retencion_iva_sat_mxn: sumField((c) => c.retencion_iva_mxn),
    total_retenciones_sat_mxn: sumField((c) => c.total_retenciones_sat_mxn),
    total_neto_dispersar_spei_mxn: sumField((c) => c.neto_dispersar_spei_mxn),
    total_pases_maestros_dia: totalMasterCounts.dia,
    total_pases_maestros_semana: totalMasterCounts.semana,
    total_pases_maestros_mes: totalMasterCounts.mes,
    cortes_por_afiliado: cortesPorAfiliado,
  };
};

    FILE: src/services/adminDashboardFetcher.ts

    // === src/services/adminDashboardFetcher.ts === //
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import {
  AFFILIATES_RUNTIME_STATE_KEY,
  KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES,
  canonicalizeAffiliateInstagram,
  cleanLocationDisplay,
  resolveCanonicalAffiliateDiscipline,
} from '../constants/exploreData';
import {
  isSupabaseQuotaExceeded,
  isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error,
  setSupabaseQuotaExceeded,
  supabase,
} from '../lib/supabase';
import { getActiveKineticLanguage } from '../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';
import {
  computeAdminTierAndDisciplineMetrics,
  isSpecialistEventOrCategory,
} from './admin/adminMetricsCalculator';
import {
  fetchPendingUgcReportsCount,
  isAffiliateMarkedDeleted,
  readDeletedAffiliatesSet,
} from './admin/adminOpsHelpers';
import { fetchWaitlistPreRegistros } from './admin/adminWaitlistService';
import {
  getMazatlanWeekAndDayBounds,
} from './kineticVenueCanonicalGeoAndHours';
import {
  calcularCorteSemanalB2B,
